                      <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold ${acc.isActive ? 'bg-blue-500/30 text-blue-300' : 'bg-white/10 text-white/60'}`}>
                        {(acc.displayName || acc.handle || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{acc.displayName || acc.handle}</p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40">@{acc.handle} · {acc.pubkey.slice(0, 8)}...</p>
                      </div>
                      {acc.isActive ? (
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">ACTIVE</span>
                      ) : (
                        <button 
                          onClick={() => handleSwitch(acc.pubkey)} 
                          className="text-[10px] font-bold text-white bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                        >
                          Switch
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {!acc.isActive && (
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => handleRemove(acc.pubkey, acc.handle)} 
                        className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-red-400/60 hover:text-red-400 flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={10} /> Remove from device
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}

                <button 
                  onClick={() => identity.addNewAccount()} 
                  className="w-full bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 border-dashed rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                >
                  <Plus size={16} /> Add Another Account
                </button>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <button 
                onClick={() => { setSwitchingTo(null); setPin(''); setError(''); }} 
                className="text-xs text-blue-400 mb-4 flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                ← Back
              </button>
              
              <div className="text-center mb-6">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-3 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <User size={28} />
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-white/60">Enter PIN for</p>
                <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white">{accounts.find(a => a.pubkey === switchingTo)?.displayName}</p>
              </div>

              <input 
                type="password"
                inputMode="numeric"
                value={pin}
                onChange={e => { setPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8)); setError(''); }}
                onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') submitPin(); }}
                placeholder="••••"
                autoFocus
                maxLength={8}
                className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-4 text-center text-2xl tracking-[0.5em] text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none font-mono mb-3"
              />

              {error && <p className="text-xs text-red-400 text-center mb-3">{error}</p>}

              <button 
                onClick={submitPin}
                disabled={loading || pin.length < 4}
                className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-white/10 disabled:text-white/30 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors"
              >
                {loading ? 'Switching...' : 'Unlock & Switch'}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/BookmarksViewer.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Bookmark, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function BookmarksViewer({ isOpen, onClose, onJump }) {
  const bookmarks = useChatStore(s => s.bookmarks);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const groups = useChatStore(s => s.groups);
  const toggleBookmark = useChatStore(s => s.toggleBookmark);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Aggregate bookmarks across all chats
  const items = [];
  Object.entries(bookmarks).forEach(([chatId, msgIds]) => {
    const isGroup = groups.some(g => g.id === chatId);
    const msgs = isGroup ? (groupMessages[chatId] || []) : (dms[chatId] || []);
    msgIds.forEach(msgId => {
      const m = msgs.find(x => x.id === msgId);
      if (m) {
        let text = '';
        if (isGroup) {
          try { text = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}').message || '(media)'; } catch { text = '(media)'; }
        } else {
          text = m.data?.message || m.data?.title || '(media)';
        }
        items.push({
          chatId, msgId, isGroup, text,
          timestamp: m.timestamp,
          chatName: isGroup 
            ? (groups.find(g => g.id === chatId)?.name || 'Group')
            : (profiles[chatId]?.displayName || `User ${chatId.slice(0, 6)}`),
          senderName: profiles[m.pubkey]?.displayName || m.pubkey.slice(0, 6)
        });
      }
    });
  });

  items.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Bookmark size={18} className="text-yellow-400" /> Saved Messages ({items.length})
            </h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {items.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <Bookmark size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">No saved messages</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/30 mt-1">Tap the bookmark icon on any message to save it</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              items.map((item, i) => (
                <div key={`${item.chatId}-${item.msgId}-${i}`} className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => { onJump(item.chatId, item.msgId, item.isGroup); onClose(); }} className="flex-1 text-left">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-400">{item.chatName}</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-white/30">·</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-white/40">{item.senderName}</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-white/30 ml-auto">{new Date(item.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-white/80 line-clamp-2">{item.text}</p>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => toggleBookmark(item.chatId, item.msgId)} className="p-1.5 text-yellow-400 hover:text-yellow-300 transition-colors">
                    <Bookmark size={14} fill="currentColor" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/BroadcastModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Send, Users, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function BroadcastModal({ isOpen, onClose, product, onBroadcast }) {
  const groups = useChatStore(s => s.groups);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen || !product) return null;

  const toggle = (id) => {
    setSelected(prev => prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (selected.length === 0) return;
    setLoading(true);
    await onBroadcast(selected, product);
    setLoading(false);
    setSelected([]);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Broadcast to Groups</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50 hover:text-white"><X size={18}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3 mb-4 flex gap-3 items-center">
            {product.image && <img src={product.image} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg object-cover" />}
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{product.title}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-green-400 font-mono">${product.priceUsd}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Select Groups</p>
          <div className="space-y-2 mb-4 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
            {groups.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-white/40 text-center py-4">You haven't joined any groups.</p>
            ) : (
              groups.map(g => (
                <button
                  key={g.id}
                  onClick={() => toggle(g.id)}
                  className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl transition-colors text-left ${
                    selected.includes(g.id) ? 'bg-blue-600/20 border border-blue-500/40' : 'bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                    selected.includes(g.id) ? 'bg-blue-500 text-white' : 'bg-white/10 text-white/50'
                  }`}>
                    {selected.includes(g.id) ? <Check size={16} /> : <Users size={16} />}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white truncate">{g.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase">{g.isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Group'}</p>
                  </div>
                </button>
              ))
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={handleSubmit}
            disabled={selected.length === 0 || loading}
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-white/10 disabled:text-white/30 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            {loading ? 'Broadcasting...' : <><Send size={16} /> Broadcast to {selected.length} Group{selected.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</>}
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

        </button>

          disabled={selectedMsgs.length === 0}
          className="p-3 bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-30"
        >
          <Forward size={20} />
        </button>
        <button 
          onClick={onDelete}
          disabled={selectedMsgs.length === 0}
          className="p-3 bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-30"
        >
          <Trash2 size={20} />
        </button>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );

=== FILE: src/components/chat/ChatContextMenu.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Archive, Trash2, BellOff, Bell, Eraser, X, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { db } from '../../db';

export default function ChatContextMenu({ isOpen, onClose, chatId, chatName, isGroup }) {
  const archivedChats = useChatStore(s => s.archivedChats);
  const toggleArchiveChat = useChatStore(s => s.toggleArchiveChat);
  const mutedUntil = useChatStore(s => s.mutedUntil);
  const setMuteDuration = useChatStore(s => s.setMuteDuration);
  const openDialog = useChatStore(s => s.openDialog);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);
  const setDms = useChatStore(s => s.setDms);
  const setGroupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.setGroupMessages);
  const [showMuteOptions, setShowMuteOptions] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isArchived = archivedChats.includes(chatId);
  const isMuted = mutedUntil[chatId] && (mutedUntil[chatId] === 'forever' || mutedUntil[chatId] > Date.now());

  const handleArchive = () => {
    toggleArchiveChat(chatId);
    setToast({ type: 'success', message: isArchived ? 'Chat unarchived' : 'Chat archived' });
    onClose();
  };

  const handleMute = (durationMs, label) => {
    setMuteDuration(chatId, durationMs);
    setToast({ type: 'success', message: durationMs === 0 ? 'Unmuted' : `Muted for ${label}` });
    onClose();
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    openDialog({
      title: 'Clear Chat',
      message: `Delete all messages in "${chatName}"? This cannot be undone.`,
      type: 'danger',
      confirmText: 'Clear',
      onConfirm: async () => {
        await db.messages.where('chatId').equals(chatId).delete();
        if (isGroup) {
          setGroupMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [chatId]: [] }));
        } else {
          setDms(prev => ({ ...prev, [chatId]: [] }));
        }
        setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Chat cleared' });
        onClose();
      }
    });
  };

  const muteOptions = [
    { duration: 3600000, label: '1 hour' },
    { duration: 28800000, label: '8 hours' },
    { duration: 86400000, label: '1 day' },
    { duration: 604800000, label: '1 week' },
    { duration: -1, label: 'Forever' }
  ];

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          
          {!showMuteOptions ? (
            <>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white truncate">{chatName}</h2>
                <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <button onClick={handleArchive} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors text-left">
                  <Archive size={18} className="text-white/60" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-white flex-1">{isArchived ? 'Unarchive Chat' : 'Archive Chat'}</span>
                </button>

                <button onClick={() => setShowMuteOptions(true)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors text-left">
                  {isMuted ? <BellOff size={18} className="text-white/60" /> : <Bell size={18} className="text-white/60" />}
                  <span className="text-sm text-white flex-1">{isMuted ? 'Unmute' : 'Mute Notifications'}</span>
                </button>

                <button onClick={handleClear} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors text-left">
                  <Eraser size={18} className="text-white/60" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-white flex-1">Clear Chat History</span>
                </button>

                <button 
                  onClick={() => {
                    openDialog({
                      title: 'Delete Chat',
                      message: `Remove "${chatName}" from your chat list? Messages will be deleted locally.`,
                      type: 'danger',
                      confirmText: 'Delete',
                      onConfirm: async () => {
                        await db.messages.where('chatId').equals(chatId).delete();
                        if (isGroup) {
                          setGroupMessages(prev => { const n = { ...prev }; delete n[chatId]; return n; });
                        } else {
                          setDms(prev => { const n = { ...prev }; delete n[chatId]; return n; });
                        }
                        onClose();
                      }
                    });
                  }}
                  className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors text-left"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={18} className="text-red-500" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-red-500 flex-1">Delete Chat</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
                <button onClick={() => setShowMuteOptions(false)} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/60"><X size={16} className="rotate-45"/></button>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Mute For...</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {isMuted && (
                  <button onClick={() => handleMute(0, 'off')} className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-green-500/10 text-left flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Bell size={18} className="text-green-400" />
                    <span className="text-sm text-green-400 font-semibold">Unmute</span>
                  </button>
                )}
                {muteOptions.map(opt => (
                  <button key={opt.label} onClick={() => handleMute(opt.duration, opt.label)} className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/5 text-left flex items-center gap-3">
                    <BellOff size={18} className="text-white/60" />
                    <span className="text-sm text-white">{opt.label}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Megaphone, Users, Info, Share2, MoreVertical, Search, X, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import VendorCatalogSheet from './VendorCatalogSheet';
import GroupInviteModal from './GroupInviteModal';
import MediaGallery from './MediaGallery';
import { exportChatAsText } from '../../lib/exportChat';
import { CopyOnLongPress } from '../CopyOnLongPress';

export default function ChatHeader({ mode, identity }) {
  const activeDm = useChatStore((s) => s.activeDm);
  const activeGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.activeGroup);
  const profiles = useChatStore((s) => s.profiles);
  const typingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.typingUsers);
  const groupTypingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.groupTypingUsers);
  const setView = useChatStore((s) => s.setView);
  const setProfilePubkey = useChatStore((s) => s.setProfilePubkey);
  const setShowGroupInfo = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowGroupInfo);
  const setToast = useChatStore((s) => s.setToast);
  const disappearingTimers = useChatStore(s => s.disappearingTimers);
  const setDisappearingTimer = useChatStore(s => s.setDisappearingTimer);
  const setChatSearch = useChatStore(s => s.setChatSearch);
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);

  const [showMore, setShowMore] = useState(false);
  const [showTimerMenu, setShowTimerMenu] = useState(false);
  const [showSearch, setShowSearch] = useState(false);
  const [showCatalog, setShowCatalog] = useState(false);
  const [showInviteModal, setShowInviteModal] = useState(false);
  const [showDmMedia, setShowDmMedia] = useState(false);

  const handleBack = () => setView('list');
  const handleCopy = async (text, successMsg) => {
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); setToast({ type: 'success', message: successMsg }); } catch (e) {}
  };

  if (mode === 'dm' && activeDm) {
    const profile = profiles[activeDm] || { displayName: `User ${activeDm.slice(0, 4)}`, picture: null };
    const activeMsgs = dms[activeDm] || [];
    const latestOrder = [...activeMsgs].reverse().find(m => m.data?.type === 'cipher_order' || m.data?.type === 'order_request');
    const orderStatus = latestOrder?.data?.status;
    const currentTimer = disappearingTimers[activeDm];

    if (showSearch) {
      return (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] border-b border-white/5 flex-shrink-0 relative z-[80]">
          <button onClick={() => { setShowSearch(false); setChatSearch(''); }} className="p-2 text-white/60"><ArrowLeft size={20} /></button>
          <input autoFocus onChange={e => setChatSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search messages..." className="flex-1 bg-[#171717] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none" />
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 glass-header flex-shrink-0 relative z-[80]">
          <button onClick={handleBack} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white"><ArrowLeft size={20} /></button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer hover:opacity-80" onClick={() => setShowCatalog(true)}>
            {profile.picture ? <img src={profile.picture} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full" /> : <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{profile.displayName.charAt(0)}</div>}
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">{profile.displayName}</h3>
                {orderStatus && (
                  <span className={`text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ml-2 ${
                    orderStatus === 'completed' ? 'text-green-400 border-green-400/20' :
                    orderStatus === 'shipped' ? 'text-purple-400 border-purple-400/20' :
                    orderStatus === 'accepted' ? 'text-blue-400 border-blue-400/20' :
                    'text-yellow-400 border-yellow-400/20'
                  }`}>
                    {orderStatus.toUpperCase()}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              {typingUsers[activeDm] ? <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-400 animate-pulse">typing...</p> : <p className="text-[11px] text-white/40">Direct Message</p>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1" />
          <div className="relative">
            <button onClick={() => setShowMore(!showMore)} className="p-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white"><MoreVertical size={20} /></button>
            {showMore && (
              <>
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[90]" onClick={() => setShowMore(false)} />
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-10 bg-[#1c1c1e] border border-white/10 rounded-xl shadow-xl z-[100] w-48 overflow-hidden">
                  <button onClick={() => { setShowSearch(true); setShowMore(false); }} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/10 text-left">
                    <Search size={16} /> Search Messages
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { setShowDmMedia(true); setShowMore(false); }} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/10 text-left border-t border-white/5">
                    <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2" ry="2"/><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5"/><polyline points="21 15 16 10 5 21"/></svg> Shared Media
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { const msgs = dms[activeDm] || []; exportChatAsText(msgs, profiles[activeDm]?.displayName || 'Chat', profiles); setShowMore(false); }} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/10 text-left border-t border-white/5">
                    <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4"/><polyline points="7 10 12 15 17 10"/><line x1="12" y1="15" x2="12" y2="3"/></svg> Export Chat
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { setShowTimerMenu(true); setShowMore(false); }} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/10 text-left border-t border-white/5">
                    <Clock size={16} /> Disappearing {currentTimer && currentTimer !== 'off' && <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] bg-blue-500 text-white px-1.5 rounded">{currentTimer}</span>}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        
        {showTimerMenu && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4" onClick={() => setShowTimerMenu(false)}>
            <div className="bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-2xl border border-white/10 w-full max-w-xs p-4" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
              <h3 className="text-white font-bold mb-3">Disappearing Messages</h3>
              {['off', '1h', '24h', '7d'].map(t => (
                <button key={t} onClick={() => { setDisappearingTimer(activeDm, t); setShowTimerMenu(false); }} className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-lg ${currentTimer === t ? 'bg-blue-500 text-white' : 'text-white/70 hover:bg-white/5'}`}>
                  {t === 'off' ? 'Off' : t === '1h' ? '1 Hour' : t === '24h' ? '24 Hours' : '7 Days'}
                </button>
              ))}
              <button onClick={() => { setShowTimerMenu(false); useChatStore.getState().openDialog({ title: 'Custom Timer', hasInput: true, placeholder: 'Enter minutes...', onConfirm: (val) => val && setDisappearingTimer(activeDm, val + 'm') }); }} className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm text-blue-400 font-bold hover:bg-white/5 rounded-lg border-t border-white/5 mt-1 pt-2">Custom...</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <MediaGallery isOpen={showDmMedia} onClose={() => setShowDmMedia(false)} chatId={activeDm} isGroup={false} />
        <VendorCatalogSheet isOpen={showCatalog} onClose={() => setShowCatalog(false)} vendorPubkey={activeDm} profiles={profiles} identity={identity} />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (mode === 'group' && activeGroup) {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 glass-header flex-shrink-0 relative z-[80]">
          <button onClick={handleBack} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white"><ArrowLeft size={20} /></button>
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${activeGroup.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
            {activeGroup.isChannel ? <Megaphone size={20} /> : <Users size={20} />}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">{activeGroup.name}</h3>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-white/40">{activeGroup.isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Group'}</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setShowInviteModal(true)} className="p-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white"><Share2 size={20} /></button>
          <button onClick={() => setShowGroupInfo(true)} className="p-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white"><Info size={20} /></button>
        </div>
        <GroupInviteModal isOpen={showInviteModal} onClose={() => setShowInviteModal(false)} group={activeGroup} />
      </>
    );
  }

  return null;
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/ChatList.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { Users, Megaphone, Pin, BellOff, Archive, Search, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { scopedGet } from '../../lib/storage';
import ChatContextMenu from './ChatContextMenu';

export default function ChatList({ onOpenChat }) {
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const groups = useChatStore(s => s.groups);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const unreadCounts = useChatStore(s => s.unreadCounts);
  const pinnedChats = useChatStore(s => s.pinnedChats);
  const archivedChats = useChatStore(s => s.archivedChats);
  const mutedUntil = useChatStore(s => s.mutedUntil);

  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');
  const [contextMenu, setContextMenu] = useState(null);
  const [showArchived, setShowArchived] = useState(false);
  const [searchBarValue, setSearchBarValue] = useState('');
  const [searchBarFocused, setSearchBarFocused] = useState(false);
  const searchTimerRef = useRef(null);

  const acceptedReqs = scopedGet('accepted_reqs', []);
  const blockedUsers = useChatStore(s => s.blockedUsers || []);

  const dmList = Object.entries(dms)
    .filter(([pubkey]) => acceptedReqs.includes(pubkey) && !blockedUsers.includes(pubkey))
    .map(([pubkey, msgs]) => {
      const profile = profiles[pubkey];
      const safeProfile = (profile && profile.name && !/^[0-9a-f]{8,64}$/i.test(profile.name))
        ? profile
        : { displayName: `User ${pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`, picture: null, name: pubkey.slice(0, 4) };
      return { type: 'dm', id: pubkey, lastMsg: msgs[msgs.length - 1], profile: safeProfile };
    });

  const groupList = groups.map(g => ({
    type: 'group',
    id: g.id,
    group: g,
    lastMsg: groupMessages[g.id]?.[groupMessages[g.id].length - 1]
  }));

  const chatMap = new Map();
  [...dmList, ...groupList].forEach(c => chatMap.set(c.id, c));
  const allChats = Array.from(chatMap.values());

  const filteredChats = allChats.filter(c => {
    const isArchived = archivedChats.includes(c.id);
    if (showArchived && !isArchived) return false;
    if (!showArchived && isArchived) return false;
    if (filter === 'dms' && c.type !== 'dm') return false;
    if (filter === 'groups' && (c.type !== 'group' || c.group?.isChannel)) return false;
    if (filter === 'channels' && (c.type !== 'group' || !c.group?.isChannel)) return false;
    
    // Local filter by name
    if (searchBarValue.trim().length > 0) {
      const nameToMatch = c.type === 'dm' ? c.profile?.displayName : c.group?.name;
      if (!nameToMatch?.toLowerCase().includes(searchBarValue.toLowerCase())) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }).sort((a, b) => {
    const aPinned = pinnedChats.includes(a.id) ? 1 : 0;
    const bPinned = pinnedChats.includes(b.id) ? 1 : 0;
    if (aPinned !== bPinned) return bPinned - aPinned;
    return (b.lastMsg?.timestamp || 0) - (a.lastMsg?.timestamp || 0);
  });

  const handleSearchChange = (val) => {
    setSearchBarValue(val);
  };

  const openContextMenu = (chat) => {
    setContextMenu({
      id: chat.id,
      name: chat.type === 'group' ? chat.group.name : (chat.profile?.displayName || 'Chat'),
      isGroup: chat.type === 'group'
    });
  };

  const renderSearchBar = () => (
    <div className="relative mb-3">
      <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#111] border rounded-xl px-3 py-2 transition-colors ${searchBarFocused ? 'border-blue-500' : 'border-white/10'}`}>
        <Search size={16} className="text-white/40" />
        <input
          type="text"
          value={searchBarValue}
          onChange={(e) => handleSearchChange(e.target.value)}
          onFocus={() => setSearchBarFocused(true)}
          onBlur={() => setSearchBarFocused(false)}
          placeholder="Search messages, groups, people..."
          className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-sm text-white placeholder-white/30 outline-none"
        />
        {searchBarValue && (
          <button onClick={() => setSearchBarValue('')} className="p-1 text-white/40 hover:text-white">
            <X size={14} />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  const renderFilterTabs = () => (
    <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3 bg-white/5 p-1 rounded-xl">
      {['all', 'dms', 'groups', 'channels'].map(f => (
        <button
          key={f}
          onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
          className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-lg transition-colors ${filter === f ? 'bg-white/10 text-white' : 'text-white/40 hover:text-white'}`}
        >
          {f}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );

  if (allChats.length === 0) {
    return (
      <>
        {renderFilterTabs()}
        {renderSearchBar()}
        <p className="text-center text-white/40 mt-10">No chats yet.</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      {archivedChats.length > 0 && (
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowArchived(!showArchived)}
          className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 mb-3 rounded-xl transition-colors ${showArchived ? 'bg-blue-600/20 border border-blue-500/40' : 'bg-[#111] border border-white/5'}`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Archive size={16} className="text-white/60" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">{showArchived ? 'Back to Chats' : 'Archived'}</span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-white/50">{archivedChats.length}</span>
        </button>
      )}

      {renderFilterTabs()}
      {renderSearchBar()}

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {filteredChats.map((chat) => {
          const isPinned = pinnedChats.includes(chat.id);
          const isMuted = mutedUntil[chat.id] && (mutedUntil[chat.id] === 'forever' || mutedUntil[chat.id] > Date.now());

          if (chat.type === 'dm') {
            const profile = chat.profile;
            return (
              <div
                key={chat.id}
                onClick={() => onOpenChat('dm', chat.id)}
                onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); openContextMenu(chat); }}
                onTouchStart={(e) => { e.currentTarget._touchTimer = setTimeout(() => openContextMenu(chat), 600); }}
                onTouchEnd={(e) => clearTimeout(e.currentTarget._touchTimer)}
                className="relative bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-4"
              >
                {isPinned && <Pin size={12} className="absolute top-2 right-2 text-blue-400" />}
                {isMuted && <BellOff size={12} className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 text-white/30" />}
                {profile.picture ? (
                  <img src={profile.picture} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover" alt="" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-[#171717] flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                    {profile.displayName.charAt(0)}
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">{profile.displayName}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-white/50 truncate">
                    {chat.lastMsg?.data?.type === 'cipher_order' ? '📦 Order'
                      : chat.lastMsg?.data?.type === 'image' ? '🖼️ Photo'
                      : chat.lastMsg?.data?.type === 'audio' ? '🎤 Voice Note'
                      : chat.lastMsg?.data?.message || ''}
                  </p>
                </div>
                {unreadCounts[chat.id] > 0 && (
                  <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white shrink-0">
                    {unreadCounts[chat.id]}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          } else {
            const Icon = chat.group.isChannel ? Megaphone : Users;
            return (
              <div
                key={chat.id}
                onClick={() => onOpenChat('group', chat.id, chat.group)}
                onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); openContextMenu(chat); }}
                onTouchStart={(e) => { e.currentTarget._touchTimer = setTimeout(() => openContextMenu(chat), 600); }}
                onTouchEnd={(e) => clearTimeout(e.currentTarget._touchTimer)}
                className="relative bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-4"
              >
                {isPinned && <Pin size={12} className="absolute top-2 right-2 text-blue-400" />}
                {isMuted && <BellOff size={12} className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 text-white/30" />}
                <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${chat.group.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                  <Icon size={20} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">
                    {chat.group.name && !chat.group.name.startsWith('Group ') ? chat.group.name : 'Unnamed Group'}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-white/50 truncate">
                    {chat.group.isChannel ? '📢 Channel' : '👥 Public Group'}
                  </p>
                </div>
                {unreadCounts[chat.id] > 0 && (
                  <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white shrink-0">
                    {unreadCounts[chat.id]}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          }
        })}
      </div>

      {contextMenu && (
        <ChatContextMenu
          isOpen={!!contextMenu}
          onClose={() => setContextMenu(null)}
          chatId={contextMenu.id}
          chatName={contextMenu.name}
          isGroup={contextMenu.isGroup}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

export default function ChatListSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-4 py-2 animate-pulse">
      {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((i) => (
        <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 mb-2 rounded-2xl bg-white/5">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white/10 flex-shrink-0"></div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="h-4 bg-white/10 rounded w-1/3 mb-2"></div>
            <div className="h-3 bg-white/5 rounded w-2/3"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/ChatPinnedBar.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Pin, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ChatPinnedBar({ unpinMessage }) {
  const activeGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.activeGroup);
  const pinnedMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.pinnedMessages);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.groupMessages);

  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);

  if (!activeGroup) return null;
  const pinnedId = pinnedMessages[activeGroup.id];
  if (!pinnedId) return null;

  const groupMsgs = groupMessages[activeGroup.id] || [];
  const pinnedMsg = groupMsgs.find((m) => m.id === pinnedId);
  if (!pinnedMsg) return null;

  let preview = 'Pinned message';
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(pinnedMsg.rawContent || '{}');
    preview = parsed.message || parsed.url || 'Pinned message';
  } catch (e) {
    preview = pinnedMsg.rawContent || 'Pinned message';
  }

  return (
    <div className="border-b border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
      <div
        onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/[0.02] transition-colors"
      >
        <Pin size={14} className="text-yellow-400 shrink-0" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p className="text-[10px] text-yellow-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Pinned</p>
          <p className={`text-xs text-black dark:text-white/70 ${expanded ? '' : 'truncate'}`}>{preview}</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={async (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            if (unpinMessage) await unpinMessage(activeGroup.id);
          }}
          className="p-1 text-black dark:text-white/40 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <X size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/ChatReplyPreview.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { X, Reply } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ChatReplyPreview() {
  const replyingTo = useChatStore((s) => s.replyingTo);
  const setReplyingTo = useChatStore((s) => s.setReplyingTo);

  if (!replyingTo) return null;

  // Extract preview text from various message formats
  let previewText = 'Message';
  if (replyingTo.data?.message) previewText = replyingTo.data.message;
  else if (replyingTo.data?.title) previewText = replyingTo.data.title;
  else if (replyingTo.data?.url) previewText = '[attachment]';
  else if (replyingTo.rawContent) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(replyingTo.rawContent);
      previewText = parsed.message || parsed.title || '[attachment]';
    } catch { previewText = replyingTo.rawContent; }
  } else if (replyingTo.text) previewText = replyingTo.text;

  // Sender display
  let sender = 'Replying to';
  if (replyingTo.isSender) sender = 'Replying to yourself';
  else if (replyingTo.pubkey) {
    const profiles = useChatStore.getState().profiles || {};
    const name = profiles[replyingTo.pubkey]?.displayName || profiles[replyingTo.pubkey]?.name;
    sender = name ? `Replying to ${name}` : `Replying to User ${replyingTo.pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`;
  }

  return (
    <div className="px-3 pt-2 bg-[#111]">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-black/40 border-l-2 border-blue-400 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
        <Reply size={14} className="text-blue-400 shrink-0" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p className="text-[10px] text-blue-400 font-bold uppercase">{sender}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/70 truncate">{previewText.slice(0, 120)}</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setReplyingTo(null)}
          className="p-1 text-black dark:text-white/40 hover:text-black dark:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <X size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { X, Camera, Users, Megaphone, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';

export default function CreateGroupModal({ createGroup }) {
  const showCreateGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.showCreateGroup);
  const setShowCreateGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowCreateGroup);
  const newGroupName = useChatStore((s) => s.newGroupName);
  const setNewGroupName = useChatStore((s) => s.setNewGroupName);
  const newGroupAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.newGroupAbout);
  const setNewGroupAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.setNewGroupAbout);
  const isChannel = useChatStore((s) => s.isChannel);
  const setIsChannel = useChatStore((s) => s.setIsChannel);
  const loading = useChatStore((s) => s.loading);

  const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

  if (!showCreateGroup) return null;

  const handleAvatarUpload = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      setAvatarPreview(url);
    }
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    setShowCreateGroup(false);
    setAvatarPreview(null);
    setNewGroupName('');
    setNewGroupAbout('');
    setIsChannel(false);
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
        exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center"
        onClick={handleClose}
      >
        <motion.div
          initial={{ y: '100%' }}
          animate={{ y: 0 }}
          exit={{ y: '100%' }}
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }}
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="w-full sm:max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] shadow-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden border border-white/10 max-h-[92dvh]"
        >
          <div className="w-full flex justify-center pt-3 pb-1">
            <div className="w-10 h-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-full" />
          </div>
          <div className="px-6 pb-4 pt-2 flex items-center justify-between">
            <button onClick={handleClose} className="text-white/60 hover:text-white p-1">
              <X size={22} strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </button>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
              {isChannel ? 'New Channel' : 'New Group'}
            </h2>
            <div className="w-8" />
          </div>
          <form
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              if (!newGroupName.trim()) return;
              createGroup(e);
              handleClose();
            }}
            className="px-6 pb-8 flex flex-col gap-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1"
          >
            <div className="flex justify-center -mt-2 mb-2">
              <div
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="relative w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-[#2c2c2e] border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden cursor-pointer group hover:border-blue-500/50 transition-colors"
              >
                {avatarPreview ? (
                  <img src={avatarPreview} alt="avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-white/40 group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors">
                    <Camera size={28} />
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-medium mt-1">Add Photo</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                  <ImageIcon size={24} className="text-white" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <input
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                ref={fileInputRef}
                onChange={handleAvatarUpload}
                className="hidden"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={newGroupName}
                onChange={(e) => setNewGroupName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={isChannel ? 'Channel Name' : 'Group Name'}
                className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3.5 text-[15px] text-white placeholder-white/30 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 transition-colors"
                autoFocus
              />
              <textarea
                value={newGroupAbout}
                onChange={(e) => setNewGroupAbout(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Description (Optional)"
                rows={2}
                className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3.5 text-[15px] text-white placeholder-white/30 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 resize-none transition-colors"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3.5 mt-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg ${isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                  {isChannel ? <Megaphone size={16} /> : <Users size={16} />}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">
                    {isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Public Group'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-white/40">
                    {isChannel ? 'Only admins can post' : 'Everyone can chat'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsChannel(!isChannel)}
                className={`w-12 h-6 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors ${isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500' : 'bg-white/20'}`}
              >
                <motion.div
                  layout
                  transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 500, damping: 30 }}
                  className="w-5 h-5 bg-white rounded-full shadow-sm"
                  style={{ marginLeft: isChannel ? 'auto' : '0' }}
                />
              </button>
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || newGroupName.trim().length === 0}
              className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-white/10 disabled:text-white/30 text-white font-semibold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-all active:scale-[0.98] mt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                `Create ${isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Group'}`
              )}
            </button>
          </form>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/DateSeparator.jsx ===
import React from 'react';

export const formatDate = (timestamp) => {
  if (!timestamp) return '';
  const date = new Date(timestamp * 1000);
  const today = new Date();
  const yesterday = new Date();
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);

  if (date.toDateString() === today.toDateString()) return 'Today';
  if (date.toDateString() === yesterday.toDateString()) return 'Yesterday';
  return date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
};

export default function DateSeparator({ timestamp }) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full flex justify-center my-4">
      <span className="bg-[#1c1c1e] text-gray-400 text-[11px] font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider border border-white/5">
        {formatDate(timestamp)}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/DmView.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import MessageBubble from '../MessageBubble';
import ChatHeader from './ChatHeader';
import ChatReplyPreview from './ChatReplyPreview';
import MessageInput from '../MessageInput';
import MessageActionsSheet from './MessageActionsSheet';
import ProfileModal from '../ProfileModal';
import ForwardModal from './ForwardModal';
import FullEmojiPicker from './FullEmojiPicker';
import ReviewModal from './ReviewModal';
import ReactionPicker from './ReactionPicker';
import ReactionsDetail from './ReactionsDetail';
import MessageEditHistory from './MessageEditHistory';
import ImageLightbox from '../ImageLightbox';
import DateSeparator from './DateSeparator';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { scopedSet, scopedRemove } from '../../lib/storage';
import { useReactions } from '../../hooks/useReactions';
import { useReputation } from '../../hooks/useReputation';
import { useScamReports } from '../../hooks/useScamReports';

export default function DmView({
  identity,
  sendMessage,
  handleMessageAction,
  handleImageUpload,
  startRecording,
  stopRecording,
  handleForwardSubmit,
}) {
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const activeDm = useChatStore(s => s.activeDm);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const deletedMsgs = useChatStore(s => s.deletedMsgs);
  const reactions = useChatStore(s => s.reactions);
  const selectedMessage = useChatStore(s => s.selectedMessage);
  const forwardingMsg = useChatStore(s => s.forwardingMsg);
  const showFullEmoji = useChatStore(s => s.showFullEmoji);
  const reviewTarget = useChatStore(s => s.reviewTarget);
  const profilePubkey = useChatStore(s => s.profilePubkey);
  const newMessage = useChatStore(s => s.newMessage);
  const isRecording = useChatStore(s => s.isRecording);
  const uploadingImage = useChatStore(s => s.uploadingImage);

  const setSelectedMessage = useChatStore(s => s.setSelectedMessage);
  const setForwardingMsg = useChatStore(s => s.setForwardingMsg);
  const setShowFullEmoji = useChatStore(s => s.setShowFullEmoji);
  const setReviewTarget = useChatStore(s => s.setReviewTarget);
  const setProfilePubkey = useChatStore(s => s.setProfilePubkey);
  const setNewMessage = useChatStore(s => s.setNewMessage);
  const setVendorStorefrontPubkey = useChatStore(s => s.setVendorStorefrontPubkey);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);
  const openChat = useChatStore(s => s.openChat);

  const { sendReaction } = useReactions({ identity });
  const { sendReview } = useReputation({ identity });
  const { isScammer } = useScamReports({ identity, targetPubkey: activeDm });

  const activeMessages = dms[activeDm] || [];
  const scrollRef = useRef(null);
  const [lightboxUrl, setLightboxUrl] = useState(null);
  const [emojiTarget, setEmojiTarget] = useState(null);
  const [reactionsDetail, setReactionsDetail] = useState(null);
  const [editingHistoryMsg, setEditingHistoryMsg] = useState(null);

  const selectionMode = useChatStore(s => s.selectionMode);
  const selectedMsgs = useChatStore(s => s.selectedMsgs);
  const toggleSelectedMsg = useChatStore(s => s.toggleSelectedMsg);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
  }, [activeDm, activeMessages.length, reactions, forwardingMsg]);

  const _vendorSet = new Set();
  activeMessages.forEach(m => {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}');
      if (p.type === 'product') _vendorSet.add(m.pubkey);
    } catch {}
  });
  const _vendorList = Array.from(_vendorSet);
  const [_vouchMap, _setVouchMap] = useState({});

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const { getAuthenticatedPool } = await import('../../lib/pool');
      const { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } = await import('../../lib/relays');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const newMap = {};
      await Promise.all(_vendorList.map(async (pk) => {
        try {
          const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10004], '#p': [pk], limit: 200 }, 3000);
          const unique = new Set(events.filter(e => e.pubkey !== pk).map(e => e.pubkey));
          newMap[pk] = unique.size;
        } catch {
          newMap[pk] = 0;
        }
      }));
      if (!cancelled) _setVouchMap(newMap);
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [_vendorList.join(','), identity?.pk]);

  const handleReplyClick = (msgId) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(`msg-${msgId}`);
    if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ x: 20, opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ x: 20, opacity: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
      className="absolute inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a] pb-[72px]"
    >
      <ChatHeader mode="dm" identity={identity} />

      <div ref={scrollRef} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden min-h-0 p-4 space-y-4">
        {activeMessages.map((msg, i) => {
          if (deletedMsgs.has(msg.id)) return null;
          const prevMsg = i > 0 ? activeMessages[i - 1] : null;
          const showDate = !prevMsg || new Date(msg.timestamp * 1000).toDateString() !== new Date(prevMsg.timestamp * 1000).toDateString();
          const showTail = i === 0 || activeMessages[i - 1].isSender !== msg.isSender;
          const repliedMsg = msg.replyTo ? activeMessages.find(m => m.id === msg.replyTo) : null;
          const msgData = msg.data || {};

          return (
            <React.Fragment key={msg.id}>
              {showDate && <DateSeparator timestamp={msg.timestamp} />}
              <MessageBubble
                identity={identity}
                msg={msg}
                data={{ ...msgData, vouchCount: _vouchMap[msg.pubkey] || 0 }}
                isScammer={isScammer}
                showTail={showTail}
                repliedTo={repliedMsg}
                repliedData={repliedMsg?.data}
                reactions={reactions[msg.id]}
                onReact={(emoji) => sendReaction(msg.id, emoji, false, null, msg.pubkey)}
                onSelect={(m, d, action) => {
                  if (action === 'close') setSelectedMessage(null);
                  else if (action === 'open_picker') {
                    setShowFullEmoji(true);
                    setSelectedMessage({ id: msg.id, pubkey: msg.pubkey, text: msgData.message || msgData.url });
                  } else {
                    setSelectedMessage({ id: msg.id, pubkey: msg.pubkey, text: msgData.message || msgData.url });
                  }
                }}
                onReplyAction={() => setSelectedMessage({ id: msg.id, pubkey: msg.pubkey, text: msgData.message || msgData.url })}
                onImageClick={setLightboxUrl}
                onReplyClick={handleReplyClick}
                onShowReactions={() => setReactionsDetail(reactions[msg.id])}
                onShowEdits={(m) => setEditingHistoryMsg(m)}
                selectionMode={selectionMode}
                isSelectedMsg={selectedMsgs.includes(msg.id)}
                onToggleSelect={(id) => toggleSelectedMsg(id)}
                isSelected={selectedMessage?.id === msg.id}
                isGroup={false}
                myPubkey={identity.pk}
                onAction={handleMessageAction}
              />
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        })}
        <div className="h-4" />
      </div>

      <ChatReplyPreview />

      <MessageInput
        value={newMessage}
        onChange={(val) => {
          setNewMessage(val);
          if (val.trim()) scopedSet(`draft-dm-${activeDm}`, val);
          else scopedRemove(`draft-dm-${activeDm}`);
        }}
        onSend={() => {
          if (newMessage.trim()) {
            sendMessage(newMessage);
            setNewMessage('');
          }
        }}
        canPost={true}
        isChannel={false}
        onImageUpload={handleImageUpload}
        startRecording={startRecording}
        stopRecording={stopRecording}
        isRecording={isRecording}
        uploadingImage={uploadingImage}
      />

      {profilePubkey && (
        <ProfileModal
          pubkey={profilePubkey}
          profile={profiles[profilePubkey]}
          identity={identity}
          onClose={() => setProfilePubkey(null)}
          onMessage={(pk) => { openChat('dm', pk); setProfilePubkey(null); }}
          onReview={(pk) => setReviewTarget(pk)}
          onViewStorefront={(pk) => setVendorStorefrontPubkey(pk)}
        />
      )}

      <MessageActionsSheet
        isOpen={!!selectedMessage}
        onClose={() => setSelectedMessage(null)}
        onAction={(action, emoji) => {
          if (action === 'open_picker') {
            setEmojiTarget(selectedMessage);
            setSelectedMessage(null);
            return;
          }
          handleMessageAction(action, emoji);
        }}
        msg={selectedMessage}
        isAdmin={false}
        isMine={selectedMessage?.pubkey === identity.pk}
        canEdit={selectedMessage?.pubkey === identity.pk && selectedMessage?.data?.type === 'text'}
      />

      {emojiTarget && (
        <ReactionPicker
          onSelect={(emoji) => {
            sendReaction(emojiTarget.id, emoji, false, null, emojiTarget.pubkey);
            setEmojiTarget(null);
          }}
          onClose={() => setEmojiTarget(null)}
        />
      )}

      <ReactionsDetail
        isOpen={!!reactionsDetail}
        onClose={() => setReactionsDetail(null)}
        reactions={reactionsDetail}
        profiles={profiles}
      />

      <MessageEditHistory
        isOpen={!!editingHistoryMsg}
        onClose={() => setEditingHistoryMsg(null)}
        msg={editingHistoryMsg}
      />

      {forwardingMsg && (
        <ForwardModal
          isOpen={!!forwardingMsg}
          onClose={() => setForwardingMsg(null)}
          onForward={handleForwardSubmit}
        />
      )}

      {showFullEmoji && selectedMessage && (
        <FullEmojiPicker
          onSelect={(emoji) => {
            const id = selectedMessage.id;
            const pk = selectedMessage.pubkey;
            setShowFullEmoji(false);
            setSelectedMessage(null);
            if (id) sendReaction(id, emoji, false, null, pk);
          }}
          onClose={() => {
            setShowFullEmoji(false);
            setSelectedMessage(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {reviewTarget && (
        <ReviewModal
          targetName={profiles[reviewTarget]?.displayName || profiles[reviewTarget]?.name || `User ${reviewTarget.slice(0, 4)}`}
          onSubmit={async (rating, text) => {
            const res = await sendReview(reviewTarget, rating, text);
            if (res.success) {
              setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Review published!' });
              return true;
            }
            setToast({ type: 'error', message: res.error || 'Failed to publish' });
            return false;
          }}
          onClose={() => setReviewTarget(null)}
        />
      )}

      <ImageLightbox url={lightboxUrl} onClose={() => setLightboxUrl(null)} />
    </motion.div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/FloatingMenu.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { Plus, X, Users, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

function MessageIcon({ size }) {
  return <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><path d="M21 15a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7l-4 4V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h14a2 2 0 0 1 2 2z"></path></svg>;
}

export default function FloatingMenu() {
  const showMenu = useChatStore((s) => s.showMenu);
  const setShowMenu = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowMenu);
  const setView = useChatStore((s) => s.setView);
  const setShowCreateGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowCreateGroup);
  const setIsChannel = useChatStore((s) => s.setIsChannel);

  return (
    <>
      {showMenu && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowMenu(false)}>
          <div className="absolute bottom-[10rem] right-6 flex flex-col gap-3 items-end">
            <button
              onClick={() => { setShowMenu(false); setView('search'); }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#1c1c1e] px-4 py-3 rounded-full border border-white/10 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">New Chat</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full text-white"><MessageIcon size={18} /></div>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setShowMenu(false); setIsChannel(false); setShowCreateGroup(true); }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#1c1c1e] px-4 py-3 rounded-full border border-white/10 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up"
              style={{ animationDelay: '50ms' }}
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">New Group</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-green-500 rounded-full text-white"><Users size={18} /></div>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setShowMenu(false); setIsChannel(true); setShowCreateGroup(true); }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#1c1c1e] px-4 py-3 rounded-full border border-white/10 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up"
              style={{ animationDelay: '100ms' }}
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">New Channel</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-purple-500 rounded-full text-white"><Megaphone size={18} /></div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <button
        onClick={() => setShowMenu(!showMenu)}
        className={`fixed bottom-24 right-6 p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg transition-all z-[70] ${
          showMenu ? 'bg-[#1c1c1e] border border-white/20 text-white rotate-45' : 'bg-white text-black hover:scale-105 active:scale-90'
        }`}
      >
        <Plus size={24} className={showMenu ? 'rotate-45 transition-transform' : 'transition-transform'} />
      </button>
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/ForwardModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { X, Search, Users, User, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ForwardModal({ isOpen, onClose, onForward }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const dms = useChatStore((s) => s.dms);
  const groups = useChatStore((s) => s.groups);
  const profiles = useChatStore((s) => s.profiles);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Build unified list of chats
  const dmList = Object.keys(dms).map(pk => ({
    id: pk,
    isGroup: false,
    name: profiles[pk]?.displayName || profiles[pk]?.name || `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}`,
    icon: User
  }));

  const groupList = groups.map(g => ({
    id: g.id,
    isGroup: true,
    name: g.name,
    isChannel: g.isChannel,
    icon: g.isChannel ? Megaphone : Users
  }));

  const allChats = [...dmList, ...groupList].filter(c => 
    c.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
  );

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/10 rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md h-[70vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl animate-slide-up">
        
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/10 flex justify-between items-center bg-[#0a0a0a]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Forward to...</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 text-white/50 hover:text-white bg-white/5 rounded-full">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3 border-b border-white/5 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
          <div className="flex items-center bg-black border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">
            <Search size={16} className="text-white/40 mr-2" />
            <input 
              type="text" 
              placeholder="Search chats and groups..." 
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-sm text-white outline-none w-full"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2">
          {allChats.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-center text-white/40 text-sm mt-10">No chats found.</p>
          )}
          {allChats.map((chat) => {
            const Icon = chat.icon;
            return (
              <div 
                key={chat.id}
                onClick={() => onForward(chat.id, chat.isGroup)}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 hover:bg-white/5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors"
              >
                <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${chat.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                  <Icon size={20} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{chat.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase">{chat.isGroup ? (chat.isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Group') : 'Direct Message'}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import EmojiPicker from 'emoji-picker-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';

export default function FullEmojiPicker({ onSelect, onClose }) {
  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div 
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
        exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        onClick={onClose}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[10000] bg-white dark:bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center pb-24 sm:items-center sm:pb-0"
      >
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: "100%" }}
          animate={{ y: 0 }}
          exit={{ y: "100%" }}
          transition={{ type: "spring", damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }}
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800"
        >
          <EmojiPicker 
            theme="dark" 
            onEmojiClick={(emojiData) => {
              onSelect(emojiData.emoji);
              onClose();
            }}
            lazyLoadEmojis={true}
            skinTonesDisabled={false}
            defaultSkinTone="neutral"
            style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: '350px', height: '400px', background: '#1c1c1e' }}
          />
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}
