import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Megaphone, Users, ShieldAlert, Store, UserPlus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import ModerationLog from './ModerationLog';
import MediaGallery from './MediaGallery';
import VendorDirectory from './VendorDirectory';
import JoinRequestsPanel from './JoinRequestsPanel';
import GroupSettingsModal from './GroupSettingsModal';
import { CopyOnLongPress } from '../CopyOnLongPress';

export default function GroupInfoSidebar({
  showGroupInfo, setShowGroupInfo, activeGroup, identity,
  groupMembers, profiles, addMember, kickMember, promoteMember, leaveGroup,
  setProfilePubkey, setToast, updateGroupSettings
}) {
  const [showModLog, setShowModLog] = useState(false);
  const [showSettingsModal, setShowSettingsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showMediaGallery, setShowMediaGallery] = useState(false);
  const [showVendorDir, setShowVendorDir] = useState(false);
  const [showJoinRequests, setShowJoinRequests] = useState(false);
  const joinRequests = useChatStore(s => s.joinRequests);
  const pendingCount = (joinRequests[activeGroup?.id] || []).length;
  const safeGroupMembers = Array.isArray(groupMembers) ? groupMembers : [];

  if (!showGroupInfo || !activeGroup) return null;

  const amIAdmin = activeGroup.creator === identity.pk
    || (safeGroupMembers.find(m => m.pubkey === identity.pk)?.role === 'admin');

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[90] flex justify-end bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowGroupInfo(false)}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0a0a0a] h-full shadow-2xl flex flex-col border-l border-white/10" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between bg-[#111]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={() => setShowGroupInfo(false)} className="text-white/60 hover:text-white"><ArrowLeft size={20} /></button>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Group Info</h2>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 flex flex-col items-center border-b border-white/5">
          <div className={`w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-3 overflow-hidden ${activeGroup.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
            {activeGroup.avatar
              ? <img src={activeGroup.avatar} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="" />
              : activeGroup.isChannel ? <Megaphone size={32} /> : <Users size={32} />}
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">{activeGroup.name}</h3>
          <p className="text-xs mt-1"><CopyOnLongPress value={activeGroup.id} displayValue={activeGroup.id} className="text-white/50 font-mono" /></p>
          <p className="text-xs text-white/40 mt-2 text-center">{activeGroup.about || 'No description'}</p>
          {amIAdmin && (
            <button onClick={() => setShowSettingsModal(true)} className="mt-3 text-xs text-blue-400 font-bold hover:text-blue-300">✏️ Edit Group</button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 px-1">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Members ({safeGroupMembers.length})</p>
            {amIAdmin && (
              <button onClick={() => {
                useChatStore.getState().openDialog({
                  title: 'Add Member', message: 'Enter the pubkey (hex) to add:', hasInput: true,
                  onConfirm: (pk) => { if (pk && pk.trim()) addMember(activeGroup.id, pk.trim()); }
                });
              }} className="text-xs text-blue-400 font-bold hover:text-blue-300">+ Add Member</button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {safeGroupMembers.map(member => {
              const isMe = member.pubkey === identity.pk;
              const isAdmin = member.role === 'admin' || member.pubkey === activeGroup.creator;
              return (
                <div key={member.pubkey} onClick={() => setProfilePubkey(member.pubkey)} className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-white/5 p-3 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
                  <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm ${isAdmin ? 'bg-purple-500/30 text-purple-300' : 'bg-blue-500/30 text-blue-300'}`}>{member.pubkey.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}</div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{isMe ? 'You' : (profiles[member.pubkey]?.displayName || profiles[member.pubkey]?.name || `User ${member.pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`)}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-white/50 uppercase">{isAdmin ? '👑 Admin' : 'Member'}</p>
                  </div>
                  {amIAdmin && !isMe && (
                    <div className="flex gap-1">
                      <button onClick={async (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        if (promoteMember) { await promoteMember(activeGroup.id, member.pubkey, isAdmin); setToast({ type: 'success', message: isAdmin ? 'Demoted' : 'Promoted' }); }
                      }} className="text-[10px] bg-purple-500/20 hover:bg-purple-500/30 text-purple-400 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold">{isAdmin ? 'Demote' : 'Promote'}</button>
                      <button onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        useChatStore.getState().openDialog({
                          title: 'Remove Member', message: `Remove ${profiles[member.pubkey]?.displayName || 'this user'}?`,
                          type: 'danger', confirmText: 'Remove',
                          onConfirm: async () => { await kickMember(activeGroup.id, member.pubkey); setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Removed' }); }
                        });
                      }} className="text-[10px] bg-red-500/20 hover:bg-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold">Remove</button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-8 space-y-2">
            {amIAdmin && pendingCount > 0 && (
              <button onClick={() => setShowJoinRequests(true)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-yellow-500/10 border border-yellow-500/20 rounded-xl text-left">
                <UserPlus size={18} className="text-yellow-400" />
                <div className="flex-1"><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-yellow-400">Join Requests</p><p className="text-[10px] text-yellow-400/60">{pendingCount} pending</p></div>
                <span className="bg-yellow-500 text-black text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{pendingCount}</span>
              </button>
            )}
            <button onClick={() => setShowVendorDir(true)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/20 rounded-xl text-left">
              <Store size={18} className="text-purple-400" />
              <div><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-purple-400">Vendor Directory</p><p className="text-[10px] text-purple-400/60">Browse verified vendors</p></div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setShowMediaGallery(true)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/20 rounded-xl text-left">
              <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" className="text-blue-400"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5"/><polyline points="21 15 16 10 5 21"/></svg>
              <div><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-400">Shared Media</p><p className="text-[10px] text-blue-400/60">Photos and voice</p></div>
            </button>
            {amIAdmin && (
              <button onClick={() => setShowModLog(true)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-yellow-500/10 border border-yellow-500/20 rounded-xl text-left">
                <ShieldAlert size={18} className="text-yellow-400" />
                <div><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-yellow-400">Moderation Log</p><p className="text-[10px] text-yellow-400/60">Admin actions</p></div>
              </button>
            )}
            <button onClick={async () => { setShowGroupInfo(false); if (leaveGroup) await leaveGroup(activeGroup.id); }} className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded-xl hover:bg-red-500/20 text-left">
              <div><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-400">Leave Group</p><p className="text-xs text-red-400/60">Remove yourself</p></div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <GroupSettingsModal isOpen={showSettingsModal} onClose={() => setShowSettingsModal(false)} group={activeGroup} identity={identity} onSave={async (u) => { if (updateGroupSettings) await updateGroupSettings(activeGroup.id, u); }} />
      <JoinRequestsPanel isOpen={showJoinRequests} onClose={() => setShowJoinRequests(false)} groupId={activeGroup.id} profiles={profiles} identity={identity} addMember={addMember} setToast={setToast} />
      <VendorDirectory isOpen={showVendorDir} onClose={() => setShowVendorDir(false)} groupId={activeGroup.id} profiles={profiles} identity={identity} onOpenVendor={(pk) => setProfilePubkey(pk)} />
      <MediaGallery isOpen={showMediaGallery} onClose={() => setShowMediaGallery(false)} chatId={activeGroup.id} isGroup={true} />
      <ModerationLog isOpen={showModLog} onClose={() => setShowModLog(false)} groupId={activeGroup.id} />
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/GroupInviteModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Copy, Check, Share2, QrCode } from 'lucide-react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function GroupInviteModal({ isOpen, onClose, group }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [showQR, setShowQR] = useState(false);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  if (!isOpen || !group) return null;

  const inviteLink = `${window.location.origin}?group=${group.id}&name=${encodeURIComponent(group.name || 'Group')}`;
  const qrValue = `cipher://group/${group.id}`;

  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(inviteLink);
      setCopied(true);
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Invite link copied!' });
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (e) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Failed to copy' });
    }
  };

  const handleNativeShare = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title: `Join ${group.name} on Cipher`,
          text: `Come join my group "${group.name}" on Cipher!`,
          url: inviteLink
        });
      } catch (e) {}
    } else {
      handleCopyLink();
    }
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Invite to {group.name}</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          {!showQR ? (
            <>
              <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-4 mb-4">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-white/40 mb-2">Invite Link</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/80 font-mono break-all mb-3">{inviteLink}</p>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleCopyLink} 
                  className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white text-xs font-bold py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  {copied ? <><Check size={14}/> Copied!</> : <><Copy size={14}/> Copy Link</>}
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setShowQR(true)} 
                  className="bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
                >
                  <QrCode size={24} className="text-white/80" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">Show QR</span>
                </button>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleNativeShare} 
                  className="bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
                >
                  <Share2 size={24} className="text-white/80" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">Share</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 text-center mt-4">
                Anyone with this link can request to join. Group admins still control membership.
              </p>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <button 
                onClick={() => setShowQR(false)} 
                className="text-xs text-blue-400 mb-4 flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                ← Back
              </button>
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl mx-auto mb-4" style={{ width: 'fit-content' }}>
                <QRCodeSVG value={qrValue} size={200} level="H" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-white/60 text-center mb-4">Scan to join {group.name}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 text-center">For in-person meetups and quick joins</p>
            </>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/GroupSettingsModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Camera, Loader } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function GroupSettingsModal({ isOpen, onClose, group, identity, onSave }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [about, setAbout] = useState('');
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState('');
  const [requireApproval, setRequireApproval] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (group) {
      setName(group.name || '');
      setAbout(group.about || '');
      setAvatar(group.avatar || '');
      setRequireApproval(group.requireApproval === true);
    }
  }, [group]);

  if (!isOpen || !group) return null;

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(file, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => identity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
            return finalizeEvent(event, identity.sk);
          }
        }
      });
      setAvatar(blob.url);
    } catch (err) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Upload failed' });
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Group name is required' });
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    await onSave({ name: name.trim(), about: about.trim(), avatar, requireApproval });
    setSaving(false);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-start justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '-100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '-100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-b-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pt-10"
        >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Group Settings</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="flex justify-center">
              <div 
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="relative w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-[#2c2c2e] border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden cursor-pointer hover:border-blue-500/50 transition-colors group"
              >
                {avatar ? (
                  <img src={avatar} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="avatar" />
                ) : (
                  <Camera size={28} className="text-white/40 group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors" />
                )}
                {uploading && (
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Loader size={24} className="animate-spin text-white" />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <input type="file" accept="image/*" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleAvatarUpload} className="hidden" />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Group Name</label>
              <input 
                type="text"
                value={name}
                onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Group name"
                className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Description</label>
              <textarea 
                value={about}
                onChange={e => setAbout(e.target.value)}
                rows={2}
                placeholder="What is this group about?"
                className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none resize-none"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3.5 mt-1">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">Require Approval to Join</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-white/40">New members wait for admin approval</p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setRequireApproval(!requireApproval)}
              className={`w-12 h-6 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors ${requireApproval ? 'bg-blue-500' : 'bg-white/20'}`}
            >
              <div className={`w-5 h-5 bg-white rounded-full shadow-sm transition-transform ${requireApproval ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-0'}`} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <button 
            onClick={handleSave}
            disabled={saving || uploading || !name.trim()}
            className="w-full mt-6 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-white/10 disabled:text-white/30 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors"
          >
            {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Changes'}
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/GroupView.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import MessageBubble from '../MessageBubble';
import ChatHeader from './ChatHeader';
import ChatPinnedBar from './ChatPinnedBar';
import ChatReplyPreview from './ChatReplyPreview';
import MessageInput from '../MessageInput';
import MessageActionsSheet from './MessageActionsSheet';
import GroupInfoSidebar from './GroupInfoSidebar';
import ProfileModal from '../ProfileModal';
import ForwardModal from './ForwardModal';
import FullEmojiPicker from './FullEmojiPicker';
import ReviewModal from './ReviewModal';
import ReactionPicker from './ReactionPicker';
import ReactionsDetail from './ReactionsDetail';
import MessageEditHistory from './MessageEditHistory';
import ImageLightbox from '../ImageLightbox';
import DateSeparator from './DateSeparator';
import PostListingModal from './PostListingModal';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { scopedGet, scopedSet, scopedRemove } from '../../lib/storage';
import { useReactions } from '../../hooks/useReactions';
import { useReputation } from '../../hooks/useReputation';
import { useScamReports } from '../../hooks/useScamReports';
import { useNIP29Group } from '../../hooks/useNIP29Group';
import { useGroupKeyReconciler } from '../../hooks/useGroupKeyReconciler';

export default function GroupView({
  identity,
  sendMessage,
  handleMessageAction,
  handleImageUpload,
  startRecording,
  stopRecording,
  handleForwardSubmit,
}) {
  const activeGroup = useChatStore(s => s.activeGroup);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const deletedMsgs = useChatStore(s => s.deletedMsgs);
  const reactions = useChatStore(s => s.reactions);
  const selectedMessage = useChatStore(s => s.selectedMessage);
  const forwardingMsg = useChatStore(s => s.forwardingMsg);
  const showFullEmoji = useChatStore(s => s.showFullEmoji);
  const reviewTarget = useChatStore(s => s.reviewTarget);
  const profilePubkey = useChatStore(s => s.profilePubkey);
  const newMessage = useChatStore(s => s.newMessage);
  const isRecording = useChatStore(s => s.isRecording);
  const uploadingImage = useChatStore(s => s.uploadingImage);
  const showGroupInfo = useChatStore(s => s.showGroupInfo);
  const groupMembers = useChatStore(s => s.groupMembers);

  const setSelectedMessage = useChatStore(s => s.setSelectedMessage);
  const setForwardingMsg = useChatStore(s => s.setForwardingMsg);
  const setShowFullEmoji = useChatStore(s => s.setShowFullEmoji);
  const setReviewTarget = useChatStore(s => s.setReviewTarget);
  const setProfilePubkey = useChatStore(s => s.setProfilePubkey);
  const setNewMessage = useChatStore(s => s.setNewMessage);
  const setShowGroupInfo = useChatStore(s => s.setShowGroupInfo);
  const setVendorStorefrontPubkey = useChatStore(s => s.setVendorStorefrontPubkey);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);
  const openChat = useChatStore(s => s.openChat);

  const { sendReaction } = useReactions({ identity });
  const { sendReview } = useReputation({ identity });
  const { isScammer } = useScamReports({ identity, targetPubkey: activeGroup?.creator });

  const {
    unpinMessage,
    addMember,
    kickMember,
    promoteMember,
    leaveGroup,
    updateGroupSettings,
    fetchGroupMembers,
    requestGroupKey,
  } = useNIP29Group({ identity });

  const groupMsgs = groupMessages[activeGroup?.id] || [];
  const scrollRef = useRef(null);
  const [lightboxUrl, setLightboxUrl] = useState(null);
  const [showListingModal, setShowListingModal] = useState(false);
  const [emojiTarget, setEmojiTarget] = useState(null);
  const [reactionsDetail, setReactionsDetail] = useState(null);
  const [editingHistoryMsg, setEditingHistoryMsg] = useState(null);

  const selectionMode = useChatStore(s => s.selectionMode);
  const selectedMsgs = useChatStore(s => s.selectedMsgs);
  const toggleSelectedMsg = useChatStore(s => s.toggleSelectedMsg);

  const canPost = !activeGroup?.isChannel || activeGroup?.creator === identity?.pk;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeGroup?.id) {
      const draft = scopedGet(`draft-group-${activeGroup.id}`, '') || '';
      setNewMessage(draft);
    }
  }, [activeGroup?.id, setNewMessage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeGroup?.id) {
      fetchGroupMembers(activeGroup.id);
      const interval = setInterval(() => fetchGroupMembers(activeGroup.id), 30000);
      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
  }, [activeGroup?.id, fetchGroupMembers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeGroup?.id) {
      import('../../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore').then(({ getCurrentGroupKey }) => {
        getCurrentGroupKey(activeGroup.id).then(key => {
          if (!key) requestGroupKey(activeGroup.id);
        });
      });
    }
  }, [activeGroup?.id, requestGroupKey]);

  useGroupKeyReconciler({
    groupId: activeGroup?.id || null,
    members: (Array.isArray(groupMembers) ? groupMembers : []).map(m => m.pubkey),
    isOwner: activeGroup?.creator === identity?.pk,
    identity,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
  }, [activeGroup?.id, groupMsgs.length, reactions, forwardingMsg]);

  const _vendorSet = new Set();
  groupMsgs.forEach(m => {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}');
      if (p.type === 'product') _vendorSet.add(m.pubkey);
    } catch {}
  });
  const _vendorList = Array.from(_vendorSet);
  const [_vouchMap, _setVouchMap] = useState({});

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const { getAuthenticatedPool } = await import('../../lib/pool');
      const { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } = await import('../../lib/relays');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const newMap = {};
      await Promise.all(_vendorList.map(async (pk) => {
        try {
          const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10004], '#p': [pk], limit: 200 }, 3000);
          const unique = new Set(events.filter(e => e.pubkey !== pk).map(e => e.pubkey));
          newMap[pk] = unique.size;
        } catch {
          newMap[pk] = 0;
        }
      }));
      if (!cancelled) _setVouchMap(newMap);
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [_vendorList.join(','), identity?.pk]);

  const handleReplyClick = (msgId) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(`msg-${msgId}`);
    if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  };

  if (!activeGroup) return null;

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ x: 20, opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ x: 20, opacity: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
      className="absolute inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a] pb-[72px]"
    >
      <ChatHeader mode="group" identity={identity} />
      <ChatPinnedBar unpinMessage={unpinMessage} />

      <div ref={scrollRef} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden min-h-0 p-4 space-y-4">
        {groupMsgs.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center text-white/30 mt-10 text-sm">No messages yet. Say hi!</div>
        )}

        {groupMsgs.map((msg, i) => {
          if (deletedMsgs.has(msg.id)) return null;
          const prevMsg = i > 0 ? groupMsgs[i - 1] : null;
          const showDate = !prevMsg || new Date(msg.timestamp * 1000).toDateString() !== new Date(prevMsg.timestamp * 1000).toDateString();
          const showTail = i === 0 || groupMsgs[i - 1].isSender !== msg.isSender;
          const repliedMsg = msg.replyTo ? groupMsgs.find(m => m.id === msg.replyTo) : null;

          let parsed = {};
          try { parsed = JSON.parse(msg.rawContent || '{}'); } catch { parsed = { type: 'text', message: msg.rawContent || '' }; }

          let repliedParsed = {};
          try { repliedParsed = JSON.parse(repliedMsg?.rawContent || '{}'); } catch {}

          return (
            <React.Fragment key={msg.id}>
              {showDate && <DateSeparator timestamp={msg.timestamp} />}
              <MessageBubble
                msg={msg}
                data={{ ...parsed, vouchCount: _vouchMap[msg.pubkey] || 0 }}
                isScammer={isScammer}
                reactions={reactions[msg.id]}
                onReact={(emoji) => sendReaction(msg.id, emoji, true, activeGroup.id, msg.pubkey)}
                onAction={handleMessageAction}
                myPubkey={identity.pk}
                showTail={showTail}
                repliedTo={repliedMsg}
                repliedData={repliedParsed}
                onSelect={(m, d, action) => {
                  if (action === 'close') setSelectedMessage(null);
                  else if (action === 'open_picker') {
                    setShowFullEmoji(true);
                    setSelectedMessage({ id: msg.id, pubkey: msg.pubkey, text: parsed.message || parsed.url });
                  } else {
                    setSelectedMessage({ id: msg.id, pubkey: msg.pubkey, text: parsed.message || parsed.url });
                  }
                }}
                onReplyAction={() => setSelectedMessage({ id: msg.id, pubkey: msg.pubkey, text: parsed.message || parsed.url })}
                onImageClick={setLightboxUrl}
                onReplyClick={handleReplyClick}
                onShowReactions={() => setReactionsDetail(reactions[msg.id])}
                onShowEdits={(m) => setEditingHistoryMsg(m)}
                selectionMode={selectionMode}
                isSelectedMsg={selectedMsgs.includes(msg.id)}
                onToggleSelect={(id) => toggleSelectedMsg(id)}
                isSelected={selectedMessage?.id === msg.id}
                isGroup={true}
                onSenderClick={(pk) => setProfilePubkey(pk)}
                onVendorClick={(pk) => setVendorStorefrontPubkey(pk)}
                senderName={profiles[msg.pubkey]?.displayName || profiles[msg.pubkey]?.name || `User ${(msg.pubkey || 'anon').slice(0, 4)}`}
              />
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        })}
        <div className="h-4" />
      </div>

      <ChatReplyPreview />

      <MessageInput
        value={newMessage}
        onChange={(val) => {
          setNewMessage(val);
          if (val.trim() && activeGroup?.id) scopedSet(`draft-group-${activeGroup.id}`, val);
          else if (activeGroup?.id) scopedRemove(`draft-group-${activeGroup.id}`);
        }}
        onSend={() => {
          if (newMessage.trim()) {
            sendMessage(newMessage);
            setNewMessage('');
          }
        }}
        canPost={canPost}
        isChannel={activeGroup.isChannel}
        onImageUpload={handleImageUpload}
        startRecording={startRecording}
        stopRecording={stopRecording}
        isRecording={isRecording}
        uploadingImage={uploadingImage}
        onPostListing={() => setShowListingModal(true)}
      />

      <GroupInfoSidebar
        showGroupInfo={showGroupInfo}
        setShowGroupInfo={setShowGroupInfo}
        activeGroup={activeGroup}
        identity={identity}
        groupMembers={groupMembers}
        profiles={profiles}
        addMember={addMember}
        kickMember={kickMember}
        promoteMember={promoteMember}
        leaveGroup={leaveGroup}
        setProfilePubkey={setProfilePubkey}
        setToast={setToast}
        updateGroupSettings={updateGroupSettings}
      />

      {profilePubkey && (
        <ProfileModal
          pubkey={profilePubkey}
          profile={profiles[profilePubkey]}
          identity={identity}
          onClose={() => setProfilePubkey(null)}
          onMessage={(pk) => { openChat('dm', pk); setProfilePubkey(null); }}
          onReview={(pk) => setReviewTarget(pk)}
          onViewStorefront={(pk) => setVendorStorefrontPubkey(pk)}
        />
      )}

      <MessageActionsSheet
        isOpen={!!selectedMessage}
        onClose={() => setSelectedMessage(null)}
        onAction={(action, emoji) => {
          if (action === 'open_picker') {
            setEmojiTarget(selectedMessage);
            setSelectedMessage(null);
            return;
          }
          handleMessageAction(action, emoji);
        }}
        msg={selectedMessage}
        isAdmin={activeGroup.creator === identity.pk}
        isMine={selectedMessage?.pubkey === identity.pk}
        canEdit={selectedMessage?.pubkey === identity.pk && selectedMessage?.data?.type === 'text'}
      />

      {emojiTarget && (
        <ReactionPicker
          onSelect={(emoji) => {
            sendReaction(emojiTarget.id, emoji, true, activeGroup.id, emojiTarget.pubkey);
            setEmojiTarget(null);
          }}
          onClose={() => setEmojiTarget(null)}
        />
      )}

      <ReactionsDetail
        isOpen={!!reactionsDetail}
        onClose={() => setReactionsDetail(null)}
        reactions={reactionsDetail}
        profiles={profiles}
      />

      <MessageEditHistory
        isOpen={!!editingHistoryMsg}
        onClose={() => setEditingHistoryMsg(null)}
        msg={editingHistoryMsg}
      />

      {forwardingMsg && (
        <ForwardModal
          isOpen={!!forwardingMsg}
          onClose={() => setForwardingMsg(null)}
          onForward={handleForwardSubmit}
        />
      )}

      {showFullEmoji && selectedMessage && (
        <FullEmojiPicker
          onSelect={(emoji) => {
            const id = selectedMessage.id;
            const pk = selectedMessage.pubkey;
            setShowFullEmoji(false);
            setSelectedMessage(null);
            if (id) sendReaction(id, emoji, true, activeGroup.id, pk);
          }}
          onClose={() => {
            setShowFullEmoji(false);
            setSelectedMessage(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {reviewTarget && (
        <ReviewModal
          targetName={profiles[reviewTarget]?.displayName || profiles[reviewTarget]?.name || `User ${reviewTarget.slice(0, 4)}`}
          onSubmit={async (rating, text) => {
            const res = await sendReview(reviewTarget, rating, text);
            if (res.success) {
              setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Review published!' });
              return true;
            }
            setToast({ type: 'error', message: res.error || 'Failed to publish' });
            return false;
          }}
          onClose={() => setReviewTarget(null)}
        />
      )}

      <PostListingModal
        isOpen={showListingModal}
        onClose={() => setShowListingModal(false)}
        onSubmit={async (data) => {
          const payload = {
            type: 'product',
            title: data.title,
            priceUsd: data.priceUsd,
            image: data.image,
            description: data.description,
          };
          await sendMessage('', false, null, null, payload);
        }}
      />

      <ImageLightbox url={lightboxUrl} onClose={() => setLightboxUrl(null)} />
    </motion.div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/IdentityBackup.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Download, Upload, Copy, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, Key } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { nip19 } from 'nostr-tools';

export default function IdentityBackup({ isOpen, onClose, identity }) {
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);
  const openDialog = useChatStore(s => s.openDialog);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('backup'); // 'backup' | 'restore' | 'qr'
  const [restoreInput, setRestoreInput] = useState('');
  const [restoring, setRestoring] = useState(false);
  const [newPin, setNewPin] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleExport = async () => {
    if (!identity?.sk) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Not unlocked' });
      return;
    }
    try {
      const nsec = nip19.nsecEncode(identity.sk);
      let mnemonic = null;
      try { mnemonic = await identity.getMnemonic?.(); } catch (e) {}

      const lines = [
        'CIPHER ACCOUNT RECOVERY',
        '=======================',
        'Exported: ' + new Date().toLocaleString(),
        '',
        'Public key (npub): ' + (identity.npub || identity.pk),
        'Private key (nsec): ' + nsec,
      ];
      if (mnemonic) {
        lines.push('');
        lines.push('Recovery Phrase (12 words):');
        lines.push(mnemonic);
        lines.push('');
        lines.push('To recover on a new device: open Cipher, tap "Restore it",');
        lines.push('choose "Use Recovery Phrase", paste the words above,');
        lines.push('and pick a new PIN.');
      }
      lines.push('');
      lines.push('WARNING: Never share this file.');
      lines.push('Anyone with these keys controls this account.');

      const blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = 'cipher-recovery-' + (identity.npub || identity.pk).slice(0, 8) + '.txt';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      document.body.removeChild(a);
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Recovery file downloaded' });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Download failed: ' + (e.message || 'unknown') });
    }
  };

  const handleCopyNpub = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(identity.npub || identity.pk);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (e) {}
  };

  const handleRestore = async () => {
    if (!restoreInput.trim()) return;
    setRestoring(true);
    try {
      let sk;
      const input = restoreInput.trim();
      
      if (input.startsWith('nsec1')) {
        const decoded = nip19.decode(input);
        sk = decoded.data;
      } else if (/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/i.test(input)) {
        // Hex private key
        const bytes = new Uint8Array(32);
        for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) bytes[i] = parseInt(input.substr(i * 2, 2), 16);
        sk = bytes;
      } else {
        throw new Error('Invalid key format. Must be nsec1... or 64-char hex.');
      }

      if (newPin.length < 4) throw new Error('Please set a PIN (4+ digits) to secure this account');
      
      const { encryptKey } = await import('../../useIdentity');
      const { getPublicKey } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk);
      
      const vault = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), newPin);
      const vaults = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_vaults') || '{}');
      
      // Generate a sensible fallback handle instead of hardcoding 'restored_user'
      const defaultHandle = 'user_' + pk.slice(0, 6);
      vaults[pk] = { vault, handle: defaultHandle, displayName: 'Restored User', createdAt: Date.now() };
      
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vaults', JSON.stringify(vaults));
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_active_pubkey', pk);
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(vault));
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_handle', defaultHandle);
      
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)));
      
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Identity restored! Reloading...' });
      setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 1000);
    } catch (err) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: err.message });
    } finally {
      setRestoring(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2"><Key size={18} className="text-blue-400"/> Identity & Backup</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          {/* Tabs */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 bg-[#0a0a0a] p-1 rounded-xl">
            <button onClick={() => setTab('backup')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors ${tab === 'backup' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/50'}`}>Backup</button>
            <button onClick={() => setTab('qr')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors ${tab === 'qr' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/50'}`}>Share QR</button>
            <button onClick={() => setTab('restore')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors ${tab === 'restore' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/50'}`}>Restore</button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            {tab === 'backup' && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 flex gap-2">
                  <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-red-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-red-400/80">Never share your private key with anyone. Cipher staff will never ask for it.</p>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-4">
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-white/40 mb-2">Public ID (Safe to share)</p>
                  <p className="text-xs font-mono text-white/80 break-all mb-3">{identity.npub || identity.pk}</p>
                  <button onClick={handleCopyNpub} className="w-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    {copied ? <><CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-green-400"/> Copied!</> : <><Copy size={14}/> Copy Public ID</>}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <button onClick={handleExport} className="w-full bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <Download size={16} /> Export Private Key
                </button>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 text-center">Save this to a password manager or offline. You'll need it to restore if you lose this device.</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {tab === 'qr' && (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                <p className="text-sm text-white/60 mb-4 text-center">Have someone scan this to add you</p>
                <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl mb-4">
                  <QRCodeSVG value={`cipher://user/${identity.npub || identity.pk}`} size={220} level="H" />
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/40 text-center">Works for vendor meetups, in-person trades, and quick DM exchanges</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {tab === 'restore' && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="bg-yellow-500/10 border border-yellow-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 flex gap-2">
                  <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-yellow-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-yellow-400/80">Restoring will replace your current identity. Export your current key first if you need it.</p>
                </div>
                <input 
                  type="password"
                  placeholder="Set a new PIN (4+ digits)"
                  value={newPin}
                  onChange={e => setNewPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8))}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-white font-mono focus:border-blue-500 outline-none mb-2"
                />
                <textarea 
                  value={restoreInput}
                  onChange={e => setRestoreInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Paste your nsec1... or 64-char hex private key"
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-white font-mono focus:border-blue-500 outline-none resize-none"
                />
                <button 
                  onClick={handleRestore}
                  disabled={restoring || !restoreInput.trim()}
                  className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-white/10 disabled:text-white/30 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  {restoring ? 'Restoring...' : <><Upload size={16}/> Restore Identity</>}
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/ImageCropper.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import Cropper from 'react-easy-crop';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Check } from 'lucide-react';

async function getCroppedBlob(imageSrc, cropPixels) {
  const img = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const i = new Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.onload = () => resolve(i);
    i.onerror = reject;
    i.src = imageSrc;
  });
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  const size = Math.min(cropPixels.width, cropPixels.height);
  canvas.width = size;
  canvas.height = size;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(
    img,
    cropPixels.x, cropPixels.y, cropPixels.width, cropPixels.height,
    0, 0, size, size
  );
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    canvas.toBlob((blob) => resolve(blob), 'image/jpeg', 0.9);
  });
}

export default function ImageCropper({ imageSrc, onComplete, onClose }) {
  const [crop, setCrop] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [croppedPixels, setCroppedPixels] = useState(null);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);

  const onCropComplete = useCallback((_, pixels) => {
    setCroppedPixels(pixels);
  }, []);

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!croppedPixels) return;
    setProcessing(true);
    try {
      const blob = await getCroppedBlob(imageSrc, croppedPixels);
      const file = new File([blob], 'avatar.jpg', { type: 'image/jpeg' });
      onComplete(file);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      setProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[100000] bg-black flex flex-col"
      >
        <div className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center border-b border-white/10">
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/60">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
          <h2 className="text-white font-bold">Crop Photo</h2>
          <button
            onClick={handleSave}
            disabled={processing || !croppedPixels}
            className="p-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full text-white disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <Check size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 relative">
          <Cropper
            image={imageSrc}
            crop={crop}
            zoom={zoom}
            aspect={1}
            cropShape="round"
            showGrid={false}
            onCropChange={setCrop}
            onCropComplete={onCropComplete}
            onZoomChange={setZoom}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="text-xs text-white/50 w-12">Zoom</span>
            <input
              type="range"
              min={1} max={3} step={0.01}
              value={zoom}
              onChange={e => setZoom(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
              className="flex-1 accent-blue-500"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/JoinRequestsPanel.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, UserPlus, Check, X as XIcon, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function JoinRequestsPanel({ isOpen, onClose, groupId, profiles, identity, addMember, setToast }) {
  const joinRequests = useChatStore(s => s.joinRequests);
  const removeJoinRequest = useChatStore(s => s.removeJoinRequest);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState({});

  if (!isOpen || !groupId) return null;
  
  const requests = joinRequests[groupId] || [];

  const handleApprove = async (pubkey) => {
    setProcessing(p => ({ ...p, [pubkey]: 'approving' }));
    try {
      await addMember(groupId, pubkey);
      removeJoinRequest(groupId, pubkey);
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Member approved' });
    } catch (e) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Failed to approve' });
    } finally {
      setProcessing(p => { const n = { ...p }; delete n[pubkey]; return n; });
    }
  };

  const handleDeny = (pubkey) => {
    setProcessing(p => ({ ...p, [pubkey]: 'denying' }));
    removeJoinRequest(groupId, pubkey);
    setToast({ type: 'info', message: 'Request denied' });
    setProcessing(p => { const n = { ...p }; delete n[pubkey]; return n; });
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-start justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '-100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '-100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-b-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pt-10 max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col"
        >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <UserPlus size={18} className="text-blue-400" /> Join Requests ({requests.length})
            </h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {requests.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <UserPlus size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">No pending requests</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/30 mt-1">New members waiting for approval will appear here</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              requests.map(pubkey => {
                const profile = profiles[pubkey] || {};
                const isApproving = processing[pubkey] === 'approving';
                const isDenying = processing[pubkey] === 'denying';
                return (
                  <div key={pubkey} className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                      {profile.picture ? (
                        <img src={profile.picture} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover" alt="" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                          {(profile.displayName || 'U').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{profile.displayName || `User ${pubkey.slice(0, 8)}`}</p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 font-mono truncate">{pubkey.slice(0, 24)}...</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <button onClick={() => handleDeny(pubkey)} disabled={isDenying || isApproving} className="flex-1 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-white/60 text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50">
                        <XIcon size={14} /> Deny
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => handleApprove(pubkey)} disabled={isApproving || isDenying} className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50">
                        <Check size={14} /> {isApproving ? 'Approving...' : 'Approve'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/MediaGallery.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Image as ImageIcon, Play, Mic } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function MediaGallery({ isOpen, onClose, chatId, isGroup }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('photos');
  const [lightbox, setLightbox] = useState(null);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);

  if (!isOpen || !chatId) return null;

  const msgs = isGroup ? (groupMessages[chatId] || []) : (dms[chatId] || []);
  
  const items = [];
  msgs.forEach(m => {
    let parsed = {};
    if (isGroup) {
      try { parsed = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
    } else {
      parsed = m.data || {};
    }
    
    if (parsed.type === 'image' && parsed.url) {
      items.push({ type: 'photo', url: parsed.url, id: m.id, timestamp: m.timestamp });
    } else if (parsed.type === 'album' && parsed.urls) {
      parsed.urls.forEach((url, i) => {
        items.push({ type: 'photo', url, id: `${m.id}-${i}`, timestamp: m.timestamp });
      });
    } else if (parsed.type === 'audio' && parsed.url) {
      items.push({ type: 'audio', url: parsed.url, id: m.id, timestamp: m.timestamp });
    }
  });

  items.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

  const filtered = filter === 'photos' 
    ? items.filter(i => i.type === 'photo')
    : items.filter(i => i.type === 'audio');

  const photoCount = items.filter(i => i.type === 'photo').length;
  const audioCount = items.filter(i => i.type === 'audio').length;

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-start justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '-100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '-100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-b-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pt-10 max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col"
        >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Shared Media</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-1 bg-[#0a0a0a] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4">
            <button onClick={() => setFilter('photos')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${filter === 'photos' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/40'}`}>
              <ImageIcon size={12} /> Photos ({photoCount})
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setFilter('audio')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${filter === 'audio' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/40'}`}>
              <Mic size={12} /> Voice ({audioCount})
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <ImageIcon size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">No {filter === 'photos' ? 'photos' : 'voice messages'} yet</p>
              </div>
            ) : filter === 'photos' ? (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1">
                {filtered.map(item => (
                  <button key={item.id} onClick={() => setLightbox(item.url)} className="aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-[#0a0a0a]">
                    <img src={item.url} className="w-full h-full object-cover hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity" alt="" />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {filtered.map(item => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3">
                    <audio controls src={item.url} className="w-full h-10" />
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-1">{new Date(item.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleString()}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>

        {lightbox && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[100000] bg-black/95 flex items-center justify-center" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setLightbox(null); }}>
            <button onClick={() => setLightbox(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-3 bg-white/10 rounded-full text-white"><X size={20}/></button>
            <img src={lightbox} className="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain" alt="" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} />
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

export default function MessageActionsSheet({
  isOpen, onClose, onAction, msg, isAdmin, isMine, canEdit

      setCanClose(false);
    }

  if (!isOpen || !msg) return null;

  const emojis = ['❤️', '👍', '🔥', '😂', '😮', '😢', '🙏'];

        className="bg-[#1c1c1e] w-[300px] rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-white/10 overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-100"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}

        {/* Emojis */}
        <div className="flex justify-around p-2 border-b border-white/10 bg-[#2c2c2e]">
          {emojis.map(emoji => (

import React from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, History } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function MessageEditHistory({ isOpen, onClose, msg }) {
