=== FILE: src/components/chat/MessageRequests.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { ChevronLeft, UserPlus, X, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { scopedGet, scopedSet } from '../../lib/storage';

export default function MessageRequests({ identity, setView, openChat }) {
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const setDms = useChatStore(s => s.setDms);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);
  const [acceptedReqs, setAcceptedReqs] = useState(() => scopedGet('accepted_reqs', []));

  // A "request" is a DM from a pubkey you've never accepted
  const requests = useMemo(() => {
    return Object.entries(dms)
      .filter(([pubkey]) => !acceptedReqs.includes(pubkey))
      .map(([pubkey, msgs]) => {
        const lastMsg = msgs[msgs.length - 1];
        const preview = lastMsg?.data?.message || lastMsg?.data?.url || '(media)';
        return {
          pubkey,
          name: profiles[pubkey]?.displayName || `User ${pubkey.slice(0, 8)}`,
          preview,
          timestamp: lastMsg?.timestamp || 0,
          count: msgs.length
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
  }, [dms, acceptedReqs, profiles]);

  const handleAccept = (pubkey) => {
    const updated = [...acceptedReqs, pubkey];
    setAcceptedReqs(updated);
    scopedSet('accepted_reqs', updated);
    setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Request accepted' });
    openChat('dm', pubkey);
  };

  const handleDecline = (pubkey) => {
    // Remove from DMs (but keep localStorage marker so they don't come back)
    setDms(prev => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      delete next[pubkey];
      return next;
    });
    setToast({ type: 'info', message: 'Request declined' });
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a] pb-24">
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/5 flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
        <button onClick={() => setView('main')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white">
          <ChevronLeft size={24} />
        </button>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Message Requests</h2>
        {requests.length > 0 && (
          <span className="ml-auto bg-blue-500 text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{requests.length}</span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-2">
        {requests.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center text-white/40 mt-20">
            <UserPlus size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-30" />
            <p className="text-sm">No pending requests</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-white/30 mt-1">New DMs from strangers will appear here</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          requests.map(r => (
            <div key={r.pubkey} className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                  {r.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{r.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-white/40">{r.count} message{r.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-white/60 mb-3 line-clamp-2">{r.preview}</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => handleDecline(r.pubkey)}
                  className="flex-1 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-white/60 text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
                >
                  <X size={14} /> Decline
                </button>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => handleAccept(r.pubkey)}
                  className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
                >
                  <Check size={14} /> Accept
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/MessageSearchResults.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { ChevronLeft, Search, MessageSquare, Users, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function MessageSearchResults({ identity, setView, openChat }) {
  const searchResults = useChatStore(s => s.searchResults);
  const searchQuery = useChatStore(s => s.searchQuery);

  const handleJump = (result) => {
    // Navigate to the chat, then scroll to the message
    if (result.type === 'dm') {
      openChat('dm', result.pubkey);
      setTimeout(() => {
        const el = document.getElementById(`msg-${result.msg.id}`);
        if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
      }, 500);
    } else {
      const groups = useChatStore.getState().groups;
      const group = groups.find(g => g.id === result.groupId);
      if (group) {
        openChat('group', result.groupId, group);
        setTimeout(() => {
          const el = document.getElementById(`msg-${result.msg.id}`);
          if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        }, 500);
      }
    }
  };

  const formatTime = (ts) => {
    const d = new Date(ts * 1000);
    return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a] pb-24">
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/5 flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
        <button onClick={() => setView('list')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white">
          <ChevronLeft size={24} />
        </button>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Search Results</h2>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40">{searchResults.length} result{searchResults.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} for "{searchQuery}"</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-2">
        {searchResults.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center text-white/40 mt-20">
            <Search size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-30" />
            <p className="text-sm">No messages found</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-white/30 mt-1">Try a different search term</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          searchResults.map((r, i) => (
            <button
              key={i}
              onClick={() => handleJump(r)}
              className="w-full bg-[#111] hover:bg-[#1a1a1a] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-start gap-3 text-left transition-colors active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${r.type === 'dm' ? 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400' : 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400'}`}>
                {r.type === 'dm' ? <MessageSquare size={18} /> : <Users size={18} />}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{r.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 shrink-0 ml-2">{formatTime(r.msg.timestamp)}</p>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/60 line-clamp-2">{r.text}</p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/30 uppercase mt-1">{r.type === 'dm' ? 'Direct Message' : 'Group'}</p>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/ModerationLog.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, ShieldAlert, UserX, UserCheck, Megaphone, FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import { db } from '../../db';

export default function ModerationLog({ isOpen, onClose, groupId }) {
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !groupId) return;
    const fetchLogs = async () => {
      try {
        // Scan local messages for system events about this group
        const allMsgs = await db.messages.where('chatId').equals(groupId).toArray();
        const sysLogs = allMsgs
          .filter(m => {
            try {
              const p = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}');
              return p.type === 'system' || p.type === 'order_update';
            } catch { return false; }
          })
          .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
          .slice(0, 100);
        setLogs(sysLogs);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchLogs();
  }, [isOpen, groupId]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const getIcon = (msg) => {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(msg.rawContent || '{}');
      const text = (p.message || '').toLowerCase();
      if (text.includes('kicked') || text.includes('removed')) return <UserX size={16} className="text-red-400" />;
      if (text.includes('joined') || text.includes('added')) return <UserCheck size={16} className="text-green-400" />;
      if (text.includes('promoted') || text.includes('admin')) return <ShieldAlert size={16} className="text-purple-400" />;
      if (text.includes('name') || text.includes('description')) return <FileText size={16} className="text-blue-400" />;
      return <Megaphone size={16} className="text-white/50" />;
    } catch { return <Megaphone size={16} className="text-white/50" />; }
  };

  const getText = (msg) => {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(msg.rawContent || '{}');
      return p.message || p.productName || 'System event';
    } catch { return 'System event'; }
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-start justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '-100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '-100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-b-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pt-10 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col"
        >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2"><ShieldAlert size={18} className="text-yellow-400"/> Moderation Log</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-white/40 mb-4">A permanent record of every action taken in this group. Sunlight is the best disinfectant.</p>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {loading ? (
              <p className="text-center text-white/40 py-8">Loading...</p>
            ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-center text-white/40 py-8 text-sm">No moderation history yet.</p>
            ) : (
              logs.map((msg, i) => (
                <div key={`${msg.id}-${i}`} className="flex gap-3 p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                  <div className="shrink-0 mt-0.5">{getIcon(msg)}</div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs text-white/90">{getText(msg)}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-white/30 mt-0.5">{new Date(msg.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleString()}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/MyOrders.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { Package, ChevronRight, Truck, CheckCircle2, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function MyOrders({ identity, setView }) {
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);

  // Aggregate all orders from all DMs
  const allOrders = [];
  Object.entries(dms).forEach(([peerPubkey, msgs]) => {
    msgs.forEach(msg => {
      if (msg.data?.type === 'cipher_order' || msg.data?.type === 'order_request') {
        allOrders.push({ ...msg, peerPubkey });
      }
    });
  });

  // Sort newest first
  allOrders.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

  const getStatusUI = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'completed': return { icon: <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="text-green-400" />, label: 'Delivered', color: 'text-green-400 bg-green-400/10 border-green-400/20' };
      case 'shipped': return { icon: <Truck size={16} className="text-purple-400" />, label: 'Shipped', color: 'text-purple-400 bg-purple-400/10 border-purple-400/20' };
      case 'accepted': return { icon: <Package size={16} className="text-blue-400" />, label: 'Processing', color: 'text-blue-400 bg-blue-400/10 border-blue-400/20' };
      case 'pending': return { icon: <Clock size={16} className="text-yellow-400" />, label: 'Pending', color: 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-400/10 border-yellow-400/20' };
      default: return { icon: <Package size={16} className="text-white/50" />, label: status?.toUpperCase() || 'UNKNOWN', color: 'text-white/50 bg-white/5 border-white/10' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-full p-4 animate-fade-in flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pb-24 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
        <button onClick={() => setView('main')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white">
          <ChevronRight size={24} className="rotate-180" />
        </button>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">My Orders</h2>
      </div>

      {allOrders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center text-white/40 mt-20">
          <Package size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-50" />
          <p>No orders yet.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {allOrders.map((order, i) => {
            const statusUI = getStatusUI(order.data.status);
            const product = order.data.product || order.data; 
            return (
              <div key={`${order.id}-${i}`} className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4 flex gap-4 items-center">
                {product.image ? (
                  <img src={product.image} className="w-14 h-14 rounded-xl object-cover" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-xl bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Package size={20} className="text-white/30" />
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{product.productName || product.title || 'Unknown Product'}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-white/50 truncate">Vendor: {profiles[order.peerPubkey]?.displayName || order.peerPubkey.slice(0, 8)}</p>
                </div>
                <div className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border flex items-center gap-1.5 ${statusUI.color}`}>
                  {statusUI.icon}
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{statusUI.label}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/MyProfileModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { scopedGet, scopedSet } from '../../lib/storage';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, groupRelays } from '../../lib/relays';

export default function MyProfileModal({ identity }) {
  const showMyProfile = useChatStore((s) => s.showMyProfile);
  const setShowMyProfile = useChatStore((s) => s.setShowMyProfile);
  const myName = useChatStore((s) => s.myName);
  const setMyName = useChatStore((s) => s.setMyName);
  const myUsername = useChatStore((s) => s.myUsername);
  const setMyUsername = useChatStore((s) => s.setMyUsername);
  const myAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.myAbout);
  const setMyAbout = useChatStore((s) => s.setMyAbout);
  const setProfiles = useChatStore((s) => s.setProfiles);
  const setToast = useChatStore((s) => s.setToast);

  const [lockedUsername, setLockedUsername] = useState(
    () => scopedGet('my_username', '')
  );
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  if (!showMyProfile) return null;

  const handleSave = async () => {
    try {
      setSaving(true);
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      // Publish Kind 0
      const profileEv = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 0,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [],
        content: JSON.stringify({
          name: myName,
          display_name: myName,
          about: myAbout,
          nip05: lockedUsername || myUsername
        })
      }, identity.sk);
      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, profileEv));

      // Publish Kind 10003 only if not locked yet
      if (!lockedUsername && myUsername) {
        const usernameEv = finalizeEvent({
          kind: 10003,
          created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
          tags: [
            ['d', 'username'],
            ['t', 'cipher_username_' + myUsername],
            ['t', 'cipher_username'],
            ['username', myUsername]
          ],
          content: ''
        }, identity.sk);
        const allPublishRelays = [...new Set([...generalRelays, ...groupRelays, 'wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.damus.io'])];
        await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(allPublishRelays, usernameEv));
        scopedSet('my_username', myUsername);
        setLockedUsername(myUsername);
      }

      setProfiles((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        [identity.pk]: {
          pubkey: identity.pk,
          name: myName,
          displayName: myName,
          about: myAbout,
          username: lockedUsername || myUsername
        }
      }));
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Profile updated!' });
      setShowMyProfile(false);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Error: ' + e.message });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#111]/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 relative">
        <button onClick={() => setShowMyProfile(false)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-white/50 hover:text-white">✕</button>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Your Profile</h2>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm text-white/60 mb-1">Display Name</label>
            <input type="text" value={myName} onChange={(e) => setMyName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Your name" className="w-full bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm text-white/60 mb-1">Username (@handle)</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <span className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white/40">@</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={lockedUsername || myUsername}
                onChange={(e) => setMyUsername(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''))}
                placeholder="alice"
                disabled={!!lockedUsername}
                className={`w-full bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 pl-8 text-white ${lockedUsername ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}`}
              />
              {lockedUsername && (
                <span className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white/40 text-xs flex items-center gap-1">🔒 Locked</span>
              )}
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-1">
              {lockedUsername ? 'Usernames cannot be changed after they are set.' : 'Pick carefully! You can only set this once.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm text-white/60 mb-1">Bio</label>
            <input type="text" value={myAbout} onChange={(e) => setMyAbout(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tell us about yourself" className="w-full bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-lg p-3 text-white" />
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg mt-2 disabled:opacity-50">
            {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Profile'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/NotificationCenter.jsx ===
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Bell, MessageSquare, Users } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function NotificationCenter({ isOpen, onClose, onJump }) {
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const groups = useChatStore(s => s.groups);
  const unreadCounts = useChatStore(s => s.unreadCounts);

  const items = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return [];
    const list = [];

    Object.entries(dms || {}).forEach(([pk, msgs]) => {
      const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1];
      if (!last) return;
      const preview = last.data?.message
        || last.data?.title
        || (last.data?.type === 'image' ? 'Photo'
          : last.data?.type === 'audio' ? 'Voice note'
          : last.data?.type === 'cipher_order' ? 'Order'
          : last.data?.type === 'order_request' ? 'Order request'
          : 'New message');
      list.push({
        type: 'dm',
        id: pk,
        name: profiles[pk]?.displayName || profiles[pk]?.name || `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}`,
        preview,
        timestamp: last.timestamp || 0,
        unread: unreadCounts[pk] || 0,
      });
    });

    Object.entries(groupMessages || {}).forEach(([gid, msgs]) => {
      const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1];
      if (!last) return;
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(last.rawContent || '{}');
        if (parsed.type === 'typing') return;
        const group = (groups || []).find(g => g.id === gid);
        list.push({
          type: 'group',
          id: gid,
          name: group?.name || 'Group',
          preview: parsed.message || parsed.title || 'New message',
          timestamp: last.timestamp || 0,
          unread: unreadCounts[gid] || 0,
        });
      } catch (e) {}
    });

    return list.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp).slice(0, 30);
  }, [isOpen, dms, groupMessages, profiles, groups, unreadCounts]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }}
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }}
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Bell size={18} className="text-blue-400" /> Activity
            </h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50">
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {items.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <Bell size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">No recent activity</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              items.map((item, i) => (
                <button
                  key={`${item.type}-${item.id}-${i}`}
                  onClick={() => { onJump(item.type, item.id); onClose(); }}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] hover:bg-[#1a1a1a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-3 text-left transition-colors"
                >
                  <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${item.type === 'dm' ? 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400' : 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400'}`}>
                    {item.type === 'dm' ? <MessageSquare size={16} /> : <Users size={16} />}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{item.name}</p>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-white/30 shrink-0 ml-2">
                        {item.timestamp ? new Date(item.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleDateString() : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-white/60 truncate">{item.preview}</p>
                  </div>
                  {item.unread > 0 && (
                    <span className="bg-blue-500 text-white text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full shrink-0">
                      {item.unread > 99 ? '99+' : item.unread}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/OrderManagerModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Package, MapPin, CheckCircle2, Truck, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function OrderManagerModal({ order, onUpdateStatus, onClose }) {
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);

  if (!order) return null;
  const d = order.data;
  console.log("🟢 Modal Order Data:", d);

  const handleUpdate = async (status) => {
    setUpdating(true);
    await onUpdateStatus(order, status);
    setUpdating(false);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 relative shadow-2xl">
        <button onClick={onClose} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-black dark:text-white/50 hover:text-black dark:text-white">
          <X size={20} />
        </button>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
          <Package className="text-blue-400" size={24} />
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">Order Details</h2>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Product</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white">{d.productName}</p>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex justify-between bg-white dark:bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Amount</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-green-400 font-bold">${d.amountFiat}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Crypto</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono text-black dark:text-white/80">{d.amountCrypto} {d.currency?.toUpperCase()}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/40 uppercase font-bold mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
              <MapPin size={10} /> Shipping Address
            </p>
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800/50">
              <p className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white whitespace-pre-wrap">{d.shippingAddress || 'No address provided'}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 pt-4">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-black dark:text-white/60 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">Update Status</p>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => handleUpdate('accepted')}
            disabled={updating || d.status === 'accepted'}
            className="w-full bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/30 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-blue-600/30 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <CheckCircle2 size={18} /> Accept Order
          </button>

          <button 
            onClick={() => handleUpdate('shipped')}
            disabled={updating || d.status === 'shipped'}
            className="w-full bg-yellow-600/20 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/30 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-yellow-600/30 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <Truck size={18} /> Mark as Shipped
          </button>

          <button 
            onClick={() => handleUpdate('cancelled')}
            disabled={updating || d.status === 'cancelled'}
            className="w-full bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 border border-red-500/30 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-red-600/30 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <XCircle size={18} /> Cancel Order
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/PinnedMessagesManager.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Pin, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function PinnedMessagesManager({ isOpen, onClose, chatId, isGroup, onUnpin, onJump }) {
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const pinnedMessages = useChatStore(s => s.pinnedMessages);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);

  if (!isOpen || !chatId) return null;

  const msgs = isGroup ? (groupMessages[chatId] || []) : (dms[chatId] || []);
  const pinnedIds = pinnedMessages[chatId];
  const pinnedList = pinnedIds ? (Array.isArray(pinnedIds) ? pinnedIds : [pinnedIds]) : [];
  const pinnedMsgs = pinnedList.map(id => msgs.find(m => m.id === id)).filter(Boolean);

  const getPreview = (msg) => {
    if (isGroup) {
      try {
        const p = JSON.parse(msg.rawContent || '{}');
        return p.message || p.title || p.url || '(media)';
      } catch { return '(media)'; }
    }
    return msg.data?.message || msg.data?.title || msg.data?.url || '(media)';
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[75vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Pin size={18} className="text-yellow-400" /> Pinned Messages ({pinnedMsgs.length})
            </h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {pinnedMsgs.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <Pin size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">No pinned messages</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              pinnedMsgs.map(m => (
                <div key={m.id} className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => { onJump(m.id); onClose(); }} className="flex-1 text-left">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-400">{profiles[m.pubkey]?.displayName || m.pubkey.slice(0, 6)}</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-white/30">{new Date(m.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-white/80 line-clamp-2">{getPreview(m)}</p>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => onUnpin(m.id)} className="p-1.5 text-red-400/60 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors">
                    <Trash2 size={14} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/PostListingModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, UploadCloud, Loader } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function PostListingModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSubmit, identity }) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [price, setPrice] = useState('');
  const [image, setImage] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [dropMinutes, setDropMinutes] = useState('');
  const [stockCount, setStockCount] = useState('');
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    
    const readAsBase64 = () => {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (ev) => {
        setImage(ev.target.result);
        setUploading(false);
        setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Image ready!' });
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    if (!identity?.sk) return readAsBase64();
    
    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(file, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => identity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
            return finalizeEvent(event, identity.sk);
          }
        }
      });
      setImage(blob.url);
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Image uploaded!' });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Blossom upload failed, falling back to Base64", err);
      readAsBase64();
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title || !price) return;
    setLoading(true);
    await onSubmit({ 
      title, 
      priceUsd: parseFloat(price), 
      image, 
      description,
      dropUntil: dropMinutes ? (Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + (parseInt(dropMinutes) * 60)) : null,
      stock: stockCount ? parseInt(stockCount) : null
    });
    setLoading(false);
    setTitle(''); setPrice(''); setImage(''); setDescription(''); setDropMinutes(''); setStockCount('');
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center sm:p-4" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full sm:max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] shadow-2xl border border-white/10 max-h-[85dvh] flex flex-col mb-[60px] sm:mb-0" 
        >
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 pb-3 border-b border-white/5 shrink-0">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Post a Listing</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50 hover:text-white"><X size={18}/></button>
          </div>
          
          <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1">
            <form id="listing-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              
              <div className="flex gap-4 items-start">
                <div onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} className="w-24 h-24 shrink-0 bg-[#0a0a0a] border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer hover:border-blue-500 transition-colors overflow-hidden">
                  {image ? (
                    <img src={image} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="preview" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-white/40">
                      {uploading ? <Loader size={20} className="animate-spin mb-1" /> : <UploadCloud size={20} className="mb-1" />}
                      <span className="text-[10px]">{uploading ? '...' : 'Upload'}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Product Title</label>
                  <input type="text" required value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. 1lb Blue Dream" className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <input type="file" accept="image/*" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleUpload} className="hidden" />

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Price (USD)</label>
                <input type="number" required step="0.01" value={price} onChange={e => setPrice(e.target.value)} placeholder="0.00" className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Description (Optional)</label>
                <textarea value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} rows={2} placeholder="Details, shipping info..." className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none resize-none" />
              </div>
              <div className="border-t border-white/5 pt-4">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-green-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">⚡ Live Drop (Optional)</p>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-white/40 uppercase mb-1 block">Countdown</label>
                    <input type="number" value={dropMinutes} onChange={e => setDropMinutes(e.target.value)} placeholder="Minutes" className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-green-500 outline-none" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-white/40 uppercase mb-1 block">Stock</label>
                    <input type="number" value={stockCount} onChange={e => setStockCount(e.target.value)} placeholder="Qty" className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-green-500 outline-none" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/30 mt-1">Leave blank for instant listing</p>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
          
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#171717] border-t border-white/5 shrink-0 rounded-b-[2rem]">
            <button type="submit" form="listing-form" disabled={loading || uploading || !title || !price} className="w-full bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 disabled:bg-green-600/50 disabled:text-white/50 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors active:scale-[0.98]">
              {loading ? 'Posting...' : 'Send to Chat'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/ProductEditModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, UploadCloud, Loader } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function ProductEditModal({ editingProduct, onClose, onSubmit, identity }) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [price, setPrice] = useState('');
  const [image, setImage] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (editingProduct?.product) {
      setTitle(editingProduct.product.title || '');
      setPrice(editingProduct.product.priceUsd?.toString() || '');
      setImage(editingProduct.product.image || '');
      setDescription(editingProduct.product.description || '');
    }
  }, [editingProduct]);

  if (!editingProduct) return null;

  const handleUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(file, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => identity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
            return finalizeEvent(event, identity.sk);
          }
        }
      });
      setImage(blob.url);
    } catch (err) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Upload failed' });
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title || !price) return;
    setSaving(true);
    await onSubmit({ title, priceUsd: parseFloat(price), image, description });
    setSaving(false);
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[90dvh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Edit Product</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Image</label>
              <div onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} className="w-full h-32 bg-[#0a0a0a] border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer hover:border-blue-500 transition-colors overflow-hidden">
                {image ? (
                  <img src={image} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="preview" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-white/40">
                    {uploading ? <Loader className="animate-spin mb-2" /> : <UploadCloud size={24} className="mb-2" />}
                    <span className="text-xs">{uploading ? 'Uploading...' : 'Tap to change photo'}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

              <input type="file" accept="image/*" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleUpload} className="hidden" />

              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Price (USD)</label>
              <input type="number" required step="0.01" value={price} onChange={e => setPrice(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Description</label>
              <textarea value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} rows={2} className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none resize-none" />

=== FILE: src/components/chat/ProfileEditor.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Camera, Loader, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { updateProfileCache } from '../../useProfiles';
import ImageCropper from './ImageCropper';

export default function ProfileEditor({ isOpen, onClose, identity, currentProfile }) {
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [about, setAbout] = useState('');
  const [picture, setPicture] = useState('');
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [pendingImage, setPendingImage] = useState(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);
  const setProfiles = useChatStore(s => s.setProfiles);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentProfile) {
      setDisplayName(currentProfile.displayName || currentProfile.name || '');
      setAbout(currentProfile.about || '');
      setPicture(currentProfile.picture || '');
    }
  }, [currentProfile, isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen || !identity) return null;

  const handleAvatarUpload = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => setPendingImage(reader.result);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
  };

  const handleCropComplete = async (croppedFile) => {
    setPendingImage(null);
    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(croppedFile, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => identity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
            return finalizeEvent(event, identity.sk);
          }
        }
      });
      setPicture(blob.url);
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Photo updated' });
    } catch (err) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Upload failed' });
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!displayName.trim()) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Display name is required' });
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const { getAuthenticatedPool } = await import('../../lib/pool');
      const { generalRelays } = await import('../../lib/relays');
      
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const existing = currentProfile || {};
      
      // Preserve the existing username (which is permanent)
      const isNpub = existing.name && existing.name.startsWith('npub');
      const username = isNpub ? '' : (existing.name || existing.username || '');
      
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 0,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [],
        content: JSON.stringify({
          name: username,
          display_name: displayName.trim(),
          about: about.trim(),
          picture: picture || null
        })
      }, identity.sk);

      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));

      // Update local cache
      const updatedProfile = {
        pubkey: identity.pk,
        name: username,
        displayName: displayName.trim(),
        about: about.trim(),
        picture: picture || null
      };
      
      await updateProfileCache(identity.pk, updatedProfile);
      setProfiles(prev => ({ ...prev, [identity.pk]: updatedProfile }));
      
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Profile saved!' });
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Save failed: ' + err.message });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Edit Profile</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            {/* Avatar */}
            <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
              <div 
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="relative w-24 h-24 rounded-full overflow-hidden cursor-pointer group border-2 border-white/10 hover:border-blue-500/50 transition-colors"
              >
                {picture ? (
                  <img src={picture} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="avatar" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-full h-full bg-[#0a0a0a] flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Camera size={28} className="text-white/40" />
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity">
                  <Camera size={20} className="text-white" />
                </div>
                {uploading && (
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/70 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Loader size={24} className="animate-spin text-white" />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <input type="file" accept="image/*" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleAvatarUpload} className="hidden" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Display Name</label>
                <input 
                  type="text"
                  value={displayName}
                  onChange={e => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Your name"
                  className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Bio</label>
                <textarea 
                  value={about}
                  onChange={e => setAbout(e.target.value)}
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="Tell people about yourself or your shop..."
                  maxLength={200}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 outline-none resize-none"
                />
                <p className="text-[10px] text-white/30 mt-1 text-right">{about.length}/200</p>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-white/40 uppercase mb-1">Username (permanent)</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-white/60 font-mono">@{currentProfile?.name?.startsWith('npub') ? 'Not Set' : (currentProfile?.name || 'unknown')}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button 
            onClick={handleSave}
            disabled={saving || uploading || !displayName.trim()}
            className="w-full mt-4 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-white/10 disabled:text-white/30 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <Save size={16}/> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Profile'}
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      
      {pendingImage && (
        <ImageCropper
          imageSrc={pendingImage}
          onComplete={handleCropComplete}
          onClose={() => setPendingImage(null)}
        />
      )}
    </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/QRScanner.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Camera, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { nip19 } from 'nostr-tools';

export default function QRScanner({ isOpen, onClose, onScanned }) {
  const videoRef = useRef(null);
  const streamRef = useRef(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    let mounted = true;
    const startCamera = async () => {
      try {
        const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ 
          video: { facingMode: 'environment' } 
        });
        if (!mounted) { stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); return; }
        streamRef.current = stream;
        if (videoRef.current) {
          videoRef.current.srcObject = stream;
          videoRef.current.play();
        }
        setScanning(true);
        detectQR();
      } catch (err) {
        setError('Camera access denied or not available. You can paste the QR data manually.');
      }
    };

    const detectQR = () => {
      if (!videoRef.current || !mounted) return;
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      
      const scanLoop = () => {
        if (!mounted || !videoRef.current) return;
        if (videoRef.current.readyState === videoRef.current.HAVE_ENOUGH_DATA) {
          canvas.width = videoRef.current.videoWidth;
          canvas.height = videoRef.current.videoHeight;
          ctx.drawImage(videoRef.current, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          
          // Use BarcodeDetector API if available (Chrome/Edge)
          if ('BarcodeDetector' in window) {
            const detector = new window.BarcodeDetector({ formats: ['qr_code'] });
            detector.detect(canvas).then(codes => {
              if (codes.length > 0) {
                const result = codes[0].rawValue;
                handleResult(result);
              }
            }).catch(() => {});
          }
        }
        if (mounted) requestAnimationFrame(scanLoop);
      };
      requestAnimationFrame(scanLoop);
    };

    const handleResult = (data) => {
      if (!mounted) return;
      try {
        // Expected format: cipher://user/npub1...
        if (data.startsWith('cipher://user/')) {
          const npub = data.replace('cipher://user/', '');
          const decoded = nip19.decode(npub);
          if (decoded.type === 'npub') {
            onScanned({ type: 'user', pubkey: decoded.data });
            onClose();
            return;
          }
        }
        // Also accept raw npub
        if (data.startsWith('npub1')) {
          const decoded = nip19.decode(data);
          if (decoded.type === 'npub') {
            onScanned({ type: 'user', pubkey: decoded.data });
            onClose();
            return;
          }
        }
        // Group invite format: cipher://group/<id>
        if (data.startsWith('cipher://group/')) {
          const groupId = data.replace('cipher://group/', '');
          onScanned({ type: 'group', groupId });
          onClose();
          return;
        }
        setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Invalid QR code' });
      } catch (e) {
        setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Invalid QR format' });
      }
    };

    startCamera();

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      if (streamRef.current) {
        streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
      }
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[999999] bg-black flex flex-col">
        <div className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center">
          <h2 className="text-white font-bold text-lg">Scan QR Code</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/10 rounded-full text-white"><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        {error ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center">
            <AlertCircle size={48} className="text-red-500 mb-4" />
            <p className="text-white font-bold mb-2">Camera Error</p>
            <p className="text-white/60 text-sm mb-6">{error}</p>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl">Close</button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex-1 relative">
            <video ref={videoRef} className="w-full h-full object-cover" playsInline muted />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 border-[60px] border-black/50 pointer-events-none">
              <div className="w-full h-full border-2 border-green-500 rounded-2xl relative">
                <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-6 h-6 border-t-4 border-l-4 border-green-500 rounded-tl" />
                <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-6 h-6 border-t-4 border-r-4 border-green-500 rounded-tr" />
                <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-6 h-6 border-b-4 border-l-4 border-green-500 rounded-bl" />
                <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-6 h-6 border-b-4 border-r-4 border-green-500 rounded-br" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="absolute bottom-8 left-0 right-0 text-center text-white/80 text-sm px-6">
              Point at a Cipher profile QR code
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}
