=== FILE: src/components/chat/ReactionsDetail.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ReactionsDetail({ isOpen, onClose, reactions, profiles }) {
  if (!isOpen || !reactions || reactions.length === 0) return null;

  // Group by emoji
  const grouped = {};
  reactions.forEach(r => {
    if (!grouped[r.emoji]) grouped[r.emoji] = [];
    if (r.pubkey) grouped[r.emoji].push(r.pubkey);
  });

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pb-8 max-h-[70vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Reactions</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-4">
            {Object.entries(grouped).map(([emoji, pubkeys]) => (
              <div key={emoji}>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                  <span className="text-2xl">{emoji}</span>
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white/60">{pubkeys.length}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  {pubkeys.map(pk => (
                    <div key={pk} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-lg p-2">
                      {profiles[pk]?.picture ? (
                        <img src={profiles[pk].picture} className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold">
                          {(profiles[pk]?.displayName || 'U').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <p className="text-xs text-white">{profiles[pk]?.displayName || `User ${(pk || "anon").slice(0, 6)}`}</p>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/ReviewModal.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Star } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ReviewModal({ targetName, onSubmit, onClose }) {
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(0);
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(0);
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (rating < 1) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    const ok = await onSubmit(rating, text.trim());
    setSubmitting(false);
    if (ok) onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 relative shadow-2xl">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-black dark:text-white/50 hover:text-black dark:text-white"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white mb-1">Rate {targetName}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-black dark:text-white/50 mb-4">
          Your review is published to the Nostr network. Vendors can't delete it.
        </p>

        {/* Star picker */}
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mb-6">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
            <button
              key={n}
              onClick={() => setRating(n)}
              onMouseEnter={() => setHover(n)}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHover(0)}
              className="transition-transform hover:scale-110 active:scale-95"
            >
              <Star
                size={36}
                className={
                  n <= (hover || rating)
                    ? 'fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400'
                    : 'text-black dark:text-white/20'
                }
              />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <textarea
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="How was your experience? (optional)"
          maxLength={500}
          rows={4}
          className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-black border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-black dark:text-white placeholder-white/30 focus:border-white/30 outline-none resize-none mb-2"
        />
        <p className="text-[10px] text-black dark:text-white/30 text-right mb-4">
          {text.length}/500
        </p>

        <button
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={rating < 1 || submitting}
          className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-black dark:text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl transition-colors"
        >
          {submitting ? 'Publishing...' : rating < 1 ? 'Select a rating' : 'Publish Review'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/SearchView.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Search, Megaphone, Users, X, UserPlus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';

export default function SearchView({ ToastUI, handleSearch, joinGroupById, openChat }) {
  const searchQuery = useChatStore((s) => s.searchQuery);
  const setSearchQuery = useChatStore((s) => s.setSearchQuery);
  const searchResults = useChatStore((s) => s.searchResults);
  const isSearching = useChatStore((s) => s.isSearching);
  const setView = useChatStore((s) => s.setView);

  const searchTimer = useRef(null);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    clearTimeout(searchTimer.current);
    
    // Don't search for empty strings or just "@" (prevents random user spam)
    if (searchQuery.trim().length < 2) {
      useChatStore.getState().setSearchResults([]);
      return;
    }
    
    searchTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      handleSearch();
    }, 800); // Wait 800ms after you stop typing
    
    return () => clearTimeout(searchTimer.current);
  }, [searchQuery]);

  const handleResultClick = (res) => {
    if (res.type === 'user') {
      openChat('dm', res.pubkey);
    } else if (res.type === 'group') {
      const localGroups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_my_groups') || '[]');
      if (!localGroups.find(g => g.id === res.id)) {
        localGroups.push({ id: res.id, name: res.name, isChannel: res.isChannel, creator: res.creator });
        localStorage.setItem('cipher_my_groups', JSON.stringify(localGroups));
      }
      openChat('group', res.id, res);
    }
  };

  const isGroupId = searchQuery.length >= 12 && searchQuery.length <= 20 && /^[a-z0-9_-]+$/i.test(searchQuery) && !searchQuery.includes(' ');

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-[#0a0a0a]">
      <ToastUI />
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-3 sticky top-0 z-10 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
        <button onClick={() => setView('list')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white transition-colors">
          <ArrowLeft size={22} />
        </button>
        <div className="flex-1 flex items-center bg-[#171717] rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 border border-white/5 focus-within:border-blue-500 transition-colors">
          <Search size={18} className="text-white/40 mr-2" />
          <input
            autoFocus
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSearch(); }}
            placeholder="Search @users, groups, or paste ID..."
            className="bg-transparent text-[15px] text-white outline-none w-full placeholder-white/30"
          />
          {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
            <button onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')} className="p-1 text-white/40 hover:text-white">
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <button onClick={handleSearch} className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-500 hover:text-blue-400">
          Search
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Results Area */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 p-4 space-y-3">
        
        {/* Smart Group ID Join Card */}
        {isGroupId && !isSearching && (
          <div 
            onClick={() => joinGroupById(searchQuery)}
            className="bg-blue-600/10 border border-blue-500/30 p-4 rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer hover:bg-blue-600/20 transition-colors mb-4"
          >
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
              <UserPlus size={24} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Join Group</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-400/80 font-mono truncate">{searchQuery}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {isSearching && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center mt-20 text-white/40">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mb-4"></div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium">Searching relays...</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {!isSearching && searchResults.length === 0 && !isGroupId && (
          <div className="text-center mt-20 px-6 flex flex-col items-center">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-[#171717] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-6">
              <Search size={32} className="text-white/20" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-2">
              {searchQuery.length > 0 ? 'No results found' : 'Find your people'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-white/40 mb-6 text-center leading-relaxed">
              {searchQuery.length > 0 
                ? `We couldn't find anything for "${searchQuery}". Try waiting a few seconds and searching again.`
                : <>Search for <span className="text-blue-400">@usernames</span>, paste an <span className="text-blue-400">npub</span>, or enter a Group ID to join a community.</>}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {searchResults.map((res, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            onClick={() => handleResultClick(res)}
            className="bg-[#111] hover:bg-[#1a1a1a] p-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/5 flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer transition-colors active:scale-[0.98]"
          >
            {res.type === 'user' ? (
              res.picture ? (
                <img src={res.picture} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg">
                  {res.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || 'U'}
                </div>
              )
            ) : (
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                {res.isChannel ? <Megaphone size={22} className="text-purple-400" /> : <Users size={22} className="text-blue-400" />}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-white truncate">{res.name}</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-medium">
                {res.type === 'user' ? 'User' : (res.isChannel ? 'Channel' : 'Public Group')}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/VendorAnalytics.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ChevronLeft, TrendingUp, DollarSign, Package, Shield, ShieldAlert, Star, Trophy, Clock, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { useVouchCount } from '../../hooks/useVouchCount';
import { useScamReports } from '../../hooks/useScamReports';
import { db } from '../../db';

export default function VendorAnalytics({ identity, setView }) {
  const dms = useChatStore(s => s.dms);
  const profiles = useChatStore(s => s.profiles);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({
    totalSales: 0,
    totalRevenue: 0,
    btcVolume: 0,
    xmrVolume: 0,
    recentOrders: [],
    topProducts: {}
  });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [timeFilter, setTimeFilter] = useState('all');
  
  const { vouchCount, tier } = useVouchCount({ identity, targetPubkey: identity?.pk });
  const { reportCount } = useScamReports({ identity, targetPubkey: identity?.pk });

  useEffect(() => {
    const computeStats = async () => {
      try {
        const allMsgs = await db.messages.toArray();
        
        // Filter completed orders where I was the vendor (peer is not me in the message)
        const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
        const filterCutoff = timeFilter === 'today' ? now - 86400
                          : timeFilter === 'week' ? now - 604800
                          : timeFilter === 'month' ? now - 2592000
                          : 0;

        const completedOrders = allMsgs.filter(m => 
          m.data?.type === 'cipher_order' && 
          m.data?.status === 'completed' &&
          m.timestamp >= filterCutoff
        );

        // Total sales
        const totalSales = completedOrders.length;
        
        // Total revenue
        let totalRevenue = 0, btcVolume = 0, xmrVolume = 0;
        completedOrders.forEach(o => {
          totalRevenue += parseFloat(o.data.amountFiat || 0);
          const cryptoAmt = parseFloat(o.data.amountCrypto || 0);
          if (o.data.currency === 'btc') btcVolume += cryptoAmt;
          if (o.data.currency === 'xmr') xmrVolume += cryptoAmt;
        });

        // Recent orders (last 10)
        const recentOrders = completedOrders
          .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
          .slice(0, 10);

        // Top products
        const topProducts = {};
        completedOrders.forEach(o => {
          const name = o.data.productName || 'Unknown';
          topProducts[name] = (topProducts[name] || 0) + 1;
        });

        setStats({ totalSales, totalRevenue, btcVolume, xmrVolume, recentOrders, topProducts });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Failed to compute vendor stats:', e);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    computeStats();
  }, [dms, timeFilter]);

  const tierData = {
    elite: { label: 'Elite Vendor', icon: <Trophy size={20} />, color: 'text-yellow-400', bg: 'bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30' },
    trusted: { label: 'Trusted Vendor', icon: <Shield size={20} />, color: 'text-green-400', bg: 'bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30' },
    new: { label: 'New Vendor', icon: <Star size={20} />, color: 'text-blue-400', bg: 'bg-blue-500/10 border-blue-500/30' }
  }[tier];

  const topProductsList = Object.entries(stats.topProducts)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a] pb-24">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/5 shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
          <button onClick={() => setView('main')} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-white/60 hover:text-white">
            <ChevronLeft size={24} />
          </button>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Vendor Dashboard</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-1 bg-[#0a0a0a] p-1 rounded-xl">
          {[
            { key: 'today', label: 'Today' },
            { key: 'week', label: '7 Days' },
            { key: 'month', label: '30 Days' },
            { key: 'all', label: 'All Time' }
          ].map(f => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setTimeFilter(f.key)}
              className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors ${timeFilter === f.key ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/40 hover:text-white'}`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
        {/* Tier Banner */}
        <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border ${tierData.bg} flex items-center gap-3`}>
          <div className={`${tierData.color}`}>{tierData.icon}</div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${tierData.color}`}>{tierData.label}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-white/50">{vouchCount} vouch{vouchCount !== 1 ? 'es' : ''} • {reportCount} report{reportCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Stat Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-green-400 mb-2"><DollarSign size={14} /><p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Revenue</p></div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">${stats.totalRevenue.toFixed(0)}</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-1">All-time USD</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-400 mb-2"><Package size={14} /><p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Sales</p></div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">{stats.totalSales}</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-1">Completed orders</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Crypto Volume */}
        <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-white/40 mb-3">Crypto Volume</p>
          <div className="flex justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-white/50 mb-1">Bitcoin</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-mono text-orange-400">{stats.btcVolume.toFixed(6)} BTC</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="text-xs text-white/50 mb-1">Monero</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-mono text-orange-400">{stats.xmrVolume.toFixed(4)} XMR</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Trust Breakdown */}
        <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-white/40 mb-3">Reputation</p>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-green-400"><Shield size={14} /><span className="text-xs">Vouches</span></div>
            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{vouchCount}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-red-400"><ShieldAlert size={14} /><span className="text-xs">Scam Reports</span></div>
            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{reportCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Top Products */}
        {topProductsList.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-white/40 mb-3">
              <TrendingUp size={14} />
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Top Products</p>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {topProductsList.map(([name, count], i) => (
                <div key={name} className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white/30 w-4">#{i + 1}</span>
                    <p className="text-xs text-white/80 truncate">{name}</p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white/60 shrink-0">{count} sold</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Recent Orders */}
        {stats.recentOrders.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-white/40 mb-3">
              <Clock size={14} />
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Recent Orders</p>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {stats.recentOrders.map(o => (
                <div key={o.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-lg">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-green-400 shrink-0" />
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-white truncate">{o.data.productName || 'Product'}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40">{new Date(o.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-green-400 font-mono shrink-0">${o.data.amountFiat}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Empty state */}
        {stats.totalSales === 0 && !loading && (
          <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
            <TrendingUp size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-30" />
            <p className="text-sm mb-1">No sales yet.</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-white/30">Post product cards in groups to get started.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/VendorCatalogSheet.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Package, ShoppingBag, Star } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, groupRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../../lib/relays';
import { useVouchCount } from '../../hooks/useVouchCount';

export default function VendorCatalogSheet({ isOpen, onClose, vendorPubkey, profiles, identity }) {
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const openChat = useChatStore(s => s.openChat);
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  const { vouchCount, tier } = useVouchCount({ identity, targetPubkey: vendorPubkey });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !vendorPubkey) return;
    let mounted = true;
    setLoading(true);

    const fetchCatalog = async () => {
      // First, scan local group messages for this vendor's products
      const found = [];
      const seen = new Set();
      Object.values(groupMessages).forEach(msgs => {
        (msgs || []).forEach(msg => {
          if (msg.pubkey !== vendorPubkey) return;
          try {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(msg.rawContent || '{}');
            if (parsed.type === 'product' && !seen.has(msg.id)) {
              seen.add(msg.id);
              found.push({ id: msg.id, ...parsed });
            }
          } catch(e) {}
        });
      });

      // Then, try to fetch from relays as fallback (products vendor posted in channels)
      try {
        if (identity?.sk && found.length < 10) {
          const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
          const allRelays = [...new Set([...generalRelays, ...groupRelays])];
          const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, allRelays, {
            kinds: [30402],
            authors: [vendorPubkey],
            '#t': ['cipher_marketplace'],
            limit: 100
          }, 4000);

          events.forEach(ev => {
            if (seen.has(ev.id)) return;
            try {
              const getTag = (name) => ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === name)?.[1];
              const content = JSON.parse(ev.content || '{}');
              const price = parseFloat(getTag('price') || content.price || '0');
              if (price > 0) {
                seen.add(ev.id);
                found.push({
                  id: getTag('d') || ev.id,
                  title: getTag('title') || content.name || 'Product',
                  priceUsd: price,
                  image: getTag('image') || content.image || '',
                  description: content.description || ''
                });
              }
            } catch(e) {}
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      if (mounted) {
        setProducts(found);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchCatalog();
    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, [isOpen, vendorPubkey, groupMessages, identity?.sk]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;
  const vendorName = profiles[vendorPubkey]?.displayName || 'Vendor';
  const vendorPic = profiles[vendorPubkey]?.picture;
  
  const tierData = {
    elite: { label: 'Elite Vendor', icon: '🏆', color: 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30' },
    trusted: { label: 'Trusted Vendor', icon: '🛡️', color: 'text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30' },
    new: { label: 'New Vendor', icon: '🟡', color: 'text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10 border-blue-500/30' }
  }[tier];

  const handleBuy = (product) => {
    onClose();
    openChat('dm', vendorPubkey);
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mt-3 mb-2" />
          
          {/* Vendor header */}
          <div className="p-5 pb-3 border-b border-white/5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
              {vendorPic ? (
                <img src={vendorPic} className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl">
                  {vendorName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white truncate">{vendorName}</h2>
                <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-[10px] font-bold mt-1 ${tierData.color}`}>
                  <span>{tierData.icon}</span> {tierData.label} • {vouchCount} vouch{vouchCount !== 1 ? 'es' : ''}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={18}/></button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-white/40 flex items-center gap-1">
              <ShoppingBag size={12}/> {products.length} active listing{products.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex justify-center py-12">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
              </div>
            ) : products.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <Package size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-50" />
                <p className="text-sm">No active listings</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/30 mt-1">This vendor hasn't posted products yet</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {products.map(p => (
                  <div key={p.id} className="bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-white/5">
                    {p.image && <img src={p.image} className="w-full h-28 object-cover" />}
                    <div className="p-3">
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">{p.title}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-green-400 font-mono mt-0.5">${p.priceUsd}</p>
                      {p.dropUntil && p.dropUntil > Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) && (
                        <p className="text-[9px] text-red-400 font-bold mt-1">⚡ LIVE DROP</p>
                      )}
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleBuy(p)}
                        className="w-full mt-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                      >
                        Buy
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/VendorDashboard.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, Package, DollarSign, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { useVendorStats } from '../../hooks/useVendorStats';
import OrderManagerModal from './OrderManagerModal';
import { useNIP44DM } from '../../hooks/useNIP44DM';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { db } from '../../db';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../../lib/relays';

export default function VendorDashboard({ identity, products, onBack }) {
  const { dms } = useChatStore();
  const { stats, loading, fetchStats } = useVendorStats({ identity });
  const { sendDM } = useNIP44DM({ identity });
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [selectedOrder, setSelectedOrder] = useState(null);
  const [fetchingOrders, setFetchingOrders] = useState(false);

  // Fetch orders from BOTH relay + local Dexie (union, deduplicated by order id)
  const loadOrders = async () => {
    setFetchingOrders(true);
    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');

      // 1. Get local overrides from Dexie (source of truth for local status changes)
      const localMsgs = await db.messages.toArray();
      const localMap = new Map();
      localMsgs.forEach(m => {
        if (m.data?.type === 'cipher_order') localMap.set(m.id, m);
      });

      // 2. Fetch DMs from Relay
      const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk], limit: 100 }, 5000);
      
      const combinedMap = new Map();

      // Add relay events, but merge local status if it exists (so it doesn't overwrite your 'shipped' click)
      for (const ev of events) {
        try {
          const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, ev.pubkey);
          const data = JSON.parse(nip44.v2.decrypt(ev.content, shared));
          
          if (data.type === 'cipher_order') {
            const localVersion = localMap.get(ev.id);
            const finalStatus = localVersion?.data?.status || data.status;
            
            combinedMap.set(ev.id, { 
              id: ev.id, chatId: ev.pubkey, pubkey: ev.pubkey, timestamp: ev.created_at, 
              data: { ...data, status: finalStatus } 
            });
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      // Add any local orders that weren't in the relay fetch
      localMsgs.forEach(m => {
        if (m.data?.type === 'cipher_order' && !combinedMap.has(m.id)) {
          combinedMap.set(m.id, m);
        }
      });

      const combined = Array.from(combinedMap.values());
      
      // Sort: Pending first, Completed last
      combined.sort((a, b) => {
        const aComp = a.data.status === 'completed' ? 1 : 0;
        const bComp = b.data.status === 'completed' ? 1 : 0;
        return aComp - bComp;
      });

      setOrders(combined);
    } finally {
      setFetchingOrders(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchStats();
    loadOrders();
  }, [dms, fetchStats]);

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (order, newStatus) => {
    try {
      const updatedMsg = { ...order, data: { ...order.data, status: newStatus } };
      if (updatedMsg.chatId) {
        await db.messages.put(updatedMsg);
      }

      const payload = {
        type: 'order_update',
        orderId: order.id,
        productName: order.data.productName,
        status: newStatus
      };
      await sendDM(order.chatId, payload, null, null);

      setOrders(prev => prev.map(o => o.id === order.id ? updatedMsg : o));
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Order status updated to: ' + newStatus.toUpperCase() });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to update status', e);
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: 'Failed to update status: ' + e.message });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-white dark:bg-black relative">
      {/* Fixed Header Area */}
      <div className="flex-shrink-0 p-4 pb-0">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={onBack} className="p-2 text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white">
              <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><path d="M19 12H5M12 19l-7-7 7-7"/></svg>
            </button>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">Vendor Dashboard</h2>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => { fetchStats(); loadOrders(); }}
            className="p-2 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#171717] rounded-full text-black dark:text-white/60 hover:text-black dark:text-white"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={18} className={(loading || fetchingOrders) ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-6">
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1"><BarChart3 size={14}/> Total Sales</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-black dark:text-white">{stats.sales}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1"><DollarSign size={14}/> Fiat Volume</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-400">${stats.revenueFiat.toFixed(2)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-black dark:text-white/80 mb-3">Orders ({orders.length})</h3>
      </div>

      {/* Scrollable Orders Area */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 px-4 pb-24">
        {fetchingOrders ? (
          <p className="text-black dark:text-white/50 text-center py-10">Fetching orders from relay...</p>
        ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-black dark:text-white/50 text-center py-10">No orders yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {orders.map(o => (
              <div 
                key={o.id} 
                onClick={() => setSelectedOrder(o)}
                className="bg-[#111] border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-4 cursor-pointer hover:border-white/30 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-bold text-black dark:text-white text-sm">{o.data.productName}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-black dark:text-white/50">SHIP TO: {o.data.shippingAddress}</p>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-green-400 font-bold">${o.data.amountFiat}</p>
                </div>
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full border ${
                  o.data.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-400 border-green-500/20' : 'bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400 border-yellow-500/20'
                }`}>
                  {o.data.status?.toUpperCase() || 'PENDING'}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {selectedOrder && (
        <OrderManagerModal
          order={selectedOrder}
          onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
          onClose={() => setSelectedOrder(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/chat/VendorDirectory.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, Store, Shield, ShieldAlert, Star, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../../lib/relays';

export default function VendorDirectory({ isOpen, onClose, groupId, profiles, identity, onOpenVendor }) {
  const [vendors, setVendors] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !groupId) return;
    let mounted = true;
    setLoading(true);

    const fetchVendors = async () => {
      const msgs = groupMessages[groupId] || [];
      const vendorSet = new Map();
      
      // Find all vendors who posted a product in this group
      msgs.forEach(m => {
        try {
          const p = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}');
          if (p.type === 'product' && !vendorSet.has(m.pubkey)) {
            vendorSet.set(m.pubkey, {
              pubkey: m.pubkey,
              productCount: 0,
              lastPosted: 0,
              vouchCount: 0,
              reportCount: 0
            });
          }
          if (p.type === 'product') {
            const v = vendorSet.get(m.pubkey);
            if (v) {
              v.productCount++;
              v.lastPosted = Math.max(v.lastPosted, m.timestamp);
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      });

      // Fetch vouch & report counts for each vendor in batch
      if (identity?.sk && vendorSet.size > 0) {
        try {
          const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
          const vendorPks = Array.from(vendorSet.keys());
          
          const [vouchEvents, reportEvents] = await Promise.all([
            queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10004], '#p': vendorPks, limit: 500 }, 4000),
            queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [1984], '#p': vendorPks, limit: 200 }, 4000)
          ]);

          // Count unique vouches/reports per vendor
          const vouchMap = {};
          const reportMap = {};
          
          vouchEvents.forEach(ev => {
            const p = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1];
            if (p && ev.pubkey !== p) {
              if (!vouchMap[p]) vouchMap[p] = new Set();
              vouchMap[p].add(ev.pubkey);
            }
          });
          
          reportEvents.forEach(ev => {
            const p = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1];
            const reportType = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'report')?.[1];
            if (p && (reportType === 'scam' || !reportType)) {
              if (!reportMap[p]) reportMap[p] = new Set();
              reportMap[p].add(ev.pubkey);
            }
          });

          vendorSet.forEach((v, pk) => {
            v.vouchCount = vouchMap[pk]?.size || 0;
            v.reportCount = reportMap[pk]?.size || 0;
          });
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      const list = Array.from(vendorSet.values())
        .sort((a, b) => {
          // Sort by scam reports (worst first, so admins see them), then vouch count, then product count
          if (b.reportCount !== a.reportCount) return b.reportCount - a.reportCount;
          if (b.vouchCount !== a.vouchCount) return b.vouchCount - a.vouchCount;
          return b.productCount - a.productCount;
        });

      if (mounted) {
        setVendors(list);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchVendors();
    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, [isOpen, groupId, groupMessages, identity?.sk]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const getTier = (v) => {
    if (v.reportCount >= 3) return { label: '⚠️ Reported', color: 'text-red-400 bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30' };
    if (v.vouchCount >= 10) return { label: '🏆 Elite', color: 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30' };
    if (v.vouchCount >= 3) return { label: '🛡️ Trusted', color: 'text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30' };
    return { label: '🟡 New', color: 'text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10 border-blue-500/30' };
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-start justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '-100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '-100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-b-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 p-5 pt-10 max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col"
        >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Store size={18} className="text-blue-400" /> Vendors in this Group
            </h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          <p className="text-[11px] text-white/40 mb-3">Sorted by trust level. Every vendor here has posted products in this group.</p>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex justify-center py-12">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
              </div>
            ) : vendors.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-12">
                <Store size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">No vendors yet</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/30 mt-1">Products posted in this group will appear here</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              vendors.map(v => {
                const tier = getTier(v);
                const profile = profiles[v.pubkey] || {};
                return (
                  <button
                    key={v.pubkey}
                    onClick={() => { onOpenVendor?.(v.pubkey); onClose(); }}
                    className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] hover:bg-[#1a1a1a] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-3 text-left transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      {profile.picture ? (
                        <img src={profile.picture} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover" alt="" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg">
                          {(profile.displayName || 'V').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">
                          {profile.displayName || profile.name || `Vendor ${v.pubkey.slice(0, 6)}`}
                        </p>
                        <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-[9px] font-bold mt-1 ${tier.color}`}>
                          {tier.label} • {v.vouchCount} vouch{v.vouchCount !== 1 ? 'es' : ''}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-white/30 shrink-0" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-3 mt-2 text-[10px] text-white/40">
                      <span>{v.productCount} listing{v.productCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
                      {v.reportCount > 0 && <span className="text-red-400">{v.reportCount} report{v.reportCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/VendorOnboardingWizard.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, ChevronRight, Wallet, CheckCircle2, Store, Send, Shield } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { scopedGet, scopedSet } from '../../lib/storage';

export default function VendorOnboardingWizard({ isOpen, onClose, onComplete }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [btc, setBtc] = useState(scopedGet('btc_address', ''));
  const [xmr, setXmr] = useState(scopedGet('xmr_address', ''));
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const validateBtc = (addr) => /^(bc1|[13])[a-zA-HJ-NP-Z0-9]{25,62}$/.test(addr);
  const validateXmr = (addr) => /^[48][0-9AB][1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{93}$/.test(addr);

  const handleNext = () => {
    setError('');
    if (step === 2) {
      if (!btc && !xmr) { setError('Please enter at least one address'); return; }
      if (btc && !validateBtc(btc)) { setError('Invalid BTC address format'); return; }
      if (xmr && !validateXmr(xmr)) { setError('Invalid XMR address format'); return; }
    }
    setStep(step + 1);
  };

  const handleFinish = () => {
    if (btc) scopedSet('btc_address', btc);
    if (xmr) scopedSet('xmr_address', xmr);
    setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'You are now a vendor!' });
    onComplete();
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] border-t sm:border border-white/10 p-6 pb-8 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4 sm:hidden" />
          
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Store size={16} />
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider">Step {step} of 3</span>
            </div>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50 hover:text-white"><X size={16}/></button>
          </div>

          {/* Progress bar */}
          <div className="w-full h-1 bg-white/5 rounded-full mb-6 overflow-hidden">
            <motion.div 
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500" 
              animate={{ width: `${(step / 3) * 100}%` }} 
              transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1">
            {/* STEP 1: Welcome */}
            {step === 1 && (
              <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} className="space-y-4">
                <div className="text-center mb-6">
                  <div className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto mb-4 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500/20 to-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Store size={36} className="text-blue-400" />
                  </div>
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Become a Vendor</h2>
                  <p className="text-sm text-white/50">Start selling in 3 simple steps</p>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex gap-3 p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <Wallet size={20} className="text-blue-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">Set payment addresses</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-white/50">BTC and/or XMR so buyers can pay you directly</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-3 p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <Send size={20} className="text-purple-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">Post your first product</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-white/50">Use the green Tag button in any group</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-3 p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <Shield size={20} className="text-green-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">Build trust</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-white/50">Successful deliveries auto-earn vouches</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {/* STEP 2: Payment Addresses */}
            {step === 2 && (
              <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-1">Payment Addresses</h2>
                  <p className="text-sm text-white/50 mb-4">Where buyers will send funds. You control these wallets, Cipher never touches your crypto.</p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Bitcoin (BTC) Address</label>
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={btc} 
                    onChange={e => { setBtc(e.target.value); setError(''); }}
                    placeholder="bc1q..." 
                    className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white font-mono focus:border-orange-500 outline-none"
                  />
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-white/30 mt-1">P2PKH, P2SH, or Bech32 addresses supported</p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block">Monero (XMR) Address</label>
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={xmr} 
                    onChange={e => { setXmr(e.target.value); setError(''); }}
                    placeholder="4..." 
                    className="w-full bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white font-mono focus:border-orange-500 outline-none"
                  />
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-white/30 mt-1">95-char Monero address (starts with 4 or 8)</p>
                </div>

                {error && <p className="text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 rounded-lg p-2">{error}</p>}
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {/* STEP 3: Done */}
            {step === 3 && (
              <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} className="text-center space-y-4">
                <div className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto rounded-full bg-green-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={40} className="text-green-400" />
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">You're a Vendor!</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-white/50">Your storefront is ready. Here's what to do next:</p>

                <div className="space-y-2 text-left">
                  <div className="p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-400 mb-1">1. Join a Promo Group</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-white/50">Find your community in the Discover tab</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-purple-400 mb-1">2. Post your first product</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-white/50">Tap the green Tag icon in the group chat input</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-green-400 mb-1">3. Broadcast to multiple groups</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-white/50">Tap the share icon on your product card</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6">
            {step < 3 ? (
              <button 
                onClick={handleNext} 
                className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                Continue <ChevronRight size={18} />
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button 
                onClick={handleFinish} 
                className="w-full bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-colors"
              >
                Start Selling
              </button>
            )}
            {step > 1 && (
              <button onClick={() => setStep(step - 1)} className="w-full text-xs text-white/40 hover:text-white mt-2 py-1">
                Back
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/chat/VendorStorefront.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X, MessageSquare, Shield, ShieldAlert, Star, Trophy, Package, Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../../store/chatStore';
import { useVouchCount } from '../../hooks/useVouchCount';
import { useScamReports } from '../../hooks/useScamReports';

export default function VendorStorefront({ isOpen, onClose, vendorPubkey, profiles, identity, onOpenChat, onBuy }) {
  const groupMessages = useChatStore(s => s.groupMessages);
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [reviews, setReviews] = useState([]);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('products');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  
  const { vouchCount, tier } = useVouchCount({ identity, targetPubkey: vendorPubkey });
  const { reportCount } = useScamReports({ identity, targetPubkey: vendorPubkey });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !vendorPubkey) return;
    
    // Collect all products this vendor posted across all groups
    const found = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    Object.values(groupMessages).forEach(msgs => {
      (msgs || []).forEach(m => {
        if (m.pubkey !== vendorPubkey) return;
        try {
          const p = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}');
          if (p.type === 'product' && !seen.has(m.id)) {
            seen.add(m.id);
            found.push({ id: m.id, ...p });
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      });
    });
    setProducts(found.sort((a, b) => (b.dropUntil || 0) - (a.dropUntil || 0)));

    // Fetch reviews (kind 10005)
    (async () => {
      try {
        const { getAuthenticatedPool } = await import('../../lib/pool');
        const { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } = await import('../../lib/relays');
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const evs = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, {
          kinds: [10005],
          '#p': [vendorPubkey],
          limit: 50
        }, 4000);
        const revs = evs.map(ev => ({
          id: ev.id,
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          rating: parseInt(ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'rating')?.[1] || '0'),
          text: ev.content,
          timestamp: ev.created_at
        })).sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
        setReviews(revs);
      } catch (e) {}
    })();
  }, [isOpen, vendorPubkey, groupMessages, identity]);

  if (!isOpen || !vendorPubkey) return null;

  const profile = profiles[vendorPubkey] || {};
  const displayName = profile.displayName || profile.name || `User ${vendorPubkey.slice(0, 8)}`;
  const bio = profile.about || '';
  const avatar = profile.picture;
  
  const tierData = {
    elite: { label: 'Elite Vendor', icon: <Trophy size={14} />, color: 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30' },
    trusted: { label: 'Trusted Vendor', icon: <Shield size={14} />, color: 'text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30' },
    new: { label: 'New Vendor', icon: <Star size={14} />, color: 'text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10 border-blue-500/30' }
  }[tier];

  const avgRating = reviews.length > 0 
    ? (reviews.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.rating, 0) / reviews.length).toFixed(1)
    : '0';

  const handleCopyPubkey = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(vendorPubkey);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (e) {}
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end justify-center" onClick={onClose}>
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} 
          transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }} 
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} 
          className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-t-[2rem] border-t border-white/10 max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mt-3 mb-2" />
          
          {/* Header with close */}
          <div className="flex justify-end p-4 pb-0">
            <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-white/5 rounded-full text-white/50 hover:text-white">
              <X size={16}/>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Profile header */}
          <div className="px-6 pb-4 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
            {avatar ? (
              <img src={avatar} className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full object-cover border-2 border-white/10 mb-3" alt="" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500/30 to-purple-500/30 text-blue-300 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-3xl mb-3 border-2 border-white/10">
                {displayName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
              </div>
            )}
            
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-1">{displayName}</h2>
            
            {bio && <p className="text-sm text-white/60 mb-3 max-w-xs">{bio}</p>}
            
            {/* Tier badge */}
            <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border text-xs font-bold ${tierData.color}`}>
              {tierData.icon}
              {tierData.label} • {vouchCount} vouch{vouchCount !== 1 ? 'es' : ''}
            </div>
            
            {/* Scam warning */}
            {reportCount > 0 && (
              <div className={`mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border text-xs font-bold ${reportCount >= 3 ? 'text-red-400 bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30' : 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30'}`}>
                <ShieldAlert size={12} />
                {reportCount} scam report{reportCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
              </div>
            )}
            
            {/* Action buttons */}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-4 w-full max-w-xs">
              <button 
                onClick={() => { onClose(); onOpenChat?.(vendorPubkey); }}
                className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm"
              >
                <MessageSquare size={16} /> Message
              </button>
              <button 
                onClick={handleCopyPubkey}
                className="p-2.5 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white/60 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                title="Copy pubkey"
              >
                <Copy size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
            
            {copied && <p className="text-[10px] text-green-400 mt-1">Pubkey copied</p>}
          </div>

          {/* Stats row */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 px-6 mb-4">
            <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white">{products.length}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase">Products</p>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white">{avgRating}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase">Rating</p>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white">{reviews.length}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase">Reviews</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tabs */}
          <div className="flex gap-1 bg-[#0a0a0a] mx-6 p-1 rounded-xl mb-3">
            <button onClick={() => setTab('products')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors ${tab === 'products' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/40'}`}>
              Products ({products.length})
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setTab('reviews')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors ${tab === 'reviews' ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'text-white/40'}`}>
              Reviews ({reviews.length})
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 pb-8">
            {tab === 'products' ? (
              products.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-8">
                  <Package size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                  <p className="text-sm">No active products</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  {products.map(p => (
                    <button 
                      key={p.id} 
                      onClick={() => { onClose(); onBuy?.(p, vendorPubkey); }}
                      className="bg-[#0a0a0a] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-white/5 hover:border-blue-500/40 transition-colors text-left"
                    >
                      {p.image && <img src={p.image} className="w-full h-28 object-cover" alt="" />}
                      <div className="p-3">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">{p.title}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-green-400 font-mono mt-0.5">${p.priceUsd}</p>
                        {p.dropUntil && p.dropUntil > Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) && (
                          <p className="text-[9px] text-red-400 font-bold mt-1">⚡ LIVE DROP</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )
            ) : (
              reviews.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center text-white/40 py-8">
                  <Star size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                  <p className="text-sm">No reviews yet</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {reviews.map(r => (
                    <div key={r.id} className="bg-[#0a0a0a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
                          {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
                            <Star key={i} size={10} className={i < r.rating ? 'text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400' : 'text-white/20'} />
                          ))}
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-white/40">{profiles[r.pubkey]?.displayName || `User ${r.pubkey.slice(0, 6)}`}</span>
                        <span className="text-[9px] text-white/30 ml-auto">{new Date(r.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                      </div>
                      {r.text && <p className="text-xs text-white/80">{r.text}</p>}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )
            )}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/components/CopyOnLongPress.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';

export const CopyOnLongPress = ({ value, displayValue, className = '', icon = false }) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [pressed, setPressed] = useState(false);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
      setCopied(true);
      setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Copied to clipboard' });
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (e) {
      setToast({ type: 'error', message: 'Copy failed' });
    }
  };

  const handleTouchStart = () => {
    setPressed(true);
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = () => {
    if (pressed) {
      handleCopy();
      setPressed(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <span 
      onClick={handleCopy}
      onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
      onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
      className={`cursor-pointer hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors ${className}`}
      title="Tap to copy"
    >
      {copied ? (
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Check size={12} className="text-green-400" /> Copied</span>
      ) : (
        displayValue || value
      )}
    </span>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/GlobalDialog.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function GlobalDialog() {
  const dialog = useChatStore(s => s.dialog);
  const closeDialog = useChatStore(s => s.closeDialog);
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (dialog) setInputValue(dialog.inputDefault || '');
  }, [dialog]);

  if (!dialog) return null;

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    if (dialog.onConfirm) dialog.onConfirm(inputValue);
    closeDialog();
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div 
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[999999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
        onClick={closeDialog}
      >
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ scale: 0.95, y: 20 }} animate={{ scale: 1, y: 0 }} exit={{ scale: 0.95, y: 20 }}
          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="bg-[#1c1c1e] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col"
        >
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">{dialog.title || 'Alert'}</h2>
            <button onClick={closeDialog} className="p-1 text-white/50 hover:text-white"><X size={18}/></button>
          </div>
          
          {dialog.message && (
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-6">{dialog.message}</p>
          )}

          {dialog.hasInput && (
            <input
              type="text"
              autoFocus
              value={inputValue}
              onChange={e => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleConfirm(); }}
              className="w-full bg-black border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder-white/30 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 mb-6"
              placeholder={dialog.placeholder || 'Type here...'}
            />
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-3 mt-auto">
            <button onClick={closeDialog} className="flex-1 py-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-white font-semibold rounded-xl transition-colors">
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={handleConfirm} 
              className={`flex-1 py-3 font-semibold rounded-xl transition-colors ${dialog.type === 'danger' ? 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white' : 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white'}`}
            >
              {dialog.confirmText || 'Confirm'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

          </button>
