import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { MessageSquare, Compass, User, ShieldCheck, ChevronRight, X } from 'lucide-react';

const steps = [
  {
    icon: <ShieldCheck size={36} className="text-white relative z-10" />,
    title: "Welcome to Cipher",
    desc: "Your decentralized, encrypted messaging & commerce app. Let's take a quick tour of how to navigate."
  },
  {
    icon: <MessageSquare size={36} className="text-blue-400 relative z-10" />,
    title: "The Chats Tab",
    desc: "Tap 'Chats' at the bottom left to access your encrypted DMs. You can also create or join Groups here."
  },
  {
    icon: <Compass size={36} className="text-purple-400 relative z-10" />,
    title: "The Explore Tab",
    desc: "Tap 'Explore' in the middle to browse the Global Marketplace. Buy items using BTC or XMR seamlessly."
  },
  {
    icon: <User size={36} className="text-green-400 relative z-10" />,
    title: "The Profile Tab",
    desc: "Tap 'Profile' on the right. Manage your wallet, open your Vendor Dashboard, or edit your identity."
  }
];

export default function TutorialModal({ isOpen, onClose }) {
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(0);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentStep < steps.length - 1) {
      setCurrentStep(prev => prev + 1);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('cipher_new_account');
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const step = steps[currentStep];

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      <motion.div 
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }} 
        animate={{ opacity: 1 }} 
        exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-6"
      >
        <motion.div 
          key={currentStep}
          initial={{ x: 30, opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ x: -30, opacity: 0 }}
          transition={{ type: "spring", damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }}
          className="relative bg-[#0a0a0a]/90 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/10 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm p-8 overflow-hidden shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)]"
        >
          {/* Skip button */}
          <button 
            onClick={() => { localStorage.removeItem('cipher_new_account'); onClose(); }}
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>

          <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center mt-2">
            
            {/* Glowing Icon Box */}
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#1a1a1a] to-[#0a0a0a] rounded-[1.5rem] flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border border-white/10 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(59,130,246,0.15)] relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/5 blur-xl"></div>
              {step.icon}
            </div>
            
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-3">{step.title}</h2>
            <p className="text-[15px] text-gray-400 leading-relaxed mb-8 px-2">{step.desc}</p>

            {/* Sleek Progress Dots */}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-8">
              {steps.map((_, i) => (
                <div 
                  key={i} 
                  className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                    i === currentStep 
                      ? 'w-6 bg-blue-500 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(59,130,246,0.5)]' 
                      : 'w-1.5 bg-white/20'
                  }`}
                />
              ))}
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={handleNext}
              className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-semibold text-[16px] tracking-wide py-3.5 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-[0_4px_20px_-4px_rgba(59,130,246,0.4)] transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              {currentStep === steps.length - 1 ? "Start Using Cipher" : "Continue"}
              {currentStep !== steps.length - 1 && <ChevronRight size={18} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/db.js ===
// src/db.js
import Dexie from 'dexie';

let _current = null;
let _currentOwner = null;
let _warnedOnce = false;

function makeDB(name) {
  const d = new Dexie(name);
  d.version(8).stores({
    messages: 'id, chatId, pubkey, timestamp, clientId, type',
    profiles: 'pubkey, name, displayName',
    groups: 'id, name',
    chatReads: 'chatId',
    groupKeys: '++id, &[groupId+epoch], groupId, epoch, isCurrent, obtainedAt',
    groupKeyGrants: '++id, &[groupId+pubkey+epoch], groupId, pubkey, epoch, sentAt',
    groupMemberSigs: '&groupId, members, updatedAt',
  });
  return d;
}

export function openDBFor(pubkey) {
  if (!pubkey) return null;
  localStorage.setItem('cipher_db_owner', pubkey);
  if (_currentOwner === pubkey && _current) return _current;
  if (_current) { try { _current.close(); } catch (_) {} }
  const name = 'CipherDB_v7_' + pubkey.slice(0, 16);
  _current = makeDB(name);
  _currentOwner = pubkey;
  return _current;
}

// Restore DB connection if HMR reloads this module
try {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('cipher_db_owner');
  if (saved) {
    const name = 'CipherDB_v7_' + saved.slice(0, 16);
    _current = makeDB(name);
    _currentOwner = saved;
  }
} catch (e) {}

export function closeDB() {
  if (_current) { try { _current.close(); } catch (_) {} }
  _current = null;
  _currentOwner = null;
}

export function currentOwner() { return _currentOwner; }

// Queue for messages that arrive before DB is open
const pendingQueue = [];
export function flushQueue() {
  if (!_current) return;
  const q = pendingQueue.splice(0);
  q.forEach(item => {
    try { _current[item.table][item.method](...item.args); } catch(e) {}
  });
}

// Safe stub returned when accessed before openDBFor().
// Reads return empty, writes no-op. Prevents pre-login crashes.
const stubTable = (tableName) => ({
  toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]),
  get: () => Promise.resolve(undefined),
  put: (...args) => { pendingQueue.push({ table: tableName, method: 'put', args }); return Promise.resolve(); },
  add: (...args) => { pendingQueue.push({ table: tableName, method: 'add', args }); return Promise.resolve(); },
  clear: () => Promise.resolve(),
  delete: () => Promise.resolve(),
  count: () => Promise.resolve(0),
  where: () => ({
    equals: () => ({
      sortBy: () => Promise.resolve([]),
      toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]),
      first: () => Promise.resolve(undefined),
      modify: () => Promise.resolve(0),
    }),
    between: () => ({ toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]) }),
    sortBy: () => Promise.resolve([]),
    toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]),
  }),
  filter: () => ({ toArray: () => Promise.resolve([]) }),
});

export const db = new Proxy({}, {
  get(_t, prop) {
    if (!_current) {
      if (!_warnedOnce) {
        _warnedOnce = true;
        console.warn('[db] accessed before openDBFor — returning empty stubs');
      }
      if (['messages', 'chatReads', 'profiles', 'groups', 'groupKeys', 'groupKeyGrants', 'groupMemberSigs'].includes(prop)) {
        return stubTable(prop);
      }
      return () => Promise.resolve();
    }
    const v = _current[prop];
    return typeof v === 'function' ? v.bind(_current) : v;
  },
});

export const saveMessage = async (chatId, msg) => {
  try { await db.messages.put({ ...msg, chatId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
};

export const getMessages = async (chatId) => {
  return await db.messages.where('chatId').equals(chatId).sortBy('timestamp');
};

export const saveProfile = async (profile) => {
  try { await db.profiles.put(profile); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
};

export const getProfile = async (pubkey) => {
  return await db.profiles.get(pubkey);
};

export const searchProfilesLocal = async (query) => {
  const lowerQuery = query.toLowerCase();
  return await db.profiles.filter(p =>
    (p.name && p.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery)) ||
    (p.displayName && p.displayName.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery))
  ).toArray();
};


=== FILE: src/Discover.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Compass, Users, Megaphone, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from './lib/pool';
import { groupRelays, queryWithTimeout } from './lib/relays';
import { useChatStore } from './store/chatStore';
import { getMyGroups, setMyGroups } from './lib/myGroups';

export default function Discover({ identity, setActiveTab }) {
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const openChat = useChatStore(s => s.openChat);
  const setToast = useChatStore(s => s.setToast);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!identity?.pk) return;
    const fetchGroups = async () => {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [39000, 9002], limit: 100 });
        
        const parsed = events.map(ev => {
          const getTag = (t) => ev.tags.find(x => x[0] === t)?.[1];
          return {
            id: getTag('h') || getTag('d'),
            name: getTag('name') || 'Unnamed Group',
            about: getTag('about') || 'No description',
            isChannel: ev.tags.some(t => t[0] === 'channel' || t[0] === 'broadcast')
          };
        }).filter(g => g.id && g.name);

        const unique = Array.from(new Map(parsed.map(g => [g.id, g])).values());
        setGroups(unique);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch groups:", err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchGroups();
  }, [identity]);

  const handleJoin = (g) => {
    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    if (!localGroups.find(x => x.id === g.id)) {
      setMyGroups(identity.pk, [...localGroups, g]);
    }
    openChat('group', g.id, g);
    if (setActiveTab) setActiveTab('chat');
    setToast({ type: 'success', message: 'Joined ' + g.name });
  };

  const filtered = groups.filter(g => g.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));

  return (
    <div className="h-full p-4 flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a] overflow-hidden pb-24">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 shrink-0">
        <Compass size={24} className="text-white" />
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">Discover</h2>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mb-6 shrink-0">
        <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white/40" />
        <input 
          type="text" 
          placeholder="Search public groups..." 
          value={search}
          onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-[#171717] border border-white/10 rounded-xl py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 space-y-3">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center mt-20">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
          </div>
        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center text-white/40 mt-20">
            <Users size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 opacity-50" />
            <p>No public groups found.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          filtered.map(g => (
            <div key={g.id} className="bg-[#111] border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-4 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">
              <div className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${g.isChannel ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                {g.isChannel ? <Megaphone size={24} /> : <Users size={24} />}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-white font-bold truncate">{g.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/50 line-clamp-2 mt-0.5">{g.about}</p>
              </div>
              <button 
                onClick={() => handleJoin(g)}
                className="bg-white text-black text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors shrink-0"
              >
                Join
              </button>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: src/hooks/useGroupKeyMember.js ===
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays } from '../lib/relays';
import { fromHex } from '../lib/crypto/groupCipher';
import { hasGroupKey, putGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore';
export function useGroupKeyMember({ identity }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!identity?.sk || !identity?.pk) return;
    let sub = null; let mounted = true;
    (async () => {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      sub = pool.subscribe(generalRelays, { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk] }, {
        onevent: async (event) => {
          if (!mounted) return;
          try {
            const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
            const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, event.pubkey);
            const plaintext = nip44.v2.decrypt(event.content, shared);
            const payload = JSON.parse(plaintext);
            if (payload?.t !== 'gkey' || !Array.isArray(payload.keys)) return;
            const { gid } = payload;
            let changed = false;
            for (const item of payload.keys) {
              if (await hasGroupKey(gid, item.ep)) continue;
              await putGroupKey(gid, item.ep, fromHex(item.k), { source: 'dm', from: event.pubkey });
              changed = true;
            }
            if (changed) window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('cipher:group-key-updated', { detail: { groupId: gid } }));
          } catch {}
        },
      });
    })();
    return () => { mounted = false; if (sub) try { sub.close(); } catch {} };
  }, [identity?.pk, identity?.sk]);
}


=== FILE: src/hooks/useGroupKeyReconciler.js ===
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { getCurrentGroupKey, getAllGroupKeys, getGrantedEpochsFor, putGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore';
import { distributeGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyDistribution';
import { generateGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupCipher';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays } from '../lib/relays';
import { db, currentOwner } from '../db';
const lastRunByGroup = new Map();
export function useGroupKeyReconciler({ groupId, members, isOwner, identity }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!groupId || !identity?.sk || !identity?.pk) return;
    if (!Array.isArray(members)) return;
    if (members.length === 0 && !isOwner) return;
    if (!currentOwner()) return;
    const sig = members.slice().sort().join(',') + '|' + (isOwner ? '1' : '0');
    if (lastRunByGroup.get(groupId) === sig) return;
    lastRunByGroup.set(groupId, sig);
    reconcile({ groupId, members, isOwner, identity }).catch(err => { console.warn('[group-key] reconcile deferred', err); lastRunByGroup.delete(groupId); });
  }, [groupId, members, isOwner, identity?.pk, identity?.sk]);
}
async function reconcile({ groupId, members, isOwner, identity }) {
  const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
  const publish = async (ev) => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));
    if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('all relays rejected');
  };
  let current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
  if (!current && isOwner) {
    const key = generateGroupKey();
    await putGroupKey(groupId, 1, key);
    current = { epoch: 1, bytes: key };
    await distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk, groupId, epoch: 1, keyBytes: key, recipients: dedupe([...members, identity.pk]), publish });
    return;
  }
  if (!current) return;
  if (isOwner) {
    const prev = await safeGetSnapshot(groupId);
    const prevSet = new Set(prev || []);
    const removed = [...prevSet].filter(pk => !members.includes(pk));
    if (removed.length > 0) {
      const nextEpoch = current.epoch + 1;
      const newKey = generateGroupKey();
      await putGroupKey(groupId, nextEpoch, newKey);
      const priorEpochs = (await getAllGroupKeys(groupId)).filter(e => e.epoch !== nextEpoch);
      await distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk, groupId, epoch: nextEpoch, keyBytes: newKey, recipients: dedupe([...members, identity.pk]), extraEpochs: priorEpochs, publish });
      await safePutSnapshot(groupId, members);
      return;
    }
    await safePutSnapshot(groupId, members);
  }
  const allEpochs = await getAllGroupKeys(groupId);
  const recipients = [];
  for (const pk of members) { const granted = await getGrantedEpochsFor(groupId, pk); if (!granted.has(current.epoch)) recipients.push(pk); }
  if (!recipients.length) return;
  await distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk, groupId, epoch: current.epoch, keyBytes: current.bytes, recipients: dedupe(recipients), extraEpochs: allEpochs.filter(e => e.epoch !== current.epoch), publish });
}
async function safeGetSnapshot(groupId) { try { const row = await db.groupMemberSigs.get(groupId); return row?.members || null; } catch { return null; } }
async function safePutSnapshot(groupId, members) {
  try { await db.groupMemberSigs.put({ groupId, signature: members.slice().sort().join(','), members: members.slice(), updatedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) }); } catch {}
}
function dedupe(arr) { return Array.from(new Set(arr.filter(Boolean))); }


=== FILE: src/hooks/useNIP29Group.js ===
import { showNotification } from '../lib/notifications';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';

const shouldSuppressGroup = (groupId) => {
  const state = useChatStore.getState();
  const mutedUntil = state.mutedUntil?.[groupId];
  const isMuted = mutedUntil === 'forever' || (mutedUntil && mutedUntil > Date.now());
  const isArchived = state.archivedChats?.includes(groupId);
  return isMuted || isArchived;
};
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { groupRelays, generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';
import { saveMessage } from '../db';
import { getMyGroups, setMyGroups } from '../lib/myGroups';
import { encodeGroupPayload, decodeGroupEvent } from '../lib/crypto/groupMessageCodec';
import { putGroupKey, getAllGroupKeys, getCurrentGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyStore';
import { generateGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupCipher';
import { distributeGroupKey } from '../lib/crypto/groupKeyDistribution';

export function useNIP29Group({ identity }) {
  const setGroupsStore = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroups);
  const setGroups = useCallback((val) => {
    setGroupsStore((prev) => {
      const next = typeof val === 'function' ? val(prev) : val;
      // Strict filter: only allow groups the user has explicitly joined/created.
      // This kills the relay WS listener that injects "lmfao".
      const myGroupIds = new Set(getMyGroups(identity?.pk).map(g => g.id));
      return next.filter(g => myGroupIds.has(g.id));
    });
  }, [identity?.pk, setGroupsStore]);
  const setGroupMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroupMessages);
  const setGroupMembers = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroupMembers);
  const setGroupTypingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.setGroupTypingUsers);
  const setPinnedMessages = useChatStore((s) => s.setPinnedMessages);
  const activeGroup = useChatStore((s) => s.activeGroup);



  const sortMessages = (a, b) => {
    const tA = a.local_timestamp || (a.timestamp * 1000);
    const tB = b.local_timestamp || (b.timestamp * 1000);
    if (tA !== tB) return tA - tB;
    return (a.id || '').localeCompare(b.id || '');
  };

  const appendSystemMessage = useCallback((groupId, text, eventId = null) => {
    const id = eventId ? 'sys-' + eventId : 'sys-' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    setGroupMessages(prev => {
      const msgs = prev[groupId] || [];
      if (msgs.find(m => m.id === id)) return prev;
      return {
        ...prev,
        [groupId]: [
          ...msgs,
          {
            id, isSender: false, isGroup: true, type: 'system',
            timestamp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            rawContent: JSON.stringify({ type: 'system', message: text })
          }
        ].sort(sortMessages)
      };
    });
  }, [setGroupMessages]);

  // CREATE GROUP
  const createGroup = useCallback(async (name, about, isChannel) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const groupId = (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() || Date.now().toString()).replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 16);

    const createEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9007, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['name', name], ['about', about || ''], ['open'], ['p', identity.pk, 'admin']],
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);

    const r9007 = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, createEv));
    const ok = r9007.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled');
    if (!ok) throw new Error('No relay accepted 9007');

    const metaTags = [['h', groupId], ['name', name], ['about', about || '']];
    if (isChannel) {
      metaTags.push(['channel', 'true']);
      metaTags.push(['broadcast', 'true']);
    }
    
    const metaEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9002, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: metaTags,
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, metaEv));

    const joinEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9021, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId]], content: 'Creator'
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, joinEv));

    try {
      const existing = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
      if (!existing) {
        const key = generateGroupKey();
        await putGroupKey(groupId, 1, key);
        await distributeGroupKey({
          senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk,
          groupId, epoch: 1, keyBytes: key, recipients: [identity.pk],
          publish: async (dmEv) => {
            const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, dmEv));
            if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('dm rejected');
          },
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[createGroup] key bootstrap deferred', e); }

    appendSystemMessage(groupId, 'You created the group');
    const newGroup = { id: groupId, name, about: about || '', isChannel, creator: identity.pk, members: [identity.pk] };

    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    if (!localGroups.find(g => g.id === groupId)) {
      localGroups.push(newGroup);
      setMyGroups(identity.pk, localGroups);
    }
    setGroups(prev => [newGroup, ...(prev || []).filter(g => g.id !== groupId)]);

    return newGroup;
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // JOIN GROUP
  const joinGroupById = useCallback(async (groupId, passedName = null) => {
    if (!groupId) return;
    let realName = passedName || 'Unnamed Group';

    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    const localMatch = localGroups.find(g => g.id === groupId);
    if (!passedName && localMatch?.name && localMatch.name !== 'Unnamed Group') {
      realName = localMatch.name;
    } else if (!passedName) {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [9002], limit: 200 }, 3000);
        const match = events.find(ev => ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'h')?.[1] === groupId);
        if (match) {
          const nameTag = match.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'name')?.[1];
          if (nameTag) realName = nameTag;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    if (realName === 'Unnamed Group') {
      const userInput = window.prompt('What is this group called?', '');
      if (userInput && userInput.trim()) realName = userInput.trim();
    }

    const newGroup = { id: groupId, name: realName, isChannel: false, creator: '' };
    const idx = localGroups.findIndex(g => g.id === groupId);
    if (idx === -1) localGroups.push(newGroup);
    else localGroups[idx].name = realName;
    setMyGroups(identity.pk, localGroups);
    setGroups(prev => [newGroup, ...(prev || []).filter(g => g.id !== groupId)]);

    return newGroup;
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // LEAVE GROUP
  const leaveGroup = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const leaveEv = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9022, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId]], content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, leaveEv));
    appendSystemMessage(groupId, 'You left the group', leaveEv.id);

    const localGroups = getMyGroups(identity.pk);
    const updated = localGroups.filter(g => g.id !== groupId);
    setMyGroups(identity.pk, updated);
    setGroups(updated);
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // SEND GROUP MESSAGE
  const sendGroupMessage = useCallback(async (groupId, payload, clientId = null, replyId = null) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const encoded = await encodeGroupPayload({ groupId, payload, senderPubkey: identity.pk, waitForKey: true });
    if (encoded.pending) {
      try {
        const reqEv = finalizeEvent({
          kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
          tags: [['h', groupId]],
          content: JSON.stringify({ type: 'gkey_req' })
        }, identity.sk);
        pool.publish(groupRelays, reqEv);
      } catch (e) {}
      return { success: false, error: 'NO_KEY' };
    }
    const tags = [['h', groupId], ...encoded.tags];
    if (clientId) tags.push(['client', clientId]);
    if (replyId) tags.push(['e', replyId, '', 'reply']);
    const event = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags, content: encoded.content
    }, identity.sk);
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, event));
    return { success: true, event, results };
  }, [identity]);

  // SEND GROUP TYPING INDICATOR
  const requestGroupKey = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['h', groupId]], content: JSON.stringify({ type: 'gkey_req' })
      }, identity.sk);
      pool.publish(groupRelays, ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [identity]);

  const sendGroupTypingIndicator = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const encoded = await encodeGroupPayload({ groupId, payload: { type: 'typing' }, senderPubkey: identity.pk });
      if (encoded.pending) return;
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 9, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['h', groupId], ...encoded.tags], content: encoded.content
      }, identity.sk);
      pool.publish(groupRelays, ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [identity]);

  // ADMIN ACTIONS
  const kickMember = useCallback(async (groupId, memberPubkey) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const allMembers = useChatStore.getState().groupMembers || [];
    const remaining = allMembers.filter(m => m.pubkey !== memberPubkey).map(m => m.pubkey);
    if (!remaining.includes(identity.pk)) remaining.push(identity.pk);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9001, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['p', memberPubkey]], content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setGroupMembers(prev => prev.filter(m => m.pubkey !== memberPubkey));
    appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${memberPubkey.slice(0, 6)}... was kicked from the group`, ev.id);
    // Rotation handled by useGroupKeyReconciler (audit H10)
    // kickMember only publishes the 9001 relay event  }
  }, [identity, setGroupMembers]);

  const promoteMember = useCallback(async (groupId, memberPubkey, isAdmin) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9000, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['p', memberPubkey], ['role', isAdmin ? 'member' : 'admin']],
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setGroupMembers(prev => prev.map(m => m.pubkey === memberPubkey ? { ...m, role: isAdmin ? 'member' : 'admin' } : m));
    appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${memberPubkey.slice(0, 6)}... was ${isAdmin ? 'demoted to member' : 'promoted to admin'}`, ev.id);
  }, [identity, setGroupMembers]);

  // ADD MEMBER
  const addMember = useCallback(async (groupId, memberPubkey) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9000, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['p', memberPubkey]], content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${memberPubkey.slice(0, 6)}... was added to the group`, ev.id);
    setGroupMembers(prev => {
      if (prev.findIndex(x => x.pubkey === memberPubkey) === -1) {
        return [...prev, { pubkey: memberPubkey, role: 'member' }];
      }
      return prev;
    });
  }, [identity, setGroupMembers]);

  // UPDATE GROUP SETTINGS (name, about, avatar)
  const updateGroupSettings = useCallback(async (groupId, updates) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    
    const tags = [['h', groupId]];
    if (updates.name) tags.push(['name', updates.name]);
    if (updates.about !== undefined) tags.push(['about', updates.about]);
    if (updates.avatar) tags.push(['picture', updates.avatar]);
    if (updates.requireApproval !== undefined) {
      tags.push(['join', updates.requireApproval ? 'approval' : 'open']);
    }
    
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9002,
      created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags,
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    
    // Update local group in store
    setGroups(prev => prev.map(g => g.id === groupId ? { ...g, ...updates } : g));
    
    // System message
    if (updates.name) {
      appendSystemMessage(groupId, `Group name changed to "${updates.name}"`, ev.id);
    } else if (updates.avatar) {
      appendSystemMessage(groupId, 'Group photo was updated', ev.id);
    } else if (updates.requireApproval !== undefined) {
      appendSystemMessage(groupId, updates.requireApproval ? 'Joining this group now requires admin approval' : 'This group is now open to join', ev.id);
    } else if (updates.about !== undefined) {
      appendSystemMessage(groupId, 'Group description was updated', ev.id);
    }
    
    return { success: true };
  }, [identity, setGroups]);

  // FETCH MEMBERS
  const fetchGroupMembers = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const evs = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [39002], '#d': [groupId], '#h': [groupId] });
        if (evs.length) {
          const t = evs[0].tags.filter(x => x[0] === 'p');
          const m = t.map(x => ({ pubkey: x[1], role: x[2] || 'member' }));
          if (!m.find(x => x.pubkey === identity.pk)) {
            m.push({ pubkey: identity.pk, role: activeGroup?.creator === identity.pk ? 'admin' : 'member' });
          }
          setGroupMembers(m);
          return m.map(x => x.pubkey);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[useNIP29Group] fetchGroupMembers failed:', err);
      }
      
      // Fallback: If relay returns no 39002, at least show the current user
      const fallback = [{ pubkey: identity.pk, role: activeGroup?.creator === identity.pk ? 'admin' : 'member' }];
      setGroupMembers(fallback);
      return [identity.pk];
    }, [identity, activeGroup, setGroupMembers]);

  // RAW WEBSOCKET SUBSCRIPTION (NIP-42 AUTH + join + subscribe)
  const subscribeToGroups = useCallback((groupIds, since) => {
    if (!groupIds || groupIds.length === 0) return () => {};

    let ws;
    let authSent = false;
    let sentReq = false;
    let isMounted = true;
    let keyHandler = null;

    const connect = () => {
      ws = new WebSocket('wss://groups.aegispayhq.com');

      ws.onopen = () => console.log('✅ Group WS connected');

      ws.onmessage = async (e) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        const msg = JSON.parse(e.data);

        if (msg[0] === 'AUTH') {
          if (authSent) return;
          authSent = true;
          const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
          const challenge = typeof msg[1] === 'string' ? msg[1] : msg[1].challenge;
          const authEv = finalizeEvent({
            kind: 22242, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            tags: [['relay', 'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com'], ['challenge', challenge]],
            content: ''
          }, identity.sk);
          ws.send(JSON.stringify(['AUTH', authEv]));
          console.log('➡️ Group WS sent AUTH');
        }
        else if (msg[0] === 'OK' && msg[2] === true && !sentReq) {
          sentReq = true;
          console.log('✅ Group WS Auth OK');
          const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');          // (auto-admin removed — see audit C3)


          setTimeout(() => {
            if (!isMounted) return;              // (auto-join removed — see audit C3)


            setTimeout(() => {
              if (!isMounted) return;
              for (const gid of groupIds) {
                ws.send(JSON.stringify(['REQ', `group-sub-${gid}`, { kinds: [7, 9, 39002, 9021, 9000], '#h': [gid], since }]));
              }
              console.log('✅ Group WS sent individual REQs');
            }, 1500);
          }, 1000);
        }
        else if (msg[0] === 'EVENT' && msg[1].startsWith('group-sub-')) {
          const event = msg[2];
          const hTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'h');
          if (!hTag) return;
          const groupId = hTag[1];

          if (event.kind === 9021) { // Join
            const pk = event.pubkey;
            setGroupMembers(prev => {
              let updated = Array.isArray(prev) ? [...prev] : [];
              if (updated.findIndex(m => m.pubkey === pk) === -1) updated.push({ pubkey: pk, role: 'member' });
              return updated;
            });
            if (pk !== identity.pk) appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}... joined the group`, event.id);
            return;
          }
          if (event.kind === 9022) { // Leave
            const pk = event.pubkey;
            setGroupMembers(prev => Array.isArray(prev) ? prev.filter(m => m.pubkey !== pk) : []);
            appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}... left the group`, event.id);
            return;
          }
          if (event.kind === 9000) { // Add / Promote
            const pk = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1];
            const role = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'role')?.[1] || 'member';
            setGroupMembers(prev => {
              let updated = Array.isArray(prev) ? [...prev] : [];
              const idx = updated.findIndex(m => m.pubkey === pk);
              if (idx !== -1) updated[idx].role = role;
              else updated.push({ pubkey: pk, role });
              return updated;
            });
            appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}... was ${role === 'admin' ? 'promoted to admin' : 'demoted to member'}`, event.id);
            return;
          }
          if (event.kind === 9001) { // Kick
            const pk = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1];
            setGroupMembers(prev => Array.isArray(prev) ? prev.filter(m => m.pubkey !== pk) : []);
            appendSystemMessage(groupId, `User ${pk.slice(0, 6)}... was kicked from the group`, event.id);
            return;
          }
          if (event.kind === 9002) { // Metadata (Name/About)
            const name = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'name')?.[1];
            const about = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'about')?.[1];
            if (name) appendSystemMessage(groupId, `Group name was changed to "${name}"`, event.id);
            else if (about) appendSystemMessage(groupId, `Group description was updated`, event.id);
            return;
          }

          if (event.kind === 9001) {
            const targetPk = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1];
            if (targetPk) {
              setGroupMembers(prev => Array.isArray(prev) ? prev.filter(m => m.pubkey !== targetPk) : []);
              setGroupMessages(prev => {
                const msgs = prev[groupId] || [];
                const sysMsg = {
                  id: 'sys-' + event.id,
                  isSender: false,
                  isGroup: true,
                  type: 'system',
                  timestamp: event.created_at,
                  rawContent: JSON.stringify({ type: 'system', message: `User ${targetPk.slice(0,6)}... was kicked` })
                };
                if (msgs.find(m => m.id === sysMsg.id)) return prev;
                return { ...prev, [groupId]: [...msgs, sysMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
              });
            }
            return;
          }

          if (event.kind === 39002) {
            const t = event.tags.filter(x => x[0] === 'p');
            const m = t.map(x => ({ pubkey: x[1], role: x[2] || 'member' }));
            if (!m.find(x => x.pubkey === identity.pk)) {
              m.push({ pubkey: identity.pk, role: 'member' });
            }
            setGroupMembers(m);
            return;
          }

          // Handle Reaction Events
          if (event.kind === 7) {
            const targetId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
            if (targetId) {
              useChatStore.getState().setReactions(prev => {
                const existing = prev[targetId] || [];
                if (existing.find(r => r.pubkey === event.pubkey && r.emoji === event.content)) return prev;
                return { ...prev, [targetId]: [...existing, { emoji: event.content, pubkey: event.pubkey }] };
              });
            }
            return;
          }

          // Audit H8: drop messages from blocked senders
          const _blocked = useChatStore.getState().blockedUsers || [];
          if (_blocked.includes(event.pubkey) && event.pubkey !== identity.pk) return;

          let isGkeyReq = false;
          try {
            const parsedQuick = JSON.parse(event.content);
            if (parsedQuick.type === 'gkey_req') {
              isGkeyReq = true;
              if (event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
                const current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
                if (current) {
                  const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
                  await distributeGroupKey({
                    senderPrivkey: identity.sk, senderPubkey: identity.pk,
                    groupId, epoch: current.epoch, keyBytes: current.bytes,
                    recipients: [event.pubkey],
                    publish: async (dmEv) => {
                      const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, dmEv));
                      if (!results.some(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')) throw new Error('dm rejected');
                    },
                  });
                }
              }
            }
          } catch (e) {}
          if (isGkeyReq) return;

          let parsed = {};
          let plaintext = event.content;
          let isPending = false;
          let decEpoch = null;
          const decoded = await decodeGroupEvent({ event, groupId });
          if (decoded.pending) { isPending = true; decEpoch = decoded.epoch; }
          else if (decoded.error) { console.warn('[group-decrypt] failed', decoded.reason); return; }
          else { plaintext = decoded.plaintext; parsed = decoded.parsed || {}; decEpoch = decoded.epoch; }

          // Audit H9: drop already-expired group messages
          if (parsed.expires_at && Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) >= parsed.expires_at) {
            return;
          }

          if (!isPending && parsed.type === 'typing') {
            const age = (Date.now() / 1000) - event.created_at;
            if (age < 10 && event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
              setGroupTypingUsers(prev => ({ ...prev, [event.pubkey]: Date.now() }));
            }
            // Don't save typing events to Dexie (audit H6)
            return;
          }

          // Safety net: If a message comes from someone not in the list, add them
          if (event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
            setGroupMembers(prev => {
              if (prev.findIndex(m => m.pubkey === event.pubkey) === -1) {
                return [...prev, { pubkey: event.pubkey, role: 'member' }];
              }
              return prev;
            });
          }

          const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
          const clientId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];

          setGroupMessages(prev => {
            const groupMsgs = prev[groupId] || [];
            const existingIndex = groupMsgs.findIndex(m => {
              if (clientId && m.clientId === clientId) return true;
              if (m.isSender === (event.pubkey === identity.pk) && !isPending && parsed.message) {
                let oldData = {};
                try { oldData = JSON.parse(m.rawContent || '{}'); } catch(e) {}
                return oldData.message === parsed.message && Math.abs(m.timestamp - event.created_at) < 5;
              }
              return false;
            });

            const baseMsg = {
              id: event.id, clientId, isSender: event.pubkey === identity.pk,
              pubkey: event.pubkey, timestamp: event.created_at,
              local_timestamp: Date.now(),
              rawContent: isPending ? null : plaintext,
              ciphertext: isPending ? event.content : null,
              epoch: decEpoch, pending: isPending,
              replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null, isGroup: true
            };

            if (existingIndex >= 0) {
              const newMsgs = [...groupMsgs];
              newMsgs[existingIndex] = { ...newMsgs[existingIndex], ...baseMsg };
              saveMessage(groupId, newMsgs[existingIndex]);
              return { ...prev, [groupId]: newMsgs };
            } else {
              saveMessage(groupId, baseMsg);
              if (event.pubkey !== identity.pk && !isPending && !shouldSuppressGroup(groupId)) {
                // Check if this is a reply to us or a mention of us
                const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
                let isReplyToMe = false;
                let isMentioned = false;
                
                if (replyTag) {
                  const msgs = useChatStore.getState().groupMessages[groupId] || [];
                  const originalMsg = msgs.find(m => m.id === replyTag[1]);
                  isReplyToMe = originalMsg && originalMsg.isSender;
                }
                
                // Check for @mention of our username
                try {
                  const myProfile = useChatStore.getState().profiles[identity.pk];
                  const myUsername = myProfile?.name || myProfile?.username;
                  if (myUsername && parsed.message && parsed.message.includes('@' + myUsername)) {
                    isMentioned = true;
                  }
                } catch (e) {}
                
                if (isReplyToMe) {
                  showNotification(`💬 Reply in ${groupId.slice(0, 6)}`, parsed.message || 'Replied to you');
                } else if (isMentioned) {
                  showNotification(`📣 You were mentioned`, parsed.message || 'Mentioned you');
                } else {
                  showNotification('New Group Message', parsed.message || 'New attachment');
                }
              }
              return { ...prev, [groupId]: [...groupMsgs, baseMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
            }
          });
        }
        else if (msg[0] === 'CLOSED') {
          console.log('❌ Group WS closed:', msg[2]);
        }
      };

      if (keyHandler) window.removeEventListener('cipher:group-key-updated', keyHandler);
      keyHandler = async (e) => {
        const { groupId: gid } = e.detail;
        if (!groupIds.includes(gid)) return;
        try {
          const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
          const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [9], '#h': [gid], limit: 100 }, 4000);
          for (const event of events) {
            const decoded = await decodeGroupEvent({ event, groupId: gid });
            if (decoded.pending || decoded.error) continue;
            const parsed = decoded.parsed || {};
            if (parsed.type === 'typing') continue;
            const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
            const clientId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];
            setGroupMessages(prev => {
              const groupMsgs = prev[gid] || [];
              const idx = groupMsgs.findIndex(m => m.id === event.id);
              const newMsg = {
                id: event.id, clientId, isSender: event.pubkey === identity.pk,
                pubkey: event.pubkey, timestamp: event.created_at,
                local_timestamp: Date.now(), rawContent: decoded.plaintext,
                epoch: decoded.epoch, pending: false,
                replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null, isGroup: true,
              };
              if (idx >= 0) { const u = [...groupMsgs]; u[idx] = { ...u[idx], ...newMsg }; return { ...prev, [gid]: u }; }
              return { ...prev, [gid]: [...groupMsgs, newMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
            });
          }
        } catch (err) { console.warn('[group-key] refresh failed', err); }
      };
      window.addEventListener('cipher:group-key-updated', keyHandler);

      ws.onclose = (e) => {
        console.log('❌ Group WS closed:', e.reason);
        if (isMounted) setTimeout(connect, 3000);
      };

      ws.onerror = (e) => console.error('Group WS error:', e);
    };

    connect();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      if (keyHandler) window.removeEventListener('cipher:group-key-updated', keyHandler);
      if (ws) ws.close();
    };
  }, [identity, setGroupMessages, setGroupTypingUsers]);

  const pinMessage = useCallback(async (groupId, messageEventId) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9005,
      created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId], ['e', messageEventId]],
      content: ''
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: messageEventId }));
    return { success: true };
  }, [identity, setPinnedMessages]);

  const unpinMessage = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const ev = finalizeEvent({
      kind: 9005,
      created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      tags: [['h', groupId]],
      content: 'unpin'
    }, identity.sk);
    await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(groupRelays, ev));
    setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: null }));
    return { success: true };
  }, [identity, setPinnedMessages]);

  const fetchPinnedMessage = useCallback(async (groupId) => {
    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, groupRelays, { kinds: [9005], '#h': [groupId], limit: 10 }, 3000);
      if (events.length === 0) {
        setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: null }));
        return null;
      }
      events.sort((a, b) => b.created_at - a.created_at);
      const eTag = events[0].tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
      setPinnedMessages(prev => ({ ...prev, [groupId]: eTag || null }));
      return eTag || null;
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }, [identity, setPinnedMessages]);

  return {
    createGroup,
    joinGroupById,
    addMember,
    leaveGroup,
    sendGroupMessage,
    sendGroupTypingIndicator,
    requestGroupKey,
    kickMember,
    promoteMember,
    fetchGroupMembers,
    updateGroupSettings,
    subscribeToGroups,
    pinMessage,
    unpinMessage,
    fetchPinnedMessage,
  };
}


=== FILE: src/hooks/useNIP44DM.js ===
import { showNotification } from '../lib/notifications';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';

const shouldSuppress = (chatId) => {
  const state = useChatStore.getState();
  const mutedUntil = state.mutedUntil?.[chatId];
  const isMuted = mutedUntil === 'forever' || (mutedUntil && mutedUntil > Date.now());
  const isArchived = state.archivedChats?.includes(chatId);
  return isMuted || isArchived;
};
import { useCallback } from 'react';

import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';
import { saveMessage } from '../db';

export function useNIP44DM({ identity }) {
  const setDms = useChatStore((s) => s.setDms);
  const setTypingUsers = useChatStore((s) => s.setTypingUsers);

  const sortMessages = (a, b) => {
    const tA = a.local_timestamp || (a.timestamp * 1000);
    const tB = b.local_timestamp || (b.timestamp * 1000);
    if (tA !== tB) return tA - tB;
    return (a.id || '').localeCompare(b.id || '');
  };

  // SEND A DM
  const sendDM = useCallback(async (recipientPubkey, payload, clientId = null, replyId = null) => {
    if (!recipientPubkey || !identity?.sk) return { success: false, error: 'Missing params' };

    // Defensive: trim and cap length
    let targetDm = recipientPubkey.trim();
    if (targetDm.length > 64) targetDm = targetDm.slice(0, 64);

    try {
      const { finalizeEvent, nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, targetDm);
      const encrypted = nip44.v2.encrypt(JSON.stringify(payload), shared);
      const tags = [['p', targetDm]];
      if (clientId) tags.push(['client', clientId]);
      if (replyId) tags.push(['e', replyId, '', 'reply']);
      const event = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 4,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags,
        content: encrypted
      }, identity.sk);
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, event));
      const okCount = results.filter(r => r.status === 'fulfilled').length;
      if (okCount === 0) {
        console.warn('[DM] All relays rejected:', results);
        return { success: false, error: 'All relays rejected', event, results };
      }
      return { success: true, event, results, relayCount: okCount };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[useNIP44DM] Send failed:', err);
      return { success: false, error: err.message };
    }
  }, [identity]);

  // SEND TYPING INDICATOR
  const sendTypingIndicator = useCallback(async (recipientPubkey) => {
    if (!recipientPubkey || !identity?.sk) return;
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent, nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, recipientPubkey);
      const encrypted = nip44.v2.encrypt(JSON.stringify({ type: 'typing' }), shared);
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 4, // Use kind 4 so strict relays accept it
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['p', recipientPubkey]],
        content: encrypted
      }, identity.sk);
      pool.publish(generalRelays, ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [identity]);

  // SUBSCRIBE TO INCOMING + OUTGOING DMs
  const subscribeToDMs = useCallback((since) => {
    let subs = [];
    const mounted = { current: true };

    const setup = async () => {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const dmFilters = [
        { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk], since },
        { kinds: [4], authors: [identity.pk], since }
      ];
      dmFilters.forEach(filter => {
        const sub = pool.subscribe(generalRelays, filter, {
          onevent: async (event) => {
            if (!mounted.current) return;
            try {
              const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
              const isSender = event.pubkey === identity.pk;
              const counterparty = isSender ? event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1] : event.pubkey;
              if (!counterparty) return;
              // Audit H8: drop messages from blocked users at ingestion
              const blocked = useChatStore.getState().blockedUsers || [];
              if (blocked.includes(counterparty)) return;
              const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, counterparty);
              let text;
              try { text = nip44.v2.decrypt(event.content, shared); }
              catch (e) { return; }
              let data = {};
              try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { data = { type: 'text', message: text }; }

              if (data.type === 'typing') {
                const age = (Date.now() / 1000) - event.created_at;
                if (age < 10 && event.pubkey !== identity.pk) {
                  setTypingUsers(prev => ({ ...prev, [counterparty]: Date.now() }));
                }
                return;
              }
              if (data.type === 'read') {
                setDms(prev => {
                  const msgs = prev[counterparty] || [];
                  return { ...prev, [counterparty]: msgs.map(m => m.isSender ? { ...m, read: true } : m) };
                });
                return;
              }
              if (data.type === 'gkey' || data.type === 'pin') return;
              // Audit H9: drop messages that already expired (delivery lag)
              if (data.expires_at && Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) >= data.expires_at) {
                console.log('[DM] Dropping expired message', { from: counterparty.slice(0, 8), expires_at: data.expires_at });
                return;
              }

              const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
              const clientId = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];
              const newMsg = {
                id: event.id, clientId, isSender,
                timestamp: event.created_at, data,
                pubkey: event.pubkey,
                replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null
              };
              await saveMessage(counterparty, newMsg);
              if (!isSender) {
                const state = useChatStore.getState();
                if (state.activeDm !== counterparty) {
                  state.setUnreadCounts(prev => ({ ...prev, [counterparty]: (prev[counterparty] || 0) + 1 }));
                }
              }
              if (!isSender && data.type !== 'typing' && data.type !== 'read' && !shouldSuppress(counterparty)) {
                // reply notification: check if this is a reply to our message
                const replyTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
                let isReplyToMe = false;
                if (replyTag) {
                  const msgs = useChatStore.getState().dms[counterparty] || [];
                  const originalMsg = msgs.find(m => m.id === replyTag[1]);
                  isReplyToMe = originalMsg && originalMsg.isSender;
                }
                const senderName = data.senderName || 'New Message';
                showNotification(isReplyToMe ? `💬 Reply from ${counterparty.slice(0, 8)}` : `DM from ${counterparty.slice(0, 8)}`, data.message || 'Sent you a file');
              }
              setDms(prev => {
                const chatMsgs = prev[counterparty] || [];
                if (chatMsgs.find(m => m.id === event.id)) return prev;
                const filtered = chatMsgs.filter(m => m.clientId !== clientId);
                return { ...prev, [counterparty]: [...filtered, newMsg].sort(sortMessages) };
              });
            } catch (e) { console.error('Live DM error:', e); }
          }
        });
        subs.push(sub);
      });
    };
    setup();
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
      subs.forEach(s => { try { s.close(); } catch (e) {} });
    };
  }, [identity, setDms, setTypingUsers]);

  // FULL FETCH OF DM HISTORY
  const fetchDMs = useCallback(async () => {
    const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
    const { nip44 } = await import('nostr-tools');
    const [sentDms, recvDms] = await Promise.all([
      queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [4], authors: [identity.pk], limit: 50 }),
      queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [4], '#p': [identity.pk], limit: 50 })
    ]);
    const allDms = Array.from(new Map([...sentDms, ...recvDms].map(ev => [ev.id, ev])).values());
    for (const ev of allDms) {
      try {
        const isSender = ev.pubkey === identity.pk;
        const counterparty = isSender ? ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1] : ev.pubkey;
        if (!counterparty) continue;
        const blocked = useChatStore.getState().blockedUsers || [];
        if (blocked.includes(counterparty)) continue;
        const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(identity.sk, counterparty);
        let text;
        try { text = nip44.v2.decrypt(ev.content, shared); }
        catch (e) { continue; }
        let data = {};
        try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { data = { type: 'text', message: text }; }
        if (data.type === 'gkey' || data.type === 'typing' || data.type === 'read' || data.type === 'pin') continue;
        if (data.expires_at && Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) >= data.expires_at) continue;
        const replyTag = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e' && t[3] === 'reply');
        const clientId = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'client')?.[1];
        await saveMessage(counterparty, {
          id: ev.id, clientId, isSender,
          timestamp: ev.created_at, data,
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          replyTo: replyTag ? replyTag[1] : null,
          isGroup: false
        });
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    const counterparties = new Set();
    for (const ev of allDms) {
      const isSender = ev.pubkey === identity.pk;
      const counterparty = isSender ? ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'p')?.[1] : ev.pubkey;
      if (counterparty) counterparties.add(counterparty);
    }
    return [...counterparties];
  }, [identity]);

  return { sendDM, sendTypingIndicator, subscribeToDMs, fetchDMs };
}


=== FILE: src/hooks/usePanicWipe.js ===
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db';

export function usePanicWipe(identity, enabled) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    let lastX = null, lastY = null, lastZ = null;
    let lastTime = 0;
    let spikeCount = 0;
    let lastSpikeTime = 0;
    // Critical: ignore motion for 3 seconds after toggling, so the tap itself doesn't wipe
    const ARM_DELAY = 3000;
    const armTime = Date.now() + ARM_DELAY;

    // Thresholds (tuned for gravity-included accelerometer, m/s²)
    const SHAKE_THRESHOLD = 35;     // magnitude of delta required for a spike
    const SPIKE_WINDOW = 1000;      // spikes must occur within this window
    const REQUIRED_SPIKES = 3;      // must detect this many spikes in the window
    const MIN_EVENT_INTERVAL = 50;  // throttle to ~20Hz

    const handleMotion = (event) => {
      // Still arming — ignore all motion
      if (Date.now() < armTime) return;

      const a = event.accelerationIncludingGravity || event.acceleration;
      if (!a || a.x === null || a.y === null || a.z === null) return;

      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - lastTime < MIN_EVENT_INTERVAL) return;
      lastTime = now;

      if (lastX !== null) {
        const dX = Math.abs(a.x - lastX);
        const dY = Math.abs(a.y - lastY);
        const dZ = Math.abs(a.z - lastZ);
        const magnitude = dX + dY + dZ;

        if (magnitude > SHAKE_THRESHOLD) {
          // Reset counter if last spike was too long ago
          if (now - lastSpikeTime > SPIKE_WINDOW) spikeCount = 0;
          spikeCount++;
          lastSpikeTime = now;

          if (spikeCount >= REQUIRED_SPIKES) {
            triggerWipe();
          }
        }
      }

      lastX = a.x;
      lastY = a.y;
      lastZ = a.z;
    };

    const triggerWipe = async () => {
      if (window.__PANIC_WIPING) return;
      window.__PANIC_WIPING = true;

      // Vibrate to confirm (three buzzes)
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 50, 100, 50, 300]);

      try {
        sessionStorage.clear();
        localStorage.clear();
        // Attempt DB delete with timeout — don't hang forever
        await Promise.race([
          db.delete().catch(() => {}),
          new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800)),
        ]);
      } catch (e) {
        // ignore
      }

      // Reload to lock screen (all data gone)
      window.location.reload();
    };

    const setup = async () => {
      // iOS 13+ requires explicit permission for motion events
      if (
        typeof DeviceMotionEvent !== 'undefined' &&
        typeof DeviceMotionEvent.requestPermission === 'function'
      ) {
        try {
          const permission = await DeviceMotionEvent.requestPermission();
          if (permission !== 'granted') {
            console.warn('Motion permission denied — panic wipe disabled');
            return;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('Motion permission request failed:', e);
          return;
        }
      }
      window.addEventListener('devicemotion', handleMotion);
    };

    setup();

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('devicemotion', handleMotion);
    };
  }, [enabled, identity]);
}


=== FILE: src/hooks/useReactions.js ===
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useChatStore } from '../store/chatStore';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, groupRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';

export function useReactions({ identity }) {
  const setReactions = useChatStore((s) => s.setReactions);

  const sendReaction = useCallback(async (msgId, emoji, isGroup, groupId, authorPubkey) => {
    console.log("🚨 SEND REACTION HOOK:", { msgId, emoji, isGroup, groupId, authorPubkey });
    if (!msgId || !emoji || !identity?.sk) return { success: false };
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      const tags = [['e', String(msgId)]];
      if (authorPubkey) tags.push(['p', String(authorPubkey)]);
      if (isGroup && groupId) tags.push(['h', String(groupId)]);

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 7,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags,
        content: emoji
      }, identity.sk);

      const relays = isGroup ? groupRelays : generalRelays;
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(relays, ev));


      // Optimistic update
      setReactions((prev) => {
        const existing = prev[msgId] || [];
        const alreadyMine = existing.find((r) => r.pubkey === identity.pk && r.emoji === emoji);
        const filtered = existing.filter((r) => !(r.pubkey === identity.pk && r.emoji === emoji));
        if (alreadyMine) {
          return { ...prev, [msgId]: filtered };
        }
        return { ...prev, [msgId]: [...filtered, { emoji, pubkey: identity.pk }] };
      });

      return { success: true, results };
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[useReactions] send failed:', e);
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [identity, setReactions]);

  const subscribeToReactions = useCallback((msgIds) => {
    if (!msgIds || msgIds.length === 0) return () => {};
    let sub = null;
    let isMounted = true;

    const setup = async () => {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        const allRelays = [...new Set([...generalRelays, ...groupRelays])];

        // 1. Fetch historical reactions
        const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, allRelays, { kinds: [7], '#e': msgIds, limit: 500 }, 4000);
        if (events.length > 0) {
          const reactionMap = {};
          events.forEach((ev) => {
            const targetId = ev.tags.find((t) => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
            if (!targetId) return;
            if (!reactionMap[targetId]) reactionMap[targetId] = [];
            if (!reactionMap[targetId].find((r) => r.pubkey === ev.pubkey && r.emoji === ev.content)) {
              reactionMap[targetId].push({ emoji: ev.content, pubkey: ev.pubkey });
            }
          });
          setReactions((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...reactionMap }));
        }

        // 2. Subscribe to live reactions
        if (isMounted) {
          sub = pool.subscribe(allRelays, { kinds: [7], '#e': msgIds }, {
            onevent: (ev) => {
              const targetId = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'e')?.[1];
              if (!targetId) return;
              setReactions(prev => {
                const existing = prev[targetId] || [];
                if (existing.find(r => r.pubkey === ev.pubkey && r.emoji === ev.content)) return prev;
                return { ...prev, [targetId]: [...existing, { emoji: ev.content, pubkey: ev.pubkey }] };
              });
            }
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[useReactions] subscribe failed:', e);
      }
    };

    setup();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      if (sub) sub.close();
    };
  }, [identity, setReactions]);

  return { sendReaction, subscribeToReactions };
}


=== FILE: src/hooks/useReputation.js ===
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';

// Defense layer cache — profile timestamps keyed by pubkey
// Prevents re-fetching the same profile on every modal open
import { scopedGet, scopedSet } from '../lib/storage';
const PROFILE_CACHE_KEY = 'rep_profile_cache';
const PROFILE_CACHE_TTL = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24h
const MIN_VOUCHER_AGE_DAYS = 7;

function loadProfileCache() {
  try { return scopedGet(PROFILE_CACHE_KEY, {}); } catch { return {}; }
}

function saveProfileCache(cache) {
  try { scopedSet(PROFILE_CACHE_KEY, cache); } catch {}
}

export function useReputation({ identity }) {
  const [reputation, setReputation] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchReputation = useCallback(async (targetPubkey) => {
    if (!targetPubkey) return;
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      // 1. Fetch raw vouches + reviews
      const [rawVouches, reviews] = await Promise.all([
        queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10004], '#p': [targetPubkey] }, 3000),
        queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, { kinds: [10005], '#p': [targetPubkey] }, 3000)
      ]);

      // 2. Defense Layer 1: reject self-vouches + duplicate vouches
      const seenVouchers = new Set();
      const vouchesAfterSelf = rawVouches.filter((ev) => {
        if (ev.pubkey === targetPubkey) return false; // self-vouch
        if (seenVouchers.has(ev.pubkey)) return false; // duplicate
        seenVouchers.add(ev.pubkey);
        return true;
      });

      // 3. Defense Layer 3: check voucher profile age
      const cache = loadProfileCache();
      const now = Date.now();
      const cachedPubkeys = Object.keys(cache);

      // Find which vouchers need profile fetch (not cached, or cache expired)
      const toFetch = vouchesAfterSelf
        .map((v) => v.pubkey)
        .filter((pk) => {
          const c = cache[pk];
          return !c || (now - c.fetchedAt) > PROFILE_CACHE_TTL;
        });

      let freshProfiles = [];
      if (toFetch.length > 0) {
        try {
          freshProfiles = await queryWithTimeout(
            pool,
            generalRelays,
            { kinds: [0], authors: toFetch },
            3000
          );
        } catch {}
      }

      // Merge into cache
      for (const ev of freshProfiles) {
        let meta = {};
        try { meta = JSON.parse(ev.content); } catch {}
        cache[ev.pubkey] = {
          createdAt: ev.created_at,          // profile's last update time
          fetchedAt: now,
          name: meta.display_name || meta.name || ev.pubkey.slice(0, 6),
          picture: meta.picture || null,
        };
      }
      saveProfileCache(cache);

      // 4. Filter vouches by age + build voucher display objects
      const minAgeSec = MIN_VOUCHER_AGE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60;
      const nowSec = Math.floor(now / 1000);
      const validVouchers = [];
      const filteredVouchers = [];

      for (const ev of vouchesAfterSelf) {
        const profile = cache[ev.pubkey];
        const ageSec = profile ? (nowSec - profile.createdAt) : 0;
        const valid = profile && ageSec >= minAgeSec;

        const info = {
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          name: profile?.name || `User ${ev.pubkey.slice(0, 4)}`,
          picture: profile?.picture || null,
          ageDays: Math.floor(ageSec / 86400),
          valid,
          reason: !profile ? 'no-profile' : (ageSec < minAgeSec ? 'too-new' : null),
        };

        if (valid) validVouchers.push(info);
        else filteredVouchers.push(info);
      }

      // 5. Reviews (also self-review protected)
      const validReviews = reviews.filter((ev) => ev.pubkey !== targetPubkey);
      let avgRating = 0;
      if (validReviews.length > 0) {
        const sum = validReviews.reduce(
          (acc, ev) => acc + parseInt(ev.tags.find((t) => t[0] === 'rating')?.[1] || '0'),
          0
        );
        avgRating = (sum / validReviews.length).toFixed(1);
      }

      const hasVouched = vouchesAfterSelf.some((ev) => ev.pubkey === identity.pk);

      setReputation({
        // Trusted count only includes age-validated vouchers
        vouchCount: validVouchers.length,
        rawVouchCount: rawVouches.length,
        filteredCount: filteredVouchers.length,
        vouchList: validVouchers,
        filteredList: filteredVouchers,
        reviewCount: validReviews.length,
        avgRating,
        hasVouched,
        recentReviews: validReviews.slice(0, 3).map((ev) => ({
          pubkey: ev.pubkey,
          rating: parseInt(ev.tags.find((t) => t[0] === 'rating')?.[1] || '0'),
          text: ev.content,
        })),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[useReputation] fetch failed:', e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [identity]);

  const sendVouch = useCallback(async (targetPubkey) => {
    // Defense Layer 1: refuse to vouch yourself
    if (targetPubkey === identity.pk) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Cannot vouch for yourself' };
    }

    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 10004,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['p', targetPubkey], ['vouch', 'true']],
        content: 'Vouched via Cipher',
      }, identity.sk);

      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));
      // Refetch to recalculate trust (invalid vouches still don't count)
      await fetchReputation(targetPubkey);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [identity, fetchReputation]);

  const sendReview = useCallback(async (targetPubkey, rating, text) => {
    if (targetPubkey === identity.pk) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Cannot review yourself' };
    }
    try {
      const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 10005,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [['p', targetPubkey], ['rating', rating.toString()]],
        content: text,
      }, identity.sk);
      await Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(generalRelays, ev));
      await fetchReputation(targetPubkey);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  }, [identity, fetchReputation]);

  return { reputation, loading, fetchReputation, sendVouch, sendReview };
}


=== FILE: src/hooks/useScamReports.js ===
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';

export function useScamReports({ identity, targetPubkey }) {
  const [isScammer, setIsScammer] = useState(false);
  const [reportCount, setReportCount] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!targetPubkey || !identity?.sk) return;
    let mounted = true;

    const fetchReports = async () => {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        // Kind 1984 is the standard NIP-56 report event
        const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, {
          kinds: [1984],
          '#p': [targetPubkey],
          limit: 50
        }, 4000);

        if (!mounted) return;

        // Count unique reporters
        const uniqueReporters = new Set();
        events.forEach(ev => {
          const typeTag = ev.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'report')?.[1];
          // Only count if they tagged it as a scam/spam
          if (typeTag === 'scam' || typeTag === 'spam' || typeTag === 'impersonation' || !typeTag) {
            uniqueReporters.add(ev.pubkey);
          }
        });

        const count = uniqueReporters.size;
        setReportCount(count);
        setIsScammer(count >= 3); // Threshold for the red banner
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to fetch reports:', err);
      }
    };

    fetchReports();
    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, [targetPubkey, identity?.sk]);

  return { isScammer, reportCount };
}

=== FILE: src/hooks/useVendorStats.js ===
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db';

export function useVendorStats({ identity, targetPubkey = null }) {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ sales: 0, revenueFiat: 0, btc: 0, xmr: 0, badges: [] });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchStats = useCallback(async () => {
    const pkToFetch = targetPubkey || identity?.pk;
    if (!pkToFetch) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const allMsgs = await db.messages.toArray();
      
      const completedIds = new Set();
      allMsgs.forEach(m => {
        if (m.data?.type === 'order_update' && m.data?.status === 'completed') {
          completedIds.add(m.data.orderId);
        }
      });

      const orders = allMsgs.filter(m => m.data?.type === 'cipher_order');
      
      let sales = 0;
      let revenueFiat = 0;
      let btc = 0;
      let xmr = 0;

      orders.forEach(o => {
        const isCompleted = completedIds.has(o.id) || o.data.status === 'completed';
        if (isCompleted) {
          sales++;
          revenueFiat += parseFloat(o.data.amountFiat || '0');
          const cryptoAmt = parseFloat(o.data.amountCrypto || '0');
          const currency = (o.data.currency || 'USD').toUpperCase();
          if (currency === 'BTC') btc += cryptoAmt;
          if (currency === 'XMR') xmr += cryptoAmt;
        }
      });

      const badges = [];
      if (sales >= 1) badges.push({ id: 'first_sale', name: 'First Sale', icon: '🏆', color: 'text-yellow-400' });
      if (sales >= 100) badges.push({ id: 'diamond_hands', name: 'Diamond Hands', icon: '💎', color: 'text-blue-400' });
      if (revenueFiat >= 1000) badges.push({ id: 'high_roller', name: '$1K Volume', icon: '💰', color: 'text-green-400' });
      if (revenueFiat >= 10000) badges.push({ id: 'whale', name: '$10K Volume', icon: '🐋', color: 'text-purple-400' });

      setStats({ sales, revenueFiat, btc, xmr, badges });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch vendor stats', e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [identity, targetPubkey]);

  return { stats, loading, fetchStats };
}

=== FILE: src/hooks/useVouchCount.js ===
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { getAuthenticatedPool } from '../lib/pool';
import { generalRelays, queryWithTimeout } from '../lib/relays';

export function useVouchCount({ identity, targetPubkey }) {
  const [vouchCount, setVouchCount] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!targetPubkey || !identity?.sk) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    let mounted = true;

    const fetchVouches = async () => {
      try {
        const pool = await getAuthenticatedPool(identity);
        // Kind 10004 = Cipher Vouch Event
        const events = await queryWithTimeout(pool, generalRelays, {
          kinds: [10004],
          '#p': [targetPubkey],
          limit: 200
        }, 4000);

        if (!mounted) return;

        // Deduplicate by reporter (one vouch per unique pubkey)
        const uniqueVouchers = new Set();
        events.forEach(ev => {
          // Don't count self-vouches
          if (ev.pubkey !== targetPubkey) uniqueVouchers.add(ev.pubkey);
        });

        setVouchCount(uniqueVouchers.size);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to fetch vouches:', err);
      } finally {
        if (mounted) setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchVouches();
    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, [targetPubkey, identity?.sk]);

  const tier = vouchCount >= 10 ? 'elite' : vouchCount >= 3 ? 'trusted' : 'new';
  return { vouchCount, tier, loading };
}
