=== FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupCipher.js ===
const subtle = globalThis.crypto.subtle;
const enc = new TextEncoder();
const dec = new TextDecoder();
export const CIPHER_VERSION = 1;
export function generateGroupKey() { const k = new Uint8Array(32); crypto.getRandomValues(k); return k; }
export function toHex(bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(''); }
export function fromHex(hex) { if (hex.length % 2) throw new Error('invalid hex'); const out = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2); for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i] = parseInt(hex.slice(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16); return out; }
export function b64(bytes) { let s = ''; for (const b of bytes) s += String.fromCharCode(b); return btoa(s); }
export function unb64(s) { const bin = atob(s); const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i); return out; }
function aad({ groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) { return enc.encode(`cipher-group-v${CIPHER_VERSION}|${groupId}|${epoch}|${senderPubkey}`); }
async function importKey(raw) { return subtle.importKey('raw', raw, { name: 'AES-GCM' }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt']); }
export async function encryptGroupMessage({ rawKey, plaintext, groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) {
  const key = await importKey(rawKey);
  const iv = new Uint8Array(12); crypto.getRandomValues(iv);
  const ct = await subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: aad({ groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) }, key, enc.encode(plaintext));
  return { v: CIPHER_VERSION, iv: b64(iv), ct: b64(new Uint8Array(ct)) };
}
export async function decryptGroupMessage({ rawKey, envelope, groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) {
  if (!envelope || envelope.v !== CIPHER_VERSION) throw new Error('unsupported envelope');
  const key = await importKey(rawKey);
  const pt = await subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: unb64(envelope.iv), additionalData: aad({ groupId, epoch, senderPubkey }) }, key, unb64(envelope.ct));
  return dec.decode(pt);
}


=== FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupKeyDistribution.js ===
import { toHex } from './groupCipher';
import { recordGrant, getGrantedEpochsFor } from './groupKeyStore';
export async function distributeGroupKey({ senderPrivkey, senderPubkey, groupId, epoch, keyBytes, recipients, publish, extraEpochs = [] }) {
  const { nip44, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  await Promise.allSettled(recipients.map(async (recipientPubkey) => {
    const granted = await getGrantedEpochsFor(groupId, recipientPubkey);
    const bundles = [];
    if (!granted.has(epoch)) bundles.push({ ep: epoch, k: toHex(keyBytes) });
    for (const e of extraEpochs) { if (!granted.has(e.epoch)) bundles.push({ ep: e.epoch, k: toHex(e.bytes) }); }
    if (!bundles.length) return;
    const payload = { v: 1, t: 'gkey', gid: groupId, by: senderPubkey, at: now, keys: bundles };
    const shared = nip44.v2.utils.getConversationKey(senderPrivkey, recipientPubkey);
    const wrapped = nip44.v2.encrypt(JSON.stringify(payload), shared);
    const event = finalizeEvent({ kind: 4, created_at: now, tags: [['p', recipientPubkey]], content: wrapped }, senderPrivkey);
    try { await publish(event); for (const b of bundles) await recordGrant(groupId, recipientPubkey, b.ep); } catch (e) {}
  }));
}


=== FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupKeyStore.js ===
import { db, currentOwner } from '../../db';
import { fromHex, toHex } from './groupCipher';
const ready = () => !!currentOwner();
export async function getGroupKey(groupId, epoch) {
  if (!ready()) return null;
  const row = await db.groupKeys.where('[groupId+epoch]').equals([groupId, epoch]).first();
  return row ? fromHex(row.keyHex) : null;
}
export async function getCurrentGroupKey(groupId) {
  if (!ready()) return null;
  const rows = await db.groupKeys.where('groupId').equals(groupId).toArray();
  if (!rows.length) return null;
  const latest = rows.reduce((a, b) => (a.epoch > b.epoch ? a : b));
  return { epoch: latest.epoch, bytes: fromHex(latest.keyHex), obtainedAt: latest.obtainedAt };
}
export async function getAllGroupKeys(groupId) {
  if (!ready()) return [];
  const rows = await db.groupKeys.where('groupId').equals(groupId).toArray();
  return rows.map(r => ({ epoch: r.epoch, bytes: fromHex(r.keyHex) }));
}
export async function putGroupKey(groupId, epoch, raw, meta = {}) {
  if (!ready()) return;
  await db.transaction('rw', db.groupKeys, async () => {
    await db.groupKeys.put({ groupId, epoch, keyHex: toHex(raw), isCurrent: true, obtainedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), ...meta });
    await db.groupKeys.where('groupId').equals(groupId).modify(row => { if (row.epoch !== epoch) row.isCurrent = false; });
  });
}
export async function hasGroupKey(groupId, epoch) { return !!(await getGroupKey(groupId, epoch)); }
export async function getGrantedEpochsFor(groupId, pubkey) {
  if (!ready()) return new Set();
  const rows = await db.groupKeyGrants.where('[groupId+pubkey+epoch]').between([groupId, pubkey, -Infinity], [groupId, pubkey, Infinity]).toArray();
  return new Set(rows.map(r => r.epoch));
}
export async function recordGrant(groupId, pubkey, epoch) {
  if (!ready()) return;
  await db.groupKeyGrants.put({ groupId, pubkey, epoch, sentAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) });
}


=== FILE: src/lib/crypto/groupMessageCodec.js ===
import { getCurrentGroupKey, getGroupKey } from './groupKeyStore';
import { encryptGroupMessage, decryptGroupMessage } from './groupCipher';
const KEY_WAIT_MS = 5000; const KEY_POLL_MS = 200;
async function waitForKey(groupId) {
  const start = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - start < KEY_WAIT_MS) { const k = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId); if (k) return k; await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, KEY_POLL_MS)); }
  return null;
}
export async function encodeGroupPayload({ groupId, payload, senderPubkey, waitForKey: wfk = false }) {
  let current = await getCurrentGroupKey(groupId);
  if (!current && wfk) current = await waitForKey(groupId);
  if (!current) return { pending: true };
  const envelope = await encryptGroupMessage({ rawKey: current.bytes, plaintext: JSON.stringify(payload), groupId, epoch: current.epoch, senderPubkey });
  return { content: JSON.stringify(envelope), epoch: current.epoch, tags: [['ep', String(current.epoch)]] };
}
export async function decodeGroupEvent({ event, groupId }) {
  const epTag = event.tags.find(t => t[0] === 'ep');
  if (!epTag) {
    try { return { plaintext: event.content, parsed: JSON.parse(event.content), legacy: true }; }
    catch { return { plaintext: event.content, parsed: { type: 'text', message: event.content }, legacy: true }; }
  }
  const epoch = parseInt(epTag[1], 10);
  const key = await getGroupKey(groupId, epoch);
  if (!key) return { pending: true, epoch };
  try {
    const env = JSON.parse(event.content);
    const plaintext = await decryptGroupMessage({ rawKey: key, envelope: env, groupId, epoch, senderPubkey: event.pubkey });
    return { plaintext, parsed: JSON.parse(plaintext), epoch };
  } catch (e) { return { error: 'decrypt-failed', epoch, reason: e.message }; }
}


=== FILE: src/lib/exportChat.js ===
export const exportChatAsText = (messages, chatName, profiles) => {
  const lines = [];
  lines.push('=== ' + chatName + ' ===');
  lines.push('Exported: ' + new Date().toLocaleString());
  lines.push('');
  
  messages.forEach(msg => {
    const date = new Date(msg.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleString();
    const sender = msg.isSender ? 'Me' : (profiles[msg.pubkey]?.displayName || msg.pubkey.slice(0, 8));
    let text = '';
    if (msg.rawContent) {
      try {
        const p = JSON.parse(msg.rawContent);
        text = p.message || `[${p.type}] ${p.title || p.url || ''}`;
      } catch { text = msg.rawContent; }
    } else if (msg.data) {
      text = msg.data.message || `[${msg.data.type}] ${msg.data.title || msg.data.url || ''}`;
    }
    lines.push(`[${date}] ${sender}: ${text}`);
  });
  
  const blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/plain' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `${chatName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '_')}_${Date.now()}.txt`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};

=== FILE: src/lib/haptics.js ===
export const haptic = (style = 'light') => {
  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.vibrate) {
    switch(style) {
      case 'light': navigator.vibrate(10); break;
      case 'medium': navigator.vibrate(25); break;
      case 'heavy': navigator.vibrate([50, 30, 50]); break; // Success/Payment
      case 'error': navigator.vibrate([50, 50, 50, 50]); break;
    }
  }
};

=== FILE: src/lib/imageCompression.js ===
// Compresses images client-side before upload
// Reduces 5MB photos to ~200-500KB without visible quality loss

export const compressImage = async (file, maxWidth = 1600, quality = 0.82) => {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        // Skip compression if image is already small
        if (file.size < 300 * 1024) {
          resolve(file);
          return;
        }

        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        let { width, height } = img;

        // Resize if too large
        if (width > maxWidth) {
          const ratio = maxWidth / width;
          width = maxWidth;
          height = height * ratio;
        }

        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

        canvas.toBlob(
          (blob) => {
            if (!blob) return reject(new Error('Compression failed'));
            const compressedFile = new File([blob], file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg'), {
              type: 'image/jpeg',
              lastModified: Date.now()
            });
            resolve(compressedFile);
          },
          'image/jpeg',
          quality
        );
      };
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to load image'));
      img.src = e.target.result;
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read file'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
};

export const formatFileSize = (bytes) => {
  if (bytes < 1024) return bytes + ' B';
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB';
  return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + ' MB';
};

=== FILE: src/lib/media.js ===
// Strips EXIF/metadata from images by re-rendering them through a Canvas.
// Audio metadata is harder without external libs, so we focus on images first.

export const stripImageMetadata = async (file) => {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = img.width;
        canvas.height = img.height;
        
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
        
        // Export as a new Blob (this drops all original EXIF data)
        canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
          if (!blob) return reject(new Error('Canvas toBlob failed'));
          const cleanFile = new File([blob], file.name.split('.')[0] + '_clean.jpg', {
            type: 'image/jpeg',
            lastModified: Date.now()
          });
          resolve(cleanFile);
        }, 'image/jpeg', 0.9); // 90% quality
      };
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to load image for stripping'));
      img.src = e.target.result;
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read file'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
};

const PREFIX = 'cipher_my_groups_';

export function getMyGroups(pubkey) {
  if (!pubkey) return [];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PREFIX + pubkey.slice(0, 16));
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}

export function setMyGroups(pubkey, groups) {
  if (!pubkey) return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PREFIX + pubkey.slice(0, 16), JSON.stringify(groups || []));
  } catch {}
}

try { localStorage.removeItem('cipher_my_groups'); } catch {}

import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';

export const requestNotificationPermission = async () => {
  // Web Notification API
  if (!Capacitor.isNativePlatform() && 'Notification' in window) {
    if (Notification.permission !== 'granted') {
      await Notification.requestPermission();
    }
  }
  // Capacitor Local Notifications (Android/iOS)
  if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
    try {
      const { LocalNotifications } = await import(/* @vite-ignore */ '@capacitor/local-notifications');
      await LocalNotifications.requestPermissions();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Capacitor Local Notifications not installed yet.');
    }
  }
};

const _shouldHideContent = () => {
  try {
    const pk = localStorage.getItem('cipher_active_pubkey') || localStorage.getItem('cipher_db_owner') || 'guest';
    const key = 'cipher_' + pk.slice(0, 8) + '_hide_notif_content';
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    return raw === null ? true : JSON.parse(raw); // default: hidden
  } catch { return true; }
};

export const showNotification = async (title, body) => {
  const shouldHide = _shouldHideContent();
  const finalBody = shouldHide ? 'New message' : body;
  // Only show if the user has the tab hidden (don't annoy them if they are staring at the chat)
  if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && !Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) return;

  if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
    try {
      const { LocalNotifications } = await import('@capacitor/local-notifications');
      await LocalNotifications.schedule({
        notifications: [{
          title,
          body: finalBody,
          id: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000),
          schedule: { at: new Date(Date.now() + 1000) },
          sound: null,
          attachments: null,
          actionTypeId: '',
          extra: null
        }]
      });
    } catch (e) {}
  } else if ('Notification' in window && Notification.permission === 'granted') {
    new Notification(title, { body: finalBody, icon: '/icon-192.png' });
  }
};

=== FILE: src/lib/pool.js ===
let pools = {};

export const getAuthenticatedPool = async (identity) => {
  if (pools[identity.pk]) return pools[identity.pk];

  const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
  const pool = new SimplePool({
    enablePing: false,
    enableReconnect: true,
    onauth: async (challenge) => {
      console.log("AUTH challenge:", challenge);
      return finalizeEvent({
        kind: 22242,
        created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tags: [
          ["relay", "wss://groups.aegispayhq.com"],
          ["challenge", challenge]
        ],
        content: ""
      }, identity.sk);
    }
  });

  const originalEnsure = pool.ensureRelay.bind(pool);
  pool.ensureRelay = async (url) => {
    const relay = await originalEnsure(url);
    if (url.includes("aegispayhq.com") && !relay.__patchedAuth) {
      relay.__patchedAuth = true;
      const originalSend = relay.send.bind(relay);
      relay.send = (msg) => {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(msg);
          if (parsed[0] === "AUTH" && typeof parsed[1] === "object") {
            msg = JSON.stringify(["AUTH", JSON.stringify(parsed[1])]);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
        originalSend(msg);
      };
    }
    return relay;
  };

  pools[identity.pk] = pool;
  return pool;
};

export const clearPool = () => {
  pools = {};
};

=== FILE: src/lib/storage.js ===
// Per-account scoped localStorage. Fixes cross-account leakage (audit H3).
// All get/set/remove calls are prefixed with the active pubkey.

export function getActivePubkey() {
  return (
    localStorage.getItem('cipher_active_pubkey') ||
    localStorage.getItem('cipher_db_owner') ||
    'guest'
  );
}

export function scopeKey(key) {
  const pk = getActivePubkey();
  return `cipher_${pk.slice(0, 8)}_${key}`;
}

export function scopedGet(key, defaultValue = null) {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(scopeKey(key));
    return v !== null ? JSON.parse(v) : defaultValue;
  } catch { return defaultValue; }
}

export function scopedSet(key, value) {
  try { localStorage.setItem(scopeKey(key), JSON.stringify(value)); } catch {}
}

export function scopedRemove(key) {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(scopeKey(key)); } catch {}
}

// One-time migration from global keys -> scoped keys.
// Runs after login (pubkey known). Idempotent.
export function migrateGlobalKeys() {
  const pk = getActivePubkey();
  if (!pk || pk === 'guest') return;

  const marker = `cipher_migrated_v1_${pk.slice(0, 8)}`;
  if (localStorage.getItem(marker)) return;

  const keys = [
    'pinned', 'archived', 'muted', 'muted_until',
    'blocked', 'bookmarks', 'msg_edits', 'join_requests',
    'accepted_reqs', 'is_vendor', 'my_products', 'cached_products',
    'btc_address', 'xmr_address', 'my_username', 'rep_profile_cache',
  ];

  for (const k of keys) {
    const g = `cipher_${k}`;
    const val = localStorage.getItem(g);
    if (val !== null) {
      const scoped = `cipher_${pk.slice(0, 8)}_${k}`;
      if (!localStorage.getItem(scoped)) {
        localStorage.setItem(scoped, val);
      }
      localStorage.removeItem(g);
    }
  }

  // Migrate drafts
  const toMove = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
    const k = localStorage.key(i);
    if (k && (k.startsWith('draft-dm-') || k.startsWith('draft-group-'))) {
      toMove.push(k);
    }
  }
  for (const k of toMove) {
    const val = localStorage.getItem(k);
    const scoped = `cipher_${pk.slice(0, 8)}_${k}`;
    if (val !== null && !localStorage.getItem(scoped)) {
      localStorage.setItem(scoped, val);
    }
    localStorage.removeItem(k);
  }

  localStorage.setItem(marker, '1');
  console.log('[storage] migrated global keys for', pk.slice(0, 8));
}


=== FILE: src/LockScreen.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Lock, Fingerprint, ChevronLeft, DownloadCloud, AtSign, Camera, RefreshCw, KeyRound, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { useChatStore } from "./store/chatStore";
import { uploadToBlossom } from '@nostr-wot/blossom';

export default function LockScreen({ identity }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState(() => {
    try {
      return (localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault') || localStorage.getItem('cipher_vaults')) ? 'unlock' : 'create';
    } catch (e) { return 'create'; }
  }); // create, unlock, restore, restore_mnemonic, setup_profile
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [mnemonic, setMnemonic] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [enableBio, setEnableBio] = useState(false);
  const [savedMnemonic, setSavedMnemonic] = useState('');
  const [mnemonicConfirmed, setMnemonicConfirmed] = useState(false);
  const [verifyIndices, setVerifyIndices] = useState([]);
  const [verifyInputs, setVerifyInputs] = useState(['', '', '']);
  const [verifyError, setVerifyError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [tempIdentity, setTempIdentity] = useState(null);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(null);
  const [uploadingAvatar, setUploadingAvatar] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (identity.hasBiometric && mode === 'unlock') handleBiometric();
  }, [identity.hasBiometric, mode]);

  const handleBiometric = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await identity.unlockWithBiometric();
    if (!res.success) setError('Biometrics failed. Use your PIN.');
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const checkUsernameAvailable = async (username) => {
    try {
      const { SimplePool } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const pool = new SimplePool();
      const relays = ['wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://groups.aegispayhq.com', 'wss://nos.lol'];
      const events = await Promise.race([
        pool.querySync(relays, { kinds: [10003], '#t': ['cipher_username_' + username], limit: 5 }),
        new Promise(r => setTimeout(() => r([{ id: 'timeout', tags: [] }]), 3000)),
      ]);
      return events.length === 0;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Precheck failed — failing CLOSED to prevent squatting:', e);
      return false; // audit H14: fail closed, not open
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true); setError('');

    const cleanHandle = handle.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '');
    if (mode === 'create' || mode === 'restore') {
      if (cleanHandle.length < 3) { setError('Handle must be at least 3 letters/numbers'); setLoading(false); return; }
    }
    if (mode !== 'restore_mnemonic' && pin.length < 4) { 
      setError('PIN must be at least 4 digits'); 
      setLoading(false); 
      return; 
    }

    try {
      if (mode === 'create') {
        const available = await checkUsernameAvailable(cleanHandle);
        if (!available) {
          setError('@' + cleanHandle + ' is already taken. Try another.');
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        const res = await identity.createVault(cleanHandle, pin, enableBio);
        setTempIdentity(res);
        setUsername(cleanHandle);
        setDisplayName(cleanHandle);
        const _words = (res.mnemonic || '').split(' ');
        const _idxs = [];
        while (_idxs.length < 3) {
          const r = Math.floor(Math.random() * _words.length);
          if (!_idxs.includes(r)) _idxs.push(r);
        }
        setVerifyIndices(_idxs);
        setVerifyInputs(['', '', '']);
        setVerifyError('');
        setSavedMnemonic(res.mnemonic || '');
        setMnemonicConfirmed(false);
        setMode('save_key');
      } else if (mode === 'unlock') {
        const res = await identity.unlock(pin);
        if (!res.success) setError(res.error || 'Invalid PIN');
      } else if (mode === 'restore') {
        const res = await identity.restoreFromRelays(cleanHandle, pin);
        if (!res.success) setError(res.error);
      } else if (mode === 'restore_mnemonic') {
        if (!mnemonic.trim() || mnemonic.trim().split(/\s+/).length < 12) {
          setError('Please enter your full 12-word phrase');
        } else {
          const res = await identity.restoreFromMnemonic(mnemonic, pin);
          if (!res.success) setError(res.error);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || 'Something went wrong');
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file || !tempIdentity) return;
    setUploadingAvatar(true);
    try {
      const blob = await uploadToBlossom(file, {
        signer: {
          getPublicKey: async () => tempIdentity.pk,
          signEvent: async (event) => {
            const { finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools');
            return finalizeEvent(event, tempIdentity.sk);
          }
        },
      });
      setAvatar(blob.url);
    } catch (err) { 
      useChatStore.getState().setToast({ type: 'info', message: 'Upload failed: ' + err.message }); 
    } finally { 
      setUploadingAvatar(false); 
    }
  };

  const handleSaveKeyContinue = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!mnemonicConfirmed) return;
    setMode('verify_key');
  };

  const handleVerifyKey = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const words = savedMnemonic.split(' ');
    const allCorrect = verifyIndices.every((idx, i) =>
      verifyInputs[i].trim().toLowerCase() === (words[idx] || '').toLowerCase()
    );
    if (!allCorrect) {
      setVerifyError('Some words are incorrect. Go back and check your phrase.');
      return;
    }
    setVerifyError('');
    setMode('setup_profile');
  };

  const handleFinishSetup = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const clean = username.replace('@', '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (clean.length < 3) return setError('Username must be 3+ characters');
    if (!/^[a-z0-9_]+$/.test(clean)) return setError('Only letters, numbers, underscores');
    setLoading(true);
    
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_new_account', 'true'); // Trigger tutorial
    await identity.finalizeLogin(tempIdentity.sk, tempIdentity.pk, tempIdentity.npub, {
      username: clean,
      displayName: displayName.trim() || clean,
      picture: avatar
    });
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const isMnemonicMode = mode === 'restore_mnemonic';

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-[100dvh] overflow-y-auto bg-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 relative">
      
      {/* Subtle top glow */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-full max-w-md h-64 bg-blue-500/10 blur-[100px] pointer-events-none"></div>

      <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-sm flex flex-col items-center">
        
        {/* Logo */}
        <img 
          src="/icon-192.png" 
          alt="Cipher" 
          className="w-28 h-28 object-contain mb-6 drop-shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(59,130,246,0.3)]" 
        />

        {/* Header */}
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2 text-center">
          {mode === 'verify_key' ? 'Verify Your Recovery Key' : mode === 'save_key' ? 'Save Your Recovery Key' : mode === 'setup_profile' ? 'Your Profile' : mode === 'create' ? 'Welcome to Cipher' : mode === 'unlock' ? 'Enter PIN' : mode === 'restore' ? 'Restore Account' : 'Recovery Phrase'}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-gray-400 text-[15px] text-center mb-8 max-w-[280px] leading-relaxed">
          {mode === 'verify_key' ? 'Enter the words below to confirm you saved the phrase.' : mode === 'save_key' ? 'Write down these 12 words. You need them + your PIN to recover this account.' : mode === 'setup_profile' ? 'Let people know who you are.' 
           : mode === 'create' ? 'Pick a handle and a PIN (4+ digits).' 
           : mode === 'unlock' ? 'Enter your PIN to unlock.' 
           : mode === 'restore' ? 'Enter your handle and PIN.'
           : 'Enter your 12-word phrase and a new PIN.'}
        </p>

        {/* Glass Card Form */}
        <form onSubmit={mode === 'verify_key' ? handleVerifyKey : mode === 'save_key' ? handleSaveKeyContinue : mode === 'setup_profile' ? handleFinishSetup : handleSubmit} className="w-full flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0a]/80 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/10 p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl">
          
          {mode === 'save_key' ? (
            <div className="space-y-6 w-full">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 w-full">
                {(savedMnemonic || '').split(' ').map((word, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-center text-[13px] text-white font-mono">
                    {word}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-400 text-sm cursor-pointer hover:text-white transition-colors">
                <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center w-5 h-5">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={mnemonicConfirmed} onChange={(e) => setMnemonicConfirmed(e.target.checked)} className="peer appearance-none w-5 h-5 border border-white/20 rounded-md checked:bg-blue-500 checked:border-blue-500 transition-colors cursor-pointer" />
                  <Check size={14} className="absolute text-white pointer-events-none opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100 transition-opacity" strokeWidth={3} />
                </div>
                I have written down these 12 words.
              </label>
            </div>
          ) : mode === 'setup_profile' ? (
            <>
              <div className="relative mb-6 group cursor-pointer mx-auto">
                <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden transition-all group-hover:border-blue-500/50">
                  {avatar ? (
                    <img src={avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <Camera size={28} className="text-gray-500 group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors" />
                  )}
                </div>
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleAvatarUpload} className="absolute inset-0 opacity-0 cursor-pointer" />
                {uploadingAvatar && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full"><RefreshCw size={20} className="animate-spin text-white" /></div>}
              </div>

              <input 
                type="text" 
                placeholder="Display Name (optional)"
                value={displayName}
                onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 transition-all mb-4"
              />
              
              <div className="w-full relative mb-6">
                <AtSign size={18} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="username"
                  value={username}
                  onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, ''))}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              {mode === 'verify_key' && (
                <div className="space-y-3 mb-6">
                  <div className="text-center mb-4">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Enter words #{verifyIndices.map(i => i + 1).join(', #')} from your recovery phrase</p>
                  </div>
                  {verifyIndices.map((idx, i) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="w-full relative">
                      <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500 font-mono text-sm pointer-events-none">Word #{idx + 1}</span>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="..."
                        value={verifyInputs[i] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          const newInputs = [...verifyInputs];
                          newInputs[i] = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
                          setVerifyInputs(newInputs);
                          setVerifyError('');
                        }}
                        className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl pl-24 pr-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 transition-all font-mono"
                        autoCapitalize="none"
                        autoCorrect="off"
                        spellCheck="false"
                      />
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {verifyError && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs text-center mt-2">{verifyError}</p>}
                </div>
              )}

              {isMnemonicMode && (
                <textarea
                  placeholder="apple banana cherry ..."
                  value={mnemonic}
                  onChange={(e) => { setMnemonic(e.target.value); setError('') }}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-5 text-[15px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 transition-all mb-4 h-28 resize-none font-mono"
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  autoCorrect="off"
                  spellCheck="false"
                />
              )}

              {(mode === 'create' || mode === 'restore') && (
                <div className="w-full relative mb-4">
                  <AtSign size={18} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="yourhandle"
                    value={handle}
                    onChange={(e) => { setHandle(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '')); setError('') }}
                    className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 transition-all"
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {!isMnemonicMode && mode !== 'verify_key' && (
                <input
                  type="password"
                  placeholder="PIN (4+ digits)"
                  value={pin}
                  onChange={(e) => { setPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8)); setError('') }}
                  className="w-full bg-[#0f0f0f] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-5 py-4 text-[16px] text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-0 transition-all tracking-[0.5em] text-center mb-4"
                  maxLength={8}
                />
              )}

              {mode === 'create' && (
                <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2 mb-2 cursor-pointer hover:text-white transition-colors">
                  <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center w-5 h-5">
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={enableBio} onChange={(e) => setEnableBio(e.target.checked)} className="peer appearance-none w-5 h-5 border border-white/20 rounded-md checked:bg-blue-500 checked:border-blue-500 transition-colors cursor-pointer" />
                    <Check size={14} className="absolute text-white pointer-events-none opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100 transition-opacity" strokeWidth={3} />
                  </div>
                  Enable Biometric Unlock
                </label>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          {error && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm text-center mt-2 mb-2 font-medium">{error}</p>}

          <button type="submit" disabled={loading || (mode === 'save_key' && !mnemonicConfirmed) || (mode === 'verify_key' && verifyInputs.some(v => !v.trim()))} className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-semibold text-[16px] tracking-wide py-4 rounded-xl mt-4 shadow-[0_4px_20px_-4px_rgba(59,130,246,0.4)] transition-all active:scale-[0.98] disabled:bg-[#1a1a1a] disabled:text-gray-600 disabled:border disabled:border-white/5 disabled:shadow-none disabled:pointer-events-none flex justify-center items-center">
            {loading ? <RefreshCw size={20} className="animate-spin" /> : mode === 'verify_key' ? 'Verify & Continue' : mode === 'save_key' ? (mnemonicConfirmed ? 'Continue' : 'Confirm you saved it') : mode === 'setup_profile' ? 'Enter Cipher' : mode === 'create' ? 'Create Account' : mode === 'unlock' ? 'Unlock' : mode === 'restore' ? 'Restore Account' : 'Recover Account'}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Footer Links */}
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          {mode === 'unlock' && (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore'); setError(''); setPin('') }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
              Forgot PIN? Restore account
            </button>
          )}

          {mode === 'create' && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 mt-2 w-full items-center">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore'); setError(''); setPin(''); }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                Restore account on this device
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore_mnemonic'); setError(''); setPin(''); setMnemonic('') }} className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                Restore with 12-word recovery phrase
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {mode === 'restore' && (
            <>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore_mnemonic'); setError(''); setPin(''); }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-2">
                <KeyRound size={14} /> Use Recovery Phrase
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('create'); setError(''); setPin('') }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                Create a new account instead
              </button>
            </>
          )}

          {mode === 'restore_mnemonic' && (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode('restore'); setError(''); setPin(''); }} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white text-sm font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-2">
              <ChevronLeft size={14} /> Back to handle restore
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { create } from 'zustand';
import { scopedGet, scopedSet } from '../lib/storage';

export const useChatStore = create((set, get) => ({
  hideNotifContent: scopedGet('hide_notif_content', true),
  setHideNotifContent: (v) => { scopedSet('hide_notif_content', v); set({ hideNotifContent: v }); },

  // NAVIGATION
  view: 'list',
  activeDm: null,
  activeGroup: null,
  setView: (view) => set({ view }),
  setActiveDm: (v) => set({ activeDm: v }),
  setActiveGroup: (v) => set({ activeGroup: v }),
  openChat: (type, id, groupObj = null) => {
    if (type === 'dm') {
      set({ view: 'dm', activeDm: id, activeGroup: null, typingUsers: {}, groupTypingUsers: {} });
    } else {
      set({ view: 'group', activeGroup: groupObj, activeDm: null, typingUsers: {}, groupTypingUsers: {} });
    }
  },
  closeChat: () => set({ view: 'list', activeDm: null, activeGroup: null }),

  // DATA
  dms: {},
  groups: [],
  groupMessages: {},
  profiles: {},
  groupMembers: [],
  pinnedMessages: {},
  disappearingTimers: scopedGet('disappearing_timers', {}),
  unreadCounts: {},
  pinnedChats: scopedGet('pinned', []),
  archivedChats: scopedGet('archived', []),
  toggleArchiveChat: (id) => set(s => {
    const next = s.archivedChats.includes(id) ? s.archivedChats.filter(x => x !== id) : [...s.archivedChats, id];
    scopedSet('archived', next);
    return { archivedChats: next };
  }),
  mutedChats: scopedGet('muted', []),
  togglePinChat: (id) => set(s => {
    const next = s.pinnedChats.includes(id) ? s.pinnedChats.filter(x => x !== id) : [...s.pinnedChats, id];
    scopedSet('pinned', next);
    return { pinnedChats: next };
  }),
  mutedUntil: scopedGet('muted_until', {}),
  setMuteDuration: (id, durationMs) => set(s => {
    const next = { ...s.mutedUntil };
    if (durationMs === 0) {
      delete next[id];
    } else if (durationMs === -1) {
      next[id] = 'forever';
    } else {
      next[id] = Date.now() + durationMs;
    }
    scopedSet('muted_until', next);
    return { mutedUntil: next };
  }),
  toggleMuteChat: (id) => set(s => {
    const next = s.mutedChats.includes(id) ? s.mutedChats.filter(x => x !== id) : [...s.mutedChats, id];
    scopedSet('muted', next);
    return { mutedChats: next };
  }),
  reactions: {},
  deletedMsgs: new Set(),

  setDms: (v) => set((s) => ({ dms: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.dms) : v })),
  setGroups: (v) => set((s) => ({ groups: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.groups) : v })),
  setGroupMessages: (v) => set((s) => ({ groupMessages: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.groupMessages) : v })),
  setProfiles: (v) => set((s) => ({ profiles: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.profiles) : v })),
  setGroupMembers: (v) => set({ groupMembers: v }),
  setPinnedMessages: (v) => set((s) => ({ pinnedMessages: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.pinnedMessages) : v })),
  setDisappearingTimer: (chatId, duration) => set((s) => {
    const next = { ...s.disappearingTimers, [chatId]: duration };
    scopedSet('disappearing_timers', next);
    return { disappearingTimers: next };
  }),
  setUnreadCounts: (v) => set((s) => ({ unreadCounts: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.unreadCounts) : v })),
  setReactions: (v) => set((s) => ({ reactions: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.reactions) : v })),
  setDeletedMsgs: (v) => set({ deletedMsgs: v }),

  // TYPING
  typingUsers: {},
  groupTypingUsers: {},
  setTypingUsers: (v) => set((s) => ({ typingUsers: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.typingUsers) : v })),
  setGroupTypingUsers: (v) => set((s) => ({ groupTypingUsers: typeof v === 'function' ? v(s.groupTypingUsers) : v })),

  // BLOCKED USERS
  blockedUsers: scopedGet('blocked', []),
  toggleBlockUser: (pubkey) => set(s => {
    const next = s.blockedUsers.includes(pubkey) ? s.blockedUsers.filter(x => x !== pubkey) : [...s.blockedUsers, pubkey];
    scopedSet('blocked', next);
    return { blockedUsers: next };
  }),

  // SEARCH
  chatSearch: '',
  setChatSearch: (v) => set({ chatSearch: v }),
  searchQuery: '',
  searchResults: [],
  isSearching: false,
  setSearchQuery: (v) => set({ searchQuery: v }),
  setSearchResults: (v) => set({ searchResults: v }),
  setIsSearching: (v) => set({ isSearching: v }),

  // ROLE
  isVendor: scopedGet('is_vendor', false) === true,
  setIsVendor: (v) => {
    scopedSet('is_vendor', v);
    set({ isVendor: v });
  },

  // MESSAGE EDIT HISTORY
  messageEdits: scopedGet('msg_edits', {}),
  addMessageEdit: (msgId, editData) => set(s => {
    const next = { ...s.messageEdits };
    if (!next[msgId]) next[msgId] = [];
    next[msgId].push({ ...editData, timestamp: Date.now() });
    scopedSet('msg_edits', next);
    return { messageEdits: next };
  }),

  // BOOKMARKS
  bookmarks: scopedGet('bookmarks', {}),
  toggleBookmark: (chatId, msgId) => set(s => {
    const next = { ...s.bookmarks };
    if (!next[chatId]) next[chatId] = [];
    if (next[chatId].includes(msgId)) {
      next[chatId] = next[chatId].filter(x => x !== msgId);
    } else {
      next[chatId] = [...next[chatId], msgId];
    }
    scopedSet('bookmarks', next);
    return { bookmarks: next };
  }),
  isBookmarked: (chatId, msgId) => {
    const s = useChatStore.getState();
    return (s.bookmarks[chatId] || []).includes(msgId);
  },

  // BULK SELECTION MODE
  selectionMode: false,
  selectedMsgs: [],
  toggleSelectionMode: () => set(s => ({ selectionMode: !s.selectionMode, selectedMsgs: [] })),
  toggleSelectedMsg: (id) => set(s => {
    const next = s.selectedMsgs.includes(id) ? s.selectedMsgs.filter(x => x !== id) : [...s.selectedMsgs, id];
    return { selectedMsgs: next };
  }),
  clearSelection: () => set({ selectionMode: false, selectedMsgs: [] }),

  // JOIN REQUESTS
  joinRequests: scopedGet('join_requests', {}),
  addJoinRequest: (groupId, pubkey) => set(s => {
    const next = { ...s.joinRequests };
    if (!next[groupId]) next[groupId] = [];
    if (!next[groupId].includes(pubkey)) next[groupId].push(pubkey);
    scopedSet('join_requests', next);
    return { joinRequests: next };
  }),
  removeJoinRequest: (groupId, pubkey) => set(s => {
    const next = { ...s.joinRequests };
    if (next[groupId]) {
      next[groupId] = next[groupId].filter(p => p !== pubkey);
      if (next[groupId].length === 0) delete next[groupId];
    }
    scopedSet('join_requests', next);
    return { joinRequests: next };
  }),

  // UI MODALS
  postListingGroup: null,
  setPostListingGroup: (v) => set({ postListingGroup: v }),

  showMenu: false,
  showCreateGroup: false,
  showGroupInfo: false,
  showMyProfile: false,
  showAddProduct: false,
  selectedMessage: null,
  profilePubkey: null,
  checkoutProduct: null,
  setShowMenu: (v) => set({ showMenu: v }),
  setShowCreateGroup: (v) => set({ showCreateGroup: v }),
  setShowGroupInfo: (v) => set({ showGroupInfo: v }),
  setShowMyProfile: (v) => set({ showMyProfile: v }),
  setShowAddProduct: (v) => set({ showAddProduct: v }),
  setSelectedMessage: (v) => set({ selectedMessage: v }),
  setProfilePubkey: (v) => set({ profilePubkey: v }),
  setCheckoutProduct: (v) => set({ checkoutProduct: v }),

  // CHAT UI STATE — migrated from Chat.jsx useState (Step 4)
  reviewTarget: null,
  setReviewTarget: (v) => set({ reviewTarget: v }),
  vendorStorefrontPubkey: null,
  setVendorStorefrontPubkey: (v) => set({ vendorStorefrontPubkey: v }),
  editingProduct: null,
  setEditingProduct: (v) => set({ editingProduct: v }),
  broadcastProduct: null,
  setBroadcastProduct: (v) => set({ broadcastProduct: v }),
  showQRScanner: false,
  setShowQRScanner: (v) => set({ showQRScanner: v }),
  showNotifCenter: false,
  setShowNotifCenter: (v) => set({ showNotifCenter: v }),
  showBookmarks: false,
  setShowBookmarks: (v) => set({ showBookmarks: v }),
  showFullEmoji: false,
  setShowFullEmoji: (v) => set({ showFullEmoji: v }),
  replyText: '',
  setReplyText: (v) => set({ replyText: v }),
  forwardingMsg: null,
  setForwardingMsg: (v) => set({ forwardingMsg: v }),
  showForwardModal: false,
  setShowForwardModal: (v) => set({ showForwardModal: v }),
  showEmojiPicker: false,
  setShowEmojiPicker: (v) => set({ showEmojiPicker: v }),

  // INPUT
  newMessage: '',
  newGroupName: '',
  newGroupAbout: '',
  isChannel: false,
  replyingTo: null,
  setNewMessage: (v) => set({ newMessage: v }),
  setNewGroupName: (v) => set({ newGroupName: v }),
  setNewGroupAbout: (v) => set({ newGroupAbout: v }),
  setIsChannel: (v) => set({ isChannel: v }),
  setReplyingTo: (v) => set({ replyingTo: v }),

  // PROFILE EDITOR
  myName: '',
  myUsername: '',
  myAbout: '',
  setMyName: (v) => set({ myName: v }),
  setMyUsername: (v) => set({ myUsername: v }),
  setMyAbout: (v) => set({ myAbout: v }),

  // PRODUCTS
  myProducts: scopedGet('my_products', []),
  newProduct: { name: '', priceUsd: '', image: '' },
  setMyProducts: (v) => {
    const products = typeof v === 'function' ? v(get().myProducts) : v;
    set({ myProducts: products });
    scopedSet('my_products', products);
  },
  setNewProduct: (v) => set({ newProduct: v }),

  // LOADING
  panicWipeEnabled: localStorage.getItem('cipher_panic_wipe') === 'true',
  loading: true,
  uploadingImage: false,
  isRecording: false,
  setLoading: (v) => set({ loading: v }),
  setPanicWipeEnabled: (v) => {
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_panic_wipe', v.toString());
    set({ panicWipeEnabled: v });
  },
  setUploadingImage: (v) => set({ uploadingImage: v }),
  setIsRecording: (v) => set({ isRecording: v }),

  // GLOBAL DIALOG
  dialog: null,
  openDialog: (config) => set({ dialog: config }),
  closeDialog: () => set({ dialog: null }),

  reload: () => set({
    pinnedChats: scopedGet('pinned', []),
    archivedChats: scopedGet('archived', []),
    mutedChats: scopedGet('muted', []),
    mutedUntil: scopedGet('muted_until', {}),
    blockedUsers: scopedGet('blocked', []),
    bookmarks: scopedGet('bookmarks', {}),
    messageEdits: scopedGet('msg_edits', {}),
    disappearingTimers: scopedGet('disappearing_timers', {}),
    joinRequests: scopedGet('join_requests', {}),
    isVendor: scopedGet('is_vendor', false) === true,
    myProducts: scopedGet('my_products', []),
  }),

  // TOAST
  toast: null,
  setToast: (toast) => {
    set({ toast });
    if (toast) setTimeout(() => set({ toast: null }), 3000);
  },
}));


=== FILE: src/useIdentity.js ===
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { registerPasskeyIdentity, unlockPasskeyIdentity, isPRFSupported } from 'nostr-passkey'
import * as bip39 from '@scure/bip39'
import { wordlist } from '@scure/bip39/wordlists/english.js'
import { generateMnemonic, mnemonicToSeedSync } from '@scure/bip39'

// Derive 32-byte secret key from BIP39 mnemonic
export function deriveSkFromMnemonic(mnemonic) {
  const seed = mnemonicToSeedSync(mnemonic);
  return new Uint8Array(seed.slice(0, 32));
}

async function encryptWithSk(sk, plaintext) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', sk, { name: 'AES-GCM' }, false, ['encrypt']);
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  const ct = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv }, key, new TextEncoder().encode(plaintext));
  return { iv: Array.from(iv), ct: Array.from(new Uint8Array(ct)) };
}

async function decryptWithSk(sk, encrypted) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', sk, { name: 'AES-GCM' }, false, ['decrypt']);
  const pt = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: new Uint8Array(encrypted.iv) }, key, new Uint8Array(encrypted.ct)
  );
  return new TextDecoder().decode(pt);
}

async function sha256(message) {
  const msgBuffer = new TextEncoder().encode(message)
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgBuffer)
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
  return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
}

async function getKey(secret) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder()
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw", enc.encode(secret), { name: "PBKDF2" }, false, ["deriveKey"]
  )
  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: "PBKDF2", salt: enc.encode("cipher_secure_salt"), iterations: 100000, hash: "SHA-256" },
    keyMaterial, { name: "AES-GCM", length: 256 }, false, ["encrypt", "decrypt"]
  )
}

export async function encryptKey(skArray, secret) {
  const key = await getKey(secret)
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12))
  const enc = new TextEncoder()
  const encrypted = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: "AES-GCM", iv }, key, enc.encode(JSON.stringify(skArray))
  )
  return { iv: Array.from(iv), data: Array.from(new Uint8Array(encrypted)) }
}

export async function decryptKey(encryptedObj, secret) {
  const key = await getKey(secret)
  const decrypted = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
    { name: "AES-GCM", iv: new Uint8Array(encryptedObj.iv) }, key, new Uint8Array(encryptedObj.data)
  )
  const dec = new TextDecoder()
  return JSON.parse(dec.decode(decrypted))
}

const getVaults = () => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cipher_vaults') || '{}');
  } catch { return {}; }
};

const saveVaults = (vaults) => {
  localStorage.setItem('cipher_vaults', JSON.stringify(vaults));
};

const getActivePubkey = () => localStorage.getItem('cipher_active_pubkey');

const setActivePubkey = (pk) => localStorage.setItem('cipher_active_pubkey', pk);

export function useIdentity() {
  const [keys, setKeys] = useState({ sk: null, pk: null, npub: null })
  const [hasVault, setHasVault] = useState(false)
  const [hasBiometric, setHasBiometric] = useState(false)
  const [loadingSession, setLoadingSession] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const init = async () => {
      const sessionSk = sessionStorage.getItem('cipher_session_sk')
      if (sessionSk) {
        try {
          const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
          const sk = Uint8Array.from(JSON.parse(sessionSk))
          const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
          setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
          setHasVault(true)
          setLoadingSession(false)
          return
        } catch (e) { sessionStorage.removeItem('cipher_session_sk') }
      }

      const vault = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault')
      if (vault) setHasVault(true)

      const bioEnabled = localStorage.getItem('cipher_biometric_enabled')
      if (bioEnabled === 'true') {
        const supported = await isPRFSupported()
        if (supported) setHasBiometric(true)
      }
      
      setLoadingSession(false)
    }
    init()
  }, [])

  const createVault = async (handle, pin) => {
    const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
    const mnemonic = generateMnemonic(wordlist, 128)
    const sk = deriveSkFromMnemonic(mnemonic)
    const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
    
    // Use the handle (lowercased) as the lookup ID for restoring
    const lookupId = await sha256(handle.toLowerCase().trim())

    // Encrypt with PIN locally
    const encryptedLocal = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), pin)
    
    // Multi-account support: store vaults by pubkey
    const vaults = getVaults();
    vaults[pk] = { 
      vault: encryptedLocal, 
      handle: handle.toLowerCase().trim(),
      displayName: handle,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      mnemonicEnc: await encryptWithSk(sk, mnemonic)
    };
    saveVaults(vaults);
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_active_pubkey', pk);
    
    // Legacy fallback (for backwards compat)
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(encryptedLocal))
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_handle', handle.toLowerCase().trim())
    localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')

    // (relay backup removed for security — recovery is phrase-only)


    return { sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk), handle, mnemonic }
  }

  // UPDATED: Accepts a full profile object
  const finalizeLogin = async (sk, pk, npub, profile) => {
    try {
      const { SimplePool, finalizeEvent } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pool = new SimplePool()
      const relays = ['wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net', 'wss://relay.damus.io', 'wss://relay.nostr.band']

      const profileData = {
        name: profile.username, // @handle (e.g. "alice")
        display_name: profile.displayName || profile.username, // "Alice's Shop"
        about: 'New to Cipher.',
        picture: profile.picture || null
      }

      const ev = finalizeEvent({
        kind: 0, created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), tags: [],
        content: JSON.stringify(profileData)
      }, sk)

      // Also publish kind:10003 username registry (critical for search)
      const username = profile?.username;
      let usernameEv = null;
      if (username) {
        usernameEv = finalizeEvent({
          kind: 10003,
          created_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
          tags: [
            ['d', 'username'],
            ['t', 'cipher_username_' + username],
            ['t', 'cipher_username'],
            ['username', username]
          ],
          content: ''
        }, sk)
      }

      const timeout = new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3000))
      const promises = [Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(relays, ev))]
      if (usernameEv) promises.push(Promise.allSettled(pool.publish(relays, usernameEv)))
      await Promise.race([Promise.all(promises), timeout])
    } catch (e) { console.error("Failed to publish initial profile", e) }

    try {
      const { saveProfile } = await import('./db')
      await saveProfile({
        pubkey: pk,
        name: profile?.username || 'user',
        displayName: profile?.displayName || profile?.username || 'User',
        picture: profile?.picture || null,
        about: 'New to Cipher.'
      })
    } catch (e) { console.error('Profile seed failed:', e) }

    sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
    setKeys({ sk, pk, npub })
    setHasVault(true)
  }

  const unlock = async (pin) => {
    try {
      // Multi-account: use the active account's vault
      const activePk = getActivePubkey();
      const vaults = getVaults();
      let vault;
      
      if (activePk && vaults[activePk]) {
        vault = vaults[activePk].vault;
      } else {
        // Fallback to legacy single vault
        const vaultStr = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault');
        if (!vaultStr) throw new Error("No local vault found");
        vault = JSON.parse(vaultStr);
      }
      
      const skArray = await decryptKey(vault, pin)
      
      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(skArray)
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
      
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')
      setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) {
      const fails = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('cipher_fails') || '0') + 1
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', fails.toString())
      if (fails >= 5) {
        // Only wipe THIS account's vault — other accounts stay intact
        const activePk = getActivePubkey();
        if (activePk) {
          try {
            const vaults = getVaults();
            delete vaults[activePk];
            saveVaults(vaults);
            // Also clear the legacy single-vault if it belongs to this account
            const legacyVault = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault');
            if (legacyVault) localStorage.removeItem('cipher_vault');
            // Clear active pointer so next open shows create/restore
            localStorage.removeItem('cipher_active_pubkey');
          } catch (e) {}
        }
        sessionStorage.removeItem('cipher_session_sk');
        window.location.reload();
      }
      return { success: false, remaining: 5 - fails }
    }
  }

  const unlockWithBiometric = async () => {
    try {
      const { secretKey, pubkey } = await unlockPasskeyIdentity()
      const { nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(secretKey)
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
      setKeys({ sk, pk: pubkey, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pubkey) })
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) { return { success: false, error: e.message } }
  }

  const restoreFromRelays = async (handle, pin) => {
    const vaults = getVaults();
    let targetPk = null;
    let targetVault = null;

    // 1. Search in multi-account vaults
    for (const [pk, data] of Object.entries(vaults)) {
      if (data.handle === handle) {
        targetPk = pk;
        targetVault = data.vault;
        break;
      }
    }

    // 2. Fallback to legacy single vault
    if (!targetPk) {
      const legacyVaultStr = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault');
      const legacyHandle = localStorage.getItem('cipher_handle');
      if (legacyVaultStr && legacyHandle === handle) {
        targetVault = JSON.parse(legacyVaultStr);
      }
    }

    if (!targetVault) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Account not found on this device. Use your 12-word recovery phrase if you are on a new device.' };
    }

    try {
      const skArray = await decryptKey(targetVault, pin);
      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(skArray);
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk);

      if (targetPk && pk !== targetPk) throw new Error('Invalid PIN');

      // Update multi-account store just in case it was legacy
      const newVaults = getVaults();
      newVaults[pk] = {
        vault: targetVault,
        handle: handle,
        displayName: newVaults[pk]?.displayName || handle,
        createdAt: newVaults[pk]?.createdAt || Date.now()
      };
      saveVaults(newVaults);

      setActivePubkey(pk);
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)));
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(targetVault));
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_handle', handle);
      
      setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) });
      setHasVault(true);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Invalid PIN' };
    }
  }


  const restoreFromMnemonic = async (mnemonicInput, newPin) => {
    try {
      const normalizedMnemonic = mnemonicInput.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      if (!bip39.validateMnemonic(normalizedMnemonic, wordlist)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid recovery phrase')
      }
      const sk = deriveSkFromMnemonic(normalizedMnemonic)
      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools')
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk)
      const newLocalVault = await encryptKey(Array.from(sk), newPin)
      const vaults = getVaults()
      vaults[pk] = {
        vault: newLocalVault,
        handle: '',
        displayName: 'Restored',
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        mnemonicEnc: await encryptWithSk(sk, normalizedMnemonic)
      }
      saveVaults(vaults)
      setActivePubkey(pk)
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(newLocalVault))
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_fails', '0')
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)))
      setKeys({ sk, pk, npub: nip19.npubEncode(pk) })
      setHasVault(true)
      return { success: true }
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message }
    }
  }

  const getMnemonic = async () => {
    if (!keys.sk || !keys.pk) return null
    const vaults = getVaults()
    const vault = vaults[keys.pk]
    if (!vault?.mnemonicEnc) return null
    try { return await decryptWithSk(keys.sk, vault.mnemonicEnc) } catch (e) { return null }
  };

  const lockApp = async () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem('cipher_session_sk')
    setKeys({ sk: null, pk: null, npub: null })
    
    // 1. Wipe sensitive chat data from memory
    try {
      const { useChatStore } = await import('./store/chatStore');
      useChatStore.setState({ 
        dms: {}, groups: [], groupMessages: {}, profiles: {}, 
        unreadCounts: {}, reactions: {}, activeDm: null, activeGroup: null, view: 'list' 
      });
    } catch(e) {}

    // 2. Close the Dexie DB connection
    try {
      const { closeDB } = await import('./db');
      closeDB();
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  const panicWipe = async () => {
    try {
      // 1. Delete ALL IndexedDB databases (messages, keys, addresses)
      if (typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined' && indexedDB.databases) {
        const dbs = await indexedDB.databases();
        await Promise.allSettled(dbs.map(d => {
          if (d.name) return indexedDB.deleteDatabase(d.name);
        }));
      }
      // 2. Unregister service workers (PWA cache)
      if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        try {
          const regs = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations();
          await Promise.allSettled(regs.map(r => r.unregister()));
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      // 3. Clear caches API
      if ('caches' in window) {
        try {
          const keys = await caches.keys();
          await Promise.allSettled(keys.map(k => caches.delete(k)));
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      // 4. Clear storage
      localStorage.clear();
      sessionStorage.clear();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[panicWipe]', e);
    } finally {
      window.location.reload();
    }
  }

  const getAccounts = () => {
    const vaults = getVaults();
    const activePk = getActivePubkey() || keys.pk;
    const result = Object.entries(vaults).map(([pk, data]) => ({
      pubkey: pk,
      handle: data.handle,
      displayName: data.displayName,
      isActive: pk === activePk,
      createdAt: data.createdAt
    }));

    // Legacy fallback: user created before multi-account support
    if (result.length === 0 && keys.pk) {
      result.push({
        pubkey: keys.pk,
        handle: localStorage.getItem('cipher_handle') || 'account',
        displayName: localStorage.getItem('cipher_handle') || 'Account',
        isActive: true,
        createdAt: Date.now()
      });
    }

    return result.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
  };

  const switchAccount = async (targetPk, pin) => {
    try {
      const vaults = getVaults();
      if (!vaults[targetPk]) throw new Error('Account not found');
      
      const skArray = await decryptKey(vaults[targetPk].vault, pin);
      const { getPublicKey, nip19 } = await import('nostr-tools');
      const sk = Uint8Array.from(skArray);
      const pk = getPublicKey(sk);
      
      if (pk !== targetPk) throw new Error('Invalid PIN for this account');
      
      setActivePubkey(targetPk);
      sessionStorage.setItem('cipher_session_sk', JSON.stringify(Array.from(sk)));
      
      // Update legacy vault too
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_vault', JSON.stringify(vaults[targetPk].vault));
      localStorage.setItem('cipher_handle', vaults[targetPk].handle);
      
      // Reload to reset all state cleanly
      window.location.reload();
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, error: e.message };
    }
  };

  const addNewAccount = () => {
    // Migrate current vault to cipher_vaults before switching, so it's not lost
    const legacyVault = localStorage.getItem('cipher_vault');
    const sessionSk = sessionStorage.getItem('cipher_session_sk');
    if (legacyVault && sessionSk && keys.pk) {
      try {
        const vaults = getVaults();
        if (!vaults[keys.pk]) {
          vaults[keys.pk] = {
            vault: JSON.parse(legacyVault),
            handle: localStorage.getItem('cipher_handle') || 'account',
            displayName: localStorage.getItem('cipher_handle') || 'Account',
            createdAt: Date.now()
          };
          saveVaults(vaults);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    sessionStorage.removeItem('cipher_session_sk');
    localStorage.removeItem('cipher_active_pubkey');
    window.location.reload();
  };

  const removeAccount = (targetPk) => {
    const vaults = getVaults();
    delete vaults[targetPk];
    saveVaults(vaults);
    
    if (getActivePubkey() === targetPk) {
      sessionStorage.clear();
      localStorage.removeItem('cipher_active_pubkey');
      window.location.reload();
    }
  };

  return { 
    ...keys, hasVault, hasBiometric, loadingSession,
    getAccounts, switchAccount, addNewAccount, removeAccount, getMnemonic, 
    unlock, unlockWithBiometric, restoreFromRelays, restoreFromMnemonic, createVault, finalizeLogin, lockApp, panicWipe
  }
}


=== FILE: src/useProfiles.js ===
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { db, saveProfile } from './db'

let listeners = new Set();

export function updateProfileCache(pubkey, data) {
  saveProfile({ pubkey, ...data }).then(() => {
    listeners.forEach(listener => listener());
  });
}

export function useProfiles(pubkeys) {
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState({})

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadLocal = async () => {
      const localData = {};
      for (const pk of pubkeys) {
        if (!pk) continue;
        const p = await db.profiles.get(pk);
        if (p) localData[pk] = p;
      }
      setProfiles(prev => ({ ...prev, ...localData }));
    };
    if (pubkeys.length > 0) loadLocal();
  }, [pubkeys.join(',')])

  useEffect(() => {
    const listener = () => {
      const loadAll = async () => {
        const localData = {};
        for (const pk of pubkeys) {
          const p = await db.profiles.get(pk);
          if (p) localData[pk] = p;
        }
        setProfiles(localData);
      };
      loadAll();
    }
    listeners.add(listener)
    return () => listeners.delete(listener)
  }, [pubkeys.join(',')])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pubkeys || pubkeys.length === 0) return

    const fetchProfiles = async () => {
      const validPubkeys = pubkeys.filter(pk => typeof pk === 'string' && pk.length >= 8)
      const missing = [];
      for (const pk of validPubkeys) {
        const exists = await db.profiles.get(pk);
        if (!exists) missing.push(pk);
      }
      
      if (missing.length === 0) return

      try {
        const { SimplePool } = await import('nostr-tools')
        const pool = new SimplePool({ enablePing: true, enableReconnect: true })
        const relays = ['wss://purplepag.es', 'wss://nos.lol', 'wss://relay.primal.net']

        const events = await Promise.race([
          pool.querySync(relays, { kinds: [0], authors: missing }),
          new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(() => resolve([]), 4000))
        ]);
        
        // If no profiles found, DON'T create fake fallback profiles
        if (events.length === 0) return; 

        for (const ev of events) {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(ev.content)
            const profileData = {
              pubkey: ev.pubkey,
              name: data.display_name || data.name || ev.pubkey.slice(0, 8),
              displayName: data.display_name || data.name || ev.pubkey.slice(0, 8),
              picture: data.picture || null,
              about: data.about || ''
            }
            await saveProfile(profileData);
          } catch (e) {}
        }
        
        listeners.forEach(l => l());
      } catch (err) { console.error("Profile fetch error", err) }
    }

    fetchProfiles()
  }, [pubkeys.join(',')])

  return profiles
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { User, Plus, AlertCircle, RefreshCw, 
  Lock, ChevronRight, ArrowLeft, Copy, CreditCard, Camera, BarChart3, Package, Key, UserPlus } from 'lucide-react'

import { useProfiles, updateProfileCache } from './useProfiles'

import { useChatStore } from './store/chatStore';
import { scopedGet, scopedSet } from './lib/storage';

import VendorAnalytics from './components/chat/VendorAnalytics';
import IdentityBackup from './components/chat/IdentityBackup';
import MessageRequests from './components/chat/MessageRequests';
import VendorOnboardingWizard from './components/chat/VendorOnboardingWizard';
import ProfileEditor from './components/chat/ProfileEditor';
import AccountSwitcher from './components/chat/AccountSwitcher';
import MyOrders from './components/chat/MyOrders';

export default function Wallet({ identity }) {
  const { isVendor, setIsVendor } = useChatStore();
  const [showPanicConfirm, setShowPanicConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [showBackup, setShowBackup] = useState(false);
  const [showVendorWizard, setShowVendorWizard] = useState(false);
